病理图像异常检测Anomaly detection in histopathology

01

AI-Based Anomaly Detection for Clinical-Grade Histopathological Diagnostics

Dippel J, Prenißl N, Hense J, et al. AI-Based Anomaly Detection for Clinical-Grade Histopathological Diagnostics. NEJM AI. 2024;1(11).

doi:10.1056/AIoa2400468 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
arXiv 预印本全文(arXiv:2406.14866v1,pdftotext,含附录)。NEJM AI 正式版付费,数字可能与正式版略有差异。
Full arXiv preprint (arXiv:2406.14866v1, pdftotext, incl. appendix). The NEJM AI version is paywalled; numbers may differ slightly from it.
只用胃、结肠活检中的常见诊断训练异常检测模型(最佳为 Outlier Exposure),无需见过任何罕见病,就能检出长尾中的少见病变:slide-AUROC 胃 95.0%、结肠 91.0%,并可泛化到另一家医院和扫描仪。
An anomaly detector trained only on frequent findings of stomach and colon biopsies (best: Outlier Exposure) flags rare diagnoses it never saw: slide-AUROC 95.0% (stomach), 91.0% (colon), and transfers to another hospital and scanner.

问题Problem

病理 AI 多为有监督分类器,只认识训练集里样本充足的常见诊断。真实胃肠活检中,前 10 种诊断约占 90% 病例,剩下约 10% 分散在 56 种少见实体(包括多种癌)里,有监督模型会漏掉或错分它们。作者把问题改写为异常检测:只学常见诊断的样子,把偏离的切片标出来交给病理医生。

Pathology AI is mostly supervised and only recognises common diagnoses. In GI biopsies the top-10 diagnoses cover ~90% of cases and the remaining ~10% spread over 56 rarer entities, which supervised models miss or misclassify. The authors recast this as anomaly detection: learn frequent findings, flag deviations for the pathologist.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 预处理:H&E 全切片在 20× 下切成 340×340 像素 patch,去掉背景 >80% 的 patch,用 Reinhard 方法做染色归一化(App. B.4)。
  2. 定义正常:胃、结肠分别建模;正常 = 该器官常见诊断的 patch;低级别腺瘤从训练集中去掉,罕见病样本完全不参与训练(§2.2)。
  3. 基线范式:CTransPath(在 32,220 张 TCGA/PAIP 切片上自监督预训练的 Swin Transformer)提特征 + kNN 距离打分;或先用 one-class 损失微调再 kNN(§2.3)。
  4. Outlier Exposure:把 2,901 张其他器官切片(前列腺、肾、肝、肺、小肠、乳腺等)和另一胃肠器官的正常 patch 当作“辅助异常”,用二元交叉熵训练分类器区分正常 vs 辅助异常;每个 batch 两类各半,OE 中近组织 / 远组织各半,去掉与正常 patch 余弦相似度 >0.9 的 OE patch(§2.3, App. B.2)。
  5. 骨干:主结果用在 OE 任务上微调的 CTransPath;从头训练的 ResNet-18 效果相近(App. Table 15)。
  6. 打分:patch 异常分 = 异常类概率,10 个测试时增强视图取平均;切片分 = 最高 10% patch 分数的均值;75 像素重叠切片后平均得到热图(§2.2, App. B.3)。
  7. 评估:5 折交叉验证;slide-AUROC(诊断标签)+ patch-AUROC(病理医生逐区域标注);外部 LMU 队列换扫描仪,不重新训练(§3.3)。
  1. Tile H&E WSIs into 340×340-px patches at 20×, drop background, Reinhard stain normalisation (App. B.4).
  2. Normal = frequent-finding patches of stomach or colon (separate models); low-grade adenomas excluded; rare cases never used in training (§2.2).
  3. Baselines: CTransPath features + kNN distance, or CTransPath fine-tuned with a one-class loss + kNN (§2.3).
  4. Outlier Exposure: binary cross-entropy classifier, normal vs 2,901 other-organ slides plus normal patches of the other GI organ; balanced batches, half near / half far tissue, OE patches with cosine >0.9 to normal removed (§2.3, App. B.2).
  5. Main OE model = CTransPath fine-tuned on the OE task; ResNet-18 from scratch is similar (App. Table 15).
  6. Patch score = anomaly-class probability (10-view TTA); slide score = mean of top-10% patches; overlap-averaged heatmaps (App. B.3).
  7. 5-fold CV; slide- and patch-AUROC; external LMU cohort without retraining (§3.3).

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精-伊红染色,最常规的病理染色,细胞核蓝紫、胞质粉红。
WSI 全切片图像
整张玻片的数字扫描,通常数万×数万像素,需切成小 patch 处理。
活检 biopsy
从病人身上取的小块组织,用于病理诊断。
长尾分布 long tail
少数诊断占绝大多数病例,大量诊断各自只有很少病例。
腺瘤 adenoma
腺上皮的良性肿瘤,高级别腺瘤可进展为癌。
假黑变病 pseudomelanosis coli
结肠黏膜无害的棕色色素沉着,形态改变极细微。
染色归一化 stain normalisation
把不同实验室 / 扫描仪的颜色分布调成一致。
H&E
Haematoxylin and eosin, the routine pathology stain.
Long tail
A few diagnoses make up most cases; many diagnoses are each rare.
Adenoma
Benign glandular tumour; high-grade adenomas can progress to cancer.

数据Data

共 5,423 张切片、约 1,700 万 patch,来自两家医院。Charité 主队列:常见诊断 1,973 张(胃 961、结肠 1,012)+ 异常 200 张,共 65 种诊断,异常切片有逐区域标注;另有 2,901 张其他器官切片作 OE 数据。LMU 外部队列:胃 192 张、结肠 157 张,换用 Leica 扫描仪。数据未公开。

5,423 slides, ~17M patches, two hospitals. Charité: 1,973 frequent-finding slides (stomach 961, colon 1,012) + 200 anomalous slides, 65 diagnoses, region annotations; 2,901 other-organ OE slides. LMU external: 192 gastric + 157 colon slides on a different scanner. Not public.

主要结果Key results

Charité OE slide-AUROC:胃 95.04%、结肠 91.01%;patch-AUROC 91.37% / 90.47%Table 1
恶性肿瘤组 slide-AUROC:胃 97.72%、结肠 96.97%Table 1, §3.1
100% 灵敏度下可判为非异常的病例:胃 36.2%、结肠 4.21%(去掉两种极细微病变后结肠 22.29%)§3.1
外部 LMU(不重训):胃 94.5%、结肠 85.88% slide-AUROC§3.3, App. Table 4
结肠假黑变病 slide-AUROC 20.28%、肠螺旋体病 23.56%(失败案例)App. Table 6
OE 数据用 ImageNet 时结肠 slide-AUROC 仅 52.17%,用同院多器官切片 91.49%App. Table 11
自编码器基线 slide-AUROC 结肠 65.58%、胃 63.73%App. Table 10
约 100 张常见诊断切片后性能趋于饱和App. D.1, Fig. 12
Charité OE slide-AUROC 95.04% (stomach), 91.01% (colon); patch-AUROC 91.37% / 90.47%Table 1
Malignant group slide-AUROC 97.72% / 96.97%Table 1
Cleared at 100% sensitivity: 36.2% stomach, 4.21% colon (22.29% colon without two subtle entities)§3.1
External LMU, no retraining: 94.5% / 85.88% slide-AUROC§3.3, App. Table 4
Failures: pseudomelanosis coli 20.28%, spirochaetosis 23.56% slide-AUROCApp. Table 6
OE with ImageNet: colon slide-AUROC 52.17% vs 91.49% with same-hospital multi-organ slidesApp. Table 11

结论Conclusions

作者认为:只用常见诊断训练的深度异常检测可以可靠地检出胃肠活检长尾中的少见病变,包括罕见原发或转移癌,并能跨医院和扫描仪泛化;按当前性能,最多约三分之一常见诊断的活检可以自动处理而不漏掉少见重病。

Deep anomaly detection trained on frequent findings reliably detects the diagnostic long tail of GI biopsies, including rare primary or metastatic cancers, and generalises across hospitals and scanners; up to a third of frequent-finding biopsies could be handled automatically without missing rare severe disease.

局限Limitations

  • (作者)非常细微的结肠改变检不出:假黑变病、胶原性 / 淋巴细胞性结肠炎、螺旋体病,其中一些病理医生也需要特殊染色。
  • (作者)单个 340 像素 patch 看不到结构性改变,需要多尺度上下文。
  • (作者)低级别腺瘤被人为排除在训练集外,“正常”的边界需人工设定。
  • 我们的理解:结肠外部 AUROC(85.88%)和结肠放行比例(4.21%)明显弱于胃;外部队列还剔除了有特殊撕裂伪影的切片。
  • 我们的理解:只说“是否异常、在哪里”,不给诊断,也没有分子层面的解释;预印本未给出代码或数据链接。
  • (Authors) very subtle colon changes are missed; some need special stains even for pathologists.
  • (Authors) single patches miss architectural context; multi-scale methods needed.
  • Our reading: colon is clearly weaker externally (85.88%) and in auto-clearing (4.21%); no code or data link in the preprint.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

这是 detect 环节在 H&E 上最接近临床级的参考。我们的理解:(1)“正常”在这里是“常见诊断”而非“健康”,肾脏基准也要先写清参考集和边界;(2)OE 很容易迁移:本文 OE 集就包含 459 张肾脏切片,反过来在肾脏 H&E 或组学上可以用其他器官 / 数据集作辅助异常,近 / 远各半采样;(3)slide + patch 两级 AUROC、按诊断分组的 AUROC、“100% 灵敏度下的放行比例”、外部队列不重训,这套评估可以直接用于课题 1。它不涉及 virtual tissue 和 revert,但热图可作为后续建模的 ROI。

The closest clinical-grade reference for the detect stage on H&E. Our reading: define the reference set explicitly (here "frequent", not "healthy"); Outlier Exposure transfers easily (the OE set already contains 459 kidney slides); and the evaluation design — slide + patch AUROC, per-diagnosis AUROC, clearance at 100% sensitivity, external cohort without retraining — carries over to the Topic 1 kidney benchmark.

02

Boehringer-Ingelheim/anomaly-detection-in-histology

Zingman I, Stierstorfer B, Lempp C, Heinemann F. Learning image representations for anomaly detection: application to discovery of histological alterations in drug development. Medical Image Analysis. 2024;92:103067. Code: github.com/Boehringer-Ingelheim/anomaly-detection-in-histology.

doi:10.1016/j.media.2023.103067 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

网页 / READMEweb page / README无本地 PDFno local PDF
GitHub README 及其中两张图(Scheme_extended、tox_pattern);配套论文 Zingman et al., Medical Image Analysis 2024 未在此精读。
GitHub README and its two figures (Scheme_extended, tox_pattern); the companion paper (Zingman et al., Medical Image Analysis 2024) was not read here.
Boehringer Ingelheim 的开源代码:先在多物种、多器官、两种染色的健康组织上训练 CNN(辅助分类 + center-loss),再用目标组织的健康特征训练 one-class SVM,在全切片中找出药物引起的组织病理改变。
Open-source Boehringer Ingelheim code: train a CNN on healthy tissue of several species, organs and two stains (auxiliary classification + center-loss), then fit a one-class SVM on healthy target features to find drug-induced histological change in whole-slide images.

问题Problem

README 的说法:要在染色组织的全切片中做异常检测,用来常规筛查药物开发中的组织病理数据,找出给药后出现的异常改变。我们的理解:这类改变种类多、事先未知,难以为每种收集标注,所以只学“正常”。

Per the README, the goal is anomaly detection in WSIs of stained tissue to routinely screen drug-development histopathology for abnormal alterations. Our reading: such changes are diverse and unknown in advance, so learning only normal tissue is attractive.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 输入:小鼠 7 个器官(脑、心、肾、肝、肺、胰、脾)和大鼠肝的健康组织图块,每种都有 H&E 和 Masson 三色两种染色(README 数据目录)。
  2. 第 1 步:CNN 编码器做辅助分类,类别是“物种 + 器官 + 染色”(如 Mouse, liver, H&E);用 class mix-up 颜色增强;损失 = 交叉熵 + λ·center-loss,让每类特征聚成紧致的簇(README 图 A)。
  3. 骨干:预训练文件名为 EfficientNet_B0_320,即 EfficientNet-B0(README);每种染色单独训练一个模型。
  4. 第 2 步:冻结编码器,只用目标类别(如小鼠肝)健康图块的特征训练 one-class SVM,在特征空间围出“正常”区域(README 图 B)。
  5. 推理:新切片切成图块 → 冻结 CNN 提特征 → one-class SVM 给出异常分 α → α > t 判为异常,得到整张切片的异常图块分布(README 图 C)。
  6. 评估:在正常小鼠肝 vs 非酒精性脂肪肝(NAFLD)小鼠肝测试集上报告平衡准确率、AU-ROC、F1;并展示异常图块比例随毒性化合物剂量升高(README 表与图)。
  1. Healthy tiles: 7 mouse organs + rat liver, each in H&E and Masson's Trichrome (README).
  2. Step 1: CNN classifies species-organ-stain categories with class mix-up colour augmentation; loss = cross-entropy + λ·center-loss (README figure A).
  3. Backbone EfficientNet-B0 (pretrained file names); one model per stain.
  4. Step 2: freeze the encoder; fit a one-class SVM on healthy target-tissue features (README figure B).
  5. Inference: tiles → frozen features → SVM score α → anomaly if α > t, giving an abnormal-tile map (README figure C).
  6. Evaluate on normal vs NAFLD mouse liver (balanced accuracy, AU-ROC, F1) and a dose-response toxicity example.

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精-伊红染色,最常规的病理染色。
Masson 三色染色 Masson's Trichrome
把胶原纤维染成蓝 / 绿色的特殊染色,常用于看纤维化。
NAFLD 非酒精性脂肪肝
与饮酒无关的肝脏脂肪堆积及相关损伤。
毒理学筛查 toxicological screening
在动物上给药后检查组织是否出现药物引起的损伤。
center-loss
把每个样本特征拉向其类别中心的损失,使同类特征更紧致。
one-class SVM
只用正常样本学一个包围正常数据的边界,边界外即异常。
Masson's Trichrome
Special stain colouring collagen blue/green; used to see fibrosis.
NAFLD
Non-alcoholic fatty liver disease.

数据Data

全部在 OSF(osf.io/gqutd)。训练集:16 个文件夹 = 小鼠 7 个器官 × 2 种染色 + 大鼠肝 × 2 种染色,只含正常组织。评测集:正常小鼠肝 vs NAFLD 小鼠肝,H&E 与 Masson 各一套。另提供预训练 CNN(.pt)和 one-class SVM(.pkl)。README 未给出图块数或切片数。

All on OSF (osf.io/gqutd): healthy training tiles in 16 folders (7 mouse organs × 2 stains + rat liver × 2 stains); test set of normal vs NAFLD mouse liver for both stains; pretrained CNNs and one-class SVMs. No tile counts in the README.

主要结果Key results

H&E:平衡准确率 94.20%、AU-ROC 97.33%、F1 94.09%README 表
Masson 三色:平衡准确率 97.51%、AU-ROC 99.03%、F1 97.51%README 表
小鼠肝中异常图块比例随实验化合物剂量(对照 / 低 / 中 / 高)升高,与对照组相比有统计显著差异README 图 tox_pattern A
对照组中血液等非病理结构造成少量假阳性;处理组的检出经病理学家确认为病理改变README 图 tox_pattern B
H&E: balanced accuracy 94.20%, AU-ROC 97.33%, F1 94.09%README table
Masson: balanced accuracy 97.51%, AU-ROC 99.03%, F1 97.51%README table
Abnormal-tile fraction rises with compound dose; significant vs controlREADME figure tox_pattern A
Few false positives in controls (blood etc.); treated-group detections confirmed by a pathologistREADME figure tox_pattern B

结论Conclusions

README 的结论性描述:该方法能检测出小鼠肝中由实验化合物诱发的组织改变,异常图块比例随剂量上升,检出经病理学家确认;预训练模型也可作为 H&E / Masson 图像的通用特征提取器用于其他任务。

Per the README, the detector finds compound-induced alterations in mouse liver, with the abnormal fraction rising with dose and detections confirmed by a pathologist; the pretrained models can also serve as feature extractors for H&E or Masson images.

局限Limitations

  • README 只报告一个评测任务(小鼠肝 NAFLD)和一个毒性示例;其他器官、病变未展示。
  • 只有啮齿类、两种染色,没有人类组织。
  • 我们的理解:输出只是图块级的正常 / 异常,没有类型、严重程度或分子解释。
  • 我们的理解:训练集没覆盖到的正常结构(如血液)会变成假阳性。
  • 本卡片未读配套论文,论文中的基线对比、消融和数据规模未核实。
  • One benchmark (mouse liver NAFLD) and one toxicity example only; rodents and two stains.
  • Our reading: tile-level normal/abnormal output only; normal structures missing from training become false positives.
  • The companion paper was not read; its baselines and ablations are unverified.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

这是 detect 环节在药物扰动场景下的直接例子:只学健康组织,找出药物造成的改变,并看到剂量-反应。我们的理解:(1)训练集含小鼠肾(H&E 与 Masson),公开的 CNN 可直接给肾脏切片提特征,搭“冻结特征 + one-class SVM”的弱基线;(2)“用现成元数据类别做辅助任务”可类比到组学(供体、部位、批次等标签);(3)“异常比例随剂量 / 分期上升”是一种不需要逐区域标注的验证方式,可用于课题 1。

A direct detect-stage example under drug perturbation. Our reading: the public CNN was trained with mouse kidney tiles, so it can give a quick frozen-feature + one-class SVM kidney baseline; metadata-category auxiliary tasks map onto omics labels (donor, site, batch); and dose- or stage-dependent anomaly fractions are a label-free validation for Topic 1.

↑ 回到目录back to index · deep-read.md

03

Pathology-Informed Latent Diffusion Model for Anomaly Detection in Lymph Node Metastasis

Wang J, Byeon K, Song J, et al. Pathology-Informed Latent Diffusion Model for Anomaly Detection in Lymph Node Metastasis. MICCAI 2025.

https://papers.miccai.org/miccai-2025/0675-Paper2270.html · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
MICCAI 2025 论文 PDF 全文(pdftotext)+ 代码仓库 README。
Full MICCAI 2025 paper PDF (pdftotext) plus the code repository README.
AnoPILaD 只用正常淋巴结图块微调文本条件的潜空间扩散模型,推理时用 CONCH 从 74 个“正常组织关键词”中选 5 个作提示,把输入重建成正常的样子,差异即异常分;胃淋巴结 patch-AUC 0.9587,跨器官到乳腺淋巴结仍有 0.8884。
AnoPILaD fine-tunes a text-conditioned latent diffusion model on normal lymph-node patches only; CONCH picks 5 of 74 normal-tissue keywords as the prompt, the input is reconstructed as normal, and the difference is the anomaly score — patch AUC 0.9587 (gastric) and 0.8884 cross-organ (breast).

问题Problem

淋巴结转移是重要预后因素,但有监督检测需要大量专家标注。无监督异常检测只用正常组织训练;已有的扩散重建方法(文中称 AnoDDPM)会部分重建出转移结构,假阳性多。作者想用病理先验(描述正常组织的文字)拉大正常与转移之间的重建差异,并检验跨器官鲁棒性。

Supervised metastasis detection needs extensive annotation. Diffusion reconstruction (AnoDDPM in the paper) partly reconstructs tumour and yields false positives. The paper adds normal-tissue text priors to widen the normal–metastasis gap and tests cross-organ robustness.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 输入:淋巴结 WSI 在 20× 下切成 256×256 像素图块;训练只用正常切片的图块(§3.1)。
  2. 关键词池:从文献收集 74 个描述正常淋巴结细胞和微环境的关键词,由资深病理医生审核(§2.2)。
  3. 加权提示:用 CONCH(在 117 万对病理图文上预训练的视觉-语言模型)算图块与每个关键词的余弦相似度,取前 5 个,相似度除以中位数作权重,经 Compel 库生成加权文本嵌入(§2.2, Fig. 2)。
  4. 训练:Stable Diffusion v1.5 潜空间扩散模型,LoRA(秩 4)微调,Adam,lr 1e-5,batch 64;目标为条件去噪损失 ‖ε − ε_θ(z_t, t, c)‖²;选 FID 最低的 400k 步模型(§2.1, §3.2)。
  5. 重建:把输入加噪到 t = 674,再在文本条件下用 PLMS 采样器 100 步去噪,得到“正常样”重建;输入与重建的差异 = 异常分(§2.1, §3.2)。
  6. 评估:每个测试集内做 z-score;patch 级 AUC / AUPR;WSI 级热图 + 2×2 腐蚀,分类用 Zmax 与 Z99,分割用 DICE / IoU(转移切片)和 TNR(正常切片),阈值 0(§3.2)。
  7. 基线:VAE 似然类(NLL、Regret、LLR、complexity)与重建类(f-AnoGAN、AE、MemAE、AnoDDPM)(§3.2)。
  1. Tile WSIs into 256×256 patches at 20×; train on normal patches only (§3.1).
  2. 74 pathologist-reviewed normal lymph-node keywords; CONCH cosine similarity picks the top 5, median-normalised weights, Compel weighted embedding (§2.2).
  3. Fine-tune Stable Diffusion v1.5 with LoRA (rank 4), conditional denoising loss; 400k-step checkpoint by FID (§3.2).
  4. Noise the input to t = 674, denoise with the prompt (PLMS, 100 steps); input–reconstruction difference = anomaly score (§2.1).
  5. z-score per test set; patch AUC/AUPR; slide Zmax/Z99 classification; DICE/IoU/TNR segmentation (§3.2).

术语Terms

淋巴结转移 lymph node metastasis
肿瘤细胞扩散到淋巴结,是癌症分期和预后的重要依据。
Camelyon16
公开的乳腺癌前哨淋巴结 WSI 数据集,带转移区标注。
宏转移 macrometastasis
直径大于 2 mm 的转移灶,文中 C16 Macro 子集即含此类切片。
多形性细胞核 pleomorphic nuclei
大小形状不一的细胞核,常见于肿瘤细胞。
CONCH
病理视觉-语言基础模型,可把图像和文字映射到同一嵌入空间。
WSI 全切片图像
整张玻片的数字扫描。
Camelyon16
Public breast sentinel lymph-node WSI dataset with metastasis annotations.
CONCH
Pathology vision-language foundation model.

数据Data

LH(两家本地医院的胃淋巴结,不公开):808 张 WSI,751 正常 / 57 转移;正常切片分为训练 643、验证 50、测试 58;训练图块 1,373,475 个。C16(Camelyon16 乳腺淋巴结,公开)只做独立测试:32 正常验证、80 正常测试、49 转移(22 张含 >2 mm 肿瘤簇);OOD 图块 55,659 个。

LH (private gastric lymph nodes, two hospital sites): 808 WSIs, 751 normal / 57 metastatic; 643 training WSIs = 1,373,475 patches. C16 (public Camelyon16): 32 val, 80 normal test, 49 metastatic (22 with >2 mm clusters); test only.

主要结果Key results

Patch 级 AUC / AUPR:LH 0.9587 / 0.9499,C16 0.8884 / 0.6987,均为最佳Table 2
最强基线 MemAE:LH 0.9290,C16 仅 0.6611(跨器官差距约 0.22 AUC)Table 2, §4
VAE 负似然 NLL 在 LH 上 AUC 0.4982(接近随机);f-AnoGAN 0.2289Table 2
WSI 分类 AUC:LH Z99 0.9943,C16 Zmax 0.6745,C16 Macro 0.8062Table 3
分割 DICE:C16 Macro 0.5420(AnoDDPM 0.3131);LH 0.4322(AnoDDPM 0.4319)Table 3
AE / MemAE 分类尚可,但热图几乎对全片打同样的分,定位差Fig. 3, §4
Patch AUC/AUPR: LH 0.9587/0.9499, C16 0.8884/0.6987, best overallTable 2
MemAE: 0.9290 on LH but 0.6611 on C16Table 2
Slide AUC: LH 0.9943 (Z99), C16 0.6745 (Zmax), C16 Macro 0.8062Table 3
Segmentation DICE on C16 Macro 0.5420 vs AnoDDPM 0.3131Table 3
AE/MemAE give near-uniform heatmaps despite fair classificationFig. 3, §4

结论Conclusions

作者认为:通过提示引入组织学上下文,给重建过程加了更强的归纳偏置,提高了对异常特征的敏感度;在两种器官上,AnoPILaD 在 patch 级和切片级都明显优于基于密度和基于重建的方法。后续工作是继续提升跨器官性能。

Prompted histological context adds an inductive bias to reconstruction, improving sensitivity to abnormal features; AnoPILaD outperforms density- and reconstruction-based methods at patch and slide level on two organs. Cross-organ performance is future work.

局限Limitations

  • (作者)跨器官性能仍需提升;C16 上切片级 AUC 只有 0.64–0.67。
  • 我们的理解:扩散时间步 t = 674 是在 LH 测试集上选的,LH 结果偏乐观。
  • 我们的理解:没有“去掉提示 / 随机关键词 / 改变关键词数”的消融,无法判断提升来自文本引导还是 LDM 本身;没有标准差。
  • 我们的理解:关键词池只针对淋巴结;LH 数据不公开;100 步采样推理成本远高于 AE。
  • (Authors) cross-organ performance needs improvement; C16 slide AUC 0.64–0.67.
  • Our reading: t = 674 was chosen on the LH test set; no prompt ablations or standard deviations.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

位于 detect 环节,并带有 revert 的影子。我们的理解:“把异常重建成正常、差异即异常”就是图像域里的反事实正常组织,与第三环“把异常 revert 回正常”形式相似;用先验(这里是文字)给生成式正常模型加条件,可类比为在组学中以细胞类型 / 区域为条件;课题 1 的肾脏基准应同时评估样本级分类和区域级分割(DICE、正常切片 TNR),并做跨队列测试——AE 类基线可能分类好看但定位很差。

Detect stage with a flavour of revert: reconstruction-to-normal is an image-space counterfactual. Our reading: condition generative normal models on priors (cell type, region) in omics, and evaluate the kidney benchmark at both sample and region level, since AE-type baselines can classify well yet localise poorly.

04

Spatial biomarker discovery via interpretable semantic learning in histopathology

Liang J, Jiang X, Reitsam NG, et al. Spatial biomarker discovery via interpretable semantic learning in histopathology. Cancer Cell. 2026;44:1–18.

doi:10.1016/j.ccell.2026.05.014 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
CC BY 4.0 开放 PDF 全文(White Rose eprint 242066,pdftotext);补充图表未读
full CC BY 4.0 open PDF (White Rose eprint 242066) via pdftotext; supplementary figures/tables not read
PathPrism 把结直肠癌 H&E 全切片分割成 8 类组织的语义图,再拆成 628 个有明确定义的空间特征,用线性模型预测预后、突变和化疗获益;VirtualWSI 在语义图上放大或缩小某类组织,看风险和特征怎样变。
PathPrism segments colorectal-cancer H&E slides into 8-class tissue maps, turns them into 628 defined spatial features, and fits linear models for prognosis, mutations and chemotherapy benefit; VirtualWSI grows or shrinks tissue classes on the map to see how risk and features change.

问题Problem

空间生物标志物(组织在肿瘤微环境中的排布模式)对精准肿瘤学很重要,但人工发现慢。深度学习和病理基础模型虽然准,却是黑箱,热图式的事后解释难以变成可定义、可量化、可验证的标志物。作者想要一个可解释、但性能不输基础模型的框架,并能做虚拟扰动。

Spatial biomarkers matter in oncology but are slow to discover by hand; deep-learning and foundation models are accurate but opaque, and heatmap explanations are hard to turn into defined, quantifiable biomarkers. The authors want an interpretable framework that matches foundation-model performance and supports virtual perturbation.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. PrismNet:WSI 在 20× 下切成 224×224 tile,冻结 UNI 提 1024 维特征 → PCA 到 32 维 → 逻辑回归分成 8 类组织(脂肪、碎屑、淋巴细胞、黏液、平滑肌、正常黏膜、间质、肿瘤上皮)+ 背景,拼回成 9 通道概率图。
  2. MacroNet:输入 9 通道概率图,ResNeXt50(输入改为 9 通道)→ 32 维 → 风险分,用负 Cox 部分似然训练,用来检验语义图是否保留预后信息。
  3. 解释 MacroNet:按高/低风险比较组织比例和 saliency 归因;把某组织通道乘以 α 做反事实,观察风险分变化。
  4. 把语义图拆成 628 个空间特征:组织占比、滑窗空间熵、单组织图和两两组织图的拓扑特征(度、聚类系数、中心性、孤立节点比例等)。
  5. 透明模型:elastic-net Cox 用全部特征得到 SPM,取前 10 个特征得到 FSPM;突变用 L1 逻辑回归;化疗获益用 Cox 交互项(化疗 × 标志物)。
  6. LLM:GPT-4o 把选出的标志物整理成机制假设和后续实验建议,另一个 GPT-4o 审阅,5 位专家打分。
  7. VirtualWSI:把某类组织的概率 × α 后重新取 argmax(边界处先变),或在界面中手动改写区域类别,再重算特征和 FSPM 风险,在 UMAP 空间标志物图谱上看轨迹。
  8. 评估:DACHS 5 折交叉验证,MCO / CR07 / TCGA 外部验证;C-index、KM 的 HR、AUC,1,000 次 bootstrap 置信区间。
  1. PrismNet: 224×224 tiles at 20×, frozen UNI features → PCA (32-d) → logistic regression into 8 tissue classes + background, reassembled into a 9-channel probability map.
  2. MacroNet: 9-channel ResNeXt50 trained with a Cox loss to check that maps keep prognostic signal; interpreted with saliency and channel × α counterfactuals.
  3. Decompose maps into 628 spatial features: fractions, sliding-window entropy, single- and pairwise-tissue graph statistics.
  4. Transparent models: elastic-net Cox (SPM; top-10 = FSPM), L1 logistic regression for mutations, Cox interaction models for chemotherapy benefit.
  5. GPT-4o organizes biomarkers into hypotheses, a second GPT-4o reviews, 5 experts rate.
  6. VirtualWSI: scale a tissue channel by α and re-argmax (or edit regions by hand), recompute features and FSPM risk, track samples on a UMAP atlas.
  7. Evaluate with DACHS 5-fold CV plus MCO / CR07 / TCGA; C-index, HR, AUC with bootstrap CIs.

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理切片染色。
WSI 全切片图像
整张玻片扫描成的超大图像,常有数十亿像素。
MSI 微卫星不稳定
DNA 错配修复缺陷导致的高突变状态,影响免疫治疗和预后。
辅助化疗 ACT
手术切除后再做的化疗,用来降低复发风险。
C-index
生存模型的排序一致性指标,0.5 为随机,1 为完美。
HR 风险比
Cox 模型中两组的死亡风险之比,>1 表示风险更高。
II/III 期
TNM 分期;II 期局部侵犯无淋巴结转移,III 期有区域淋巴结转移。
MSI
microsatellite instability, a hypermutated state from DNA mismatch-repair defects
ACT
adjuvant chemotherapy given after surgery
C-index
rank agreement of a survival model; 0.5 random, 1 perfect
HR
hazard ratio between two groups in a Cox model

数据Data

11 个结直肠癌队列、约 7,000 例患者的 H&E 切片。预后:DACHS 2,281 例(主队列)、MCO 1,395、CR07 579、TCGA 232;突变另加 GECCO 联盟 5 个队列;辅助化疗:MCO、IDEA 669、HeCOG 970。分割用公开 patch 数据 NCT-CRC-HE-100K(10 万张)和 CRC-VAL-HE-7K(7,180 张)。另用 TCGA-BRCA(642)和 HeCOG-BRCA(38)验证乳腺癌迁移。

About 7,000 CRC patients in 11 cohorts: DACHS 2,281 (primary), MCO 1,395, CR07 579, TCGA 232 for prognosis; five GECCO cohorts added for mutations; MCO, IDEA 669 and HeCOG 970 for chemotherapy. Segmentation on public NCT-CRC-HE-100K (100k patches) and CRC-VAL-HE-7K (7,180). Breast transfer on TCGA-BRCA (642) and HeCOG-BRCA (38).

主要结果Key results

PrismNet 分割 macro-F1 0.948、MCC 0.958、macro-AUROC 0.988(CRC-VAL-HE-7K)Fig. 2A
MacroNet DACHS 5 折 C-index 0.716 ± 0.020;外部 KM 分层 HR 3.68(MCO)、2.48(CR07)、3.27(TCGA)Fig. 2B, 2D
SPM 与 FSPM(仅 10 个特征)C-index 均 > 0.7,与基础模型相当Fig. 4G
空间特征与基础模型 embedding 合并后,空间特征约占 25% 的系数贡献Fig. 4K
MSI 中位 AUC 0.85(DACHS)/ 0.78(7 个外部队列);TCGA 上 BMPR2 / BRAF / TP53 AUC 0.78 / 0.75 / 0.66Fig. 5B–E
III 期:LYM–MUC–CC > 5 者化疗 HR 9.08,≤ 5 者 HR 0.29,交互 HR 32.45(p = 0.001)Fig. 5J
VirtualWSI 扰动诱导的状态转变,在无 LYM–MUC 结构的患者中分出化疗获益亚组(HR 0.27,p = 0.010)Fig. 7L
PrismNet macro-F1 0.948, MCC 0.958, macro-AUROC 0.988 on CRC-VAL-HE-7KFig. 2A
MacroNet DACHS C-index 0.716 ± 0.020; external HR 3.68 (MCO), 2.48 (CR07), 3.27 (TCGA)Fig. 2B, 2D
SPM and 10-feature FSPM both reach C-index > 0.7, on par with foundation modelsFig. 4G
Spatial features carry ~25% of coefficient weight when fused with foundation-model embeddingsFig. 4K
MSI median AUC 0.85 (DACHS) / 0.78 (7 external cohorts); BMPR2 / BRAF / TP53 AUC 0.78 / 0.75 / 0.66 on TCGAFig. 5B–E
Stage III: LYM–MUC–CC > 5 chemotherapy HR 9.08, ≤ 5 HR 0.29, interaction HR 32.45 (p = 0.001)Fig. 5J
Perturbation-induced transitions identify a chemotherapy-benefit subgroup (HR 0.27, p = 0.010)Fig. 7L

结论Conclusions

作者认为:把 WSI 编码成有病理含义的空间特征谱,可以在不损失性能的前提下实现透明建模,把预测、假设生成、扰动探索和疾病空间图谱连在一起;该框架原则上也可用于空间多组学和 3D 病理。

The authors conclude that encoding WSIs into pathologically meaningful spatial features enables transparent modelling without losing performance, linking prediction, hypothesis generation, perturbation and spatial disease atlases; they see it as compatible with spatial multi-omics and 3D pathology.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:只能捕捉预先定义的组织类别所能表达的模式;换瘤种要重新标注和训练 PrismNet。
  • 作者:VirtualWSI 是模型审讯,不代表生物因果或物理可实现的组织状态。
  • 作者:预后性能处于中等范围,化疗等结论只是假设生成;LLM 不产生经验证的结论。
  • 我们的理解:不少治疗相关结论来自小亚组(HeCOG-BRCA 仅 38 例),HR 9.08、交互 HR 32.45 这样的效应量在小样本里不稳定。
  • Authors: limited to patterns expressible by the predefined tissue classes; new tumour types need new annotation and training.
  • Authors: VirtualWSI is model interrogation, not causality or realizable tissue states.
  • Authors: moderate prognostic performance, so chemotherapy findings are hypothesis-generating; the LLM gives no validated insight.
  • Our reading: several treatment findings come from small subgroups (HeCOG-BRCA n = 38); effect sizes like HR 9.08 are unstable.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是 detect → model → revert 在 H&E 上的可解释原型。628 维空间特征可以直接当作异常检测的特征空间(比黑箱 embedding 更容易让病理医生核对);VirtualWSI 是最简单的'可操纵虚拟组织';但'回复'停在组织层面,没有连到基因。对肾脏 Topic 1,可以借用'冻结基础模型 + PCA + 逻辑回归'的廉价组织分割和单/多组织图特征,但要建立肾脏自己的组织类别,并用配对的 Xenium / snRNA 把组织层面的异常连到基因。

Our reading: an interpretable detect → model → revert prototype on H&E. The 628 spatial features can serve as an anomaly-detection feature space that pathologists can check; VirtualWSI is the simplest manipulable virtual tissue; but 'revert' stays at tissue level, with no gene link. For kidney Topic 1, reuse the cheap frozen-encoder + PCA + LR segmentation and the graph features with a kidney-specific tissue ontology, and use paired Xenium / snRNA to connect tissue-level anomalies to genes.

↑ 回到目录back to index · deep-read.md

05

Counterfactual Diffusion Models Provide Interpretable Explanations of Artificial Intelligence Models in Pathology

Žigutytė L, Lenz T, Han T, et al. Counterfactual Diffusion Models Provide Interpretable Explanations of Artificial Intelligence Models in Pathology. Cancer Res. 2026;86(17):4414–4433.

doi:10.1158/0008-5472.CAN-25-5645 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

全文已读full text read无本地 PDFno local PDF
PMC 全文(PMC13535314,CC BY 4.0);补充图表未读
PMC full text (PMC13535314, CC BY 4.0); supplementary figures/tables not read
MoPaDi 用 diffusion autoencoder 把病理 tile 编码成 512 维向量,沿分类器的决策方向移动这个向量再解码,生成"如果它属于另一类会长什么样"的反事实 H&E 图像,让病理医生看到分类器依赖的形态线索。
MoPaDi encodes a pathology tile into a 512-d vector with a diffusion autoencoder, moves it along a classifier's decision direction and decodes it, producing counterfactual H&E images that show which morphology the classifier relies on.

问题Problem

病理 AI 常用的解释方法(attention 热图、CAM)只告诉你模型看了哪里,不告诉你看到了什么特征,还容易带来确认偏差。反事实解释(要怎么改图才会被分到另一类)在病理里多基于 GAN;而病理标签大多只有 slide 级,需要与多示例学习(MIL)结合。

Attention heatmaps and CAMs show where a model looks, not which features it uses, and invite confirmation bias. Pathology counterfactuals so far mostly used GANs, and most labels are slide-level, so counterfactuals must work with multiple-instance learning (MIL).

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 数据预处理:WSI 统一到 0.5 μm/像素,切成 512×512 tile,只保留病理标注的肿瘤区域;NCT-CRC-HE-100K 用 224×224 的现成 patch。
  2. Diffusion autoencoder:CNN 编码器输出语义向量 z(512 维),DDPM 以 z 为条件重建图像;两者用噪声预测损失联合训练,训到 FID < 20,推理用 DDIM。
  3. 分类器:tile 级标签用 one-vs-rest 逻辑回归;slide/患者级标签用 transformer 型 MIL(cross-attention + self-attention 池化),五折交叉验证。
  4. 方向 d:线性分类器取权重向量;MIL 分类器取目标类 logit 对该 tile 的 z 的梯度。
  5. 生成反事实:z' = z + α·d(再按特征维度 512 做缩放,Eq. C),与原图反演得到的噪声 x_T 一起解码;α 逐步增大,得到一串渐变图;每张 WSI 取贡献最大的 5 个 tile。
  6. 染色 vs 形态:用 Vahadane 染色迁移造两张混合图(原形态 + 反事实染色;原染色 + 反事实形态),把 logit 变化按 Shapley 方式分成染色和形态两部分。
  7. 验证:翻转率随 α 的变化;用 UNI2 / Virchow2 / CONCH 训练的独立分类器检查方向;DeepCMorph 核分割统计细胞组成;病理医生结构化问卷和盲法真假判别。
  1. Tile WSIs at 0.5 µm/px into 512×512 tumour-region tiles (224×224 for NCT-CRC-HE-100K).
  2. Train a diffusion autoencoder: CNN encoder → z (512-d), DDPM conditioned on z, joint noise-prediction loss until FID < 20; DDIM at inference.
  3. Train a classifier on z: one-vs-rest logistic regression (tile labels) or a transformer MIL (slide labels), five-fold CV.
  4. Direction d = linear weights, or gradient of the target-class MIL logit w.r.t. the tile's z.
  5. Decode z + α·d (scaled for the 512 feature dimensions, Eq. C) with the tile's inverted noise x_T for increasing α; top-5 tiles per WSI.
  6. Split the logit change into stain vs morphology with Vahadane hybrids and a Shapley-style decomposition.
  7. Validate with flip rate vs α, independent UNI2 / Virchow2 / CONCH classifiers, DeepCMorph cell counts, pathologist surveys and a blinded realism test.

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理切片染色。
MSI / MSIH
微卫星不稳定 / 高度不稳定,结直肠癌的一种分子分型,和免疫治疗相关。
MIL 多示例学习
只有整张切片(一袋 tile)的标签时,学习从 tile 集合预测标签的方法。
FFPE / 冰冻切片
福尔马林固定石蜡包埋(常规)与冰冻切片;两者外观不同,是一种域偏移。
LUAD / LUSC
肺腺癌 / 肺鳞癌。
HCC / CCA
肝细胞癌 / 胆管癌。
ILC / IDC
乳腺浸润性小叶癌 / 浸润性导管癌。
MSI / MSIH
microsatellite instability (high), a colorectal-cancer molecular subtype
MIL
learning a slide label from a bag of tiles
FFPE vs frozen
routine paraffin sections vs frozen sections; a domain shift

数据Data

训练:NCT-CRC-HE-100K(10 万张 224×224 patch,9 类组织;测试 CRC-VAL-HE-7K 7,180 张)、TCGA-CRC(588 张 WSI / 582 例)、TCGA-BRCA(1,115 WSI / 1,046 例)、TCGA 泛癌 tile 集(32 种癌)。外部验证:CPTAC-COAD(105 例,24 例 MSIH)、CPTAC 乳腺(100 例)、CPTAC 肺(333 例 / 1,534 WSI)均为冰冻切片,以及 Charité 肝癌队列(496 例)。

Training: NCT-CRC-HE-100K (100k patches, 9 classes; test CRC-VAL-HE-7K, 7,180), TCGA-CRC (588 WSIs / 582 patients), TCGA-BRCA (1,115 WSIs), TCGA pan-cancer tiles (32 cancers). External (frozen): CPTAC-COAD (105 patients, 24 MSIH), CPTAC breast (100), CPTAC lung (333 / 1,534 WSIs); plus a Charité liver cohort (496).

主要结果Key results

重建 MS-SSIM 0.966–0.992;合成图 FID 4.71–36.95Table 1
CRC 组织分类 AUC:正常黏膜 0.91、肿瘤上皮 0.98Results §Tissue type
MSI MIL 五折 AUC 0.73 ± 0.08,CPTAC-COAD 外部 0.67Results §MSI
朝 MSIH 的 logit 变化 +8.9 中,形态 +7.4、染色 +1.1;108 例全部形态主导Fig. 5B
4 位评估者盲评 300 张,合成图 41.7% 被判为真Results; Fig. S12
肝 HCC vs CCA:MIL AUC 0.96,外部 0.85,α = 0.08 时 100% 翻转Fig. 6B
肺 LUAD vs LUSC:MIL AUC 0.91,外部 0.77,α = 0.06 时 98.8% 翻转Fig. 7C
Reconstruction MS-SSIM 0.966–0.992; synthetic FID 4.71–36.95Table 1
CRC tissue AUC: normal mucosa 0.91, tumour epithelium 0.98Results §Tissue type
MSI MIL five-fold AUC 0.73 ± 0.08; CPTAC-COAD external 0.67Results §MSI
Toward MSIH: logit +8.9 = morphology +7.4 + stain +1.1; morphology dominates in all 108 patientsFig. 5B
4 blinded raters, 300 tiles: 41.7% of synthetic tiles judged realResults; Fig. S12
Liver HCC vs CCA: MIL AUC 0.96, external 0.85, 100% flipped at α = 0.08Fig. 6B
Lung LUAD vs LUSC: MIL AUC 0.91, external 0.77, 98.8% flipped at α = 0.06Fig. 7C

结论Conclusions

作者认为:MoPaDi 生成的反事实图像逼真,能让病理医生看到与分类器预测相关的形态特征,补充常规的高注意力区域检查;在 MSI 任务中预测变化主要来自形态而非染色。反事实只代表模型内部的模拟,不代表生物因果。

The authors conclude that MoPaDi produces realistic counterfactuals that let pathologists see classifier-linked morphology, complementing attention inspection, and that MSI predictions were mainly morphology- rather than stain-driven. Counterfactuals are model-internal simulations, not biological causality.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:只在 tile 层面;每个数据集要单独训练昂贵的 autoencoder;未系统评估放大倍数。
  • 作者:解释质量依赖分类器;MSI 分类器只有中等性能,反事实更多是在审计模型。
  • 作者:Vahadane 无法完美分离染色和结构;未评估折叠、气泡等伪影,分类器学到的伪影线索可能被保留或强化。
  • 我们的理解:病理医生评估规模小(如 MSI 任务 52 个转变、3 位病理医生),且尚未与热图方法做正式对照。
  • Authors: tile-level only; a costly autoencoder per dataset; magnification not studied.
  • Authors: explanations are only as good as the classifier; the MSI model is moderate.
  • Authors: stain/structure separation is approximate; artifacts (folds, bubbles) not evaluated and may be reinforced.
  • Our reading: expert studies are small (e.g. 52 MSI transitions, 3 pathologists) and there is no formal comparison with heatmaps yet.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是 detect → model → revert 中'回复'的图像版原型——把肿瘤 tile 推向'正常'并画出来。方向 d 目前来自有监督分类器;把它换成异常分数的梯度,就能做'把异常拉回健康'的反事实(论文未做)。对肾脏 Topic 1,染色–形态分解和多编码器一致性检查可直接用来区分批次伪影与真实形态改变。

Our reading: an imaging prototype of 'revert' — push a tumour tile toward 'normal' and render it. The direction comes from a supervised classifier; swapping in an anomaly-score gradient would give revert-to-healthy counterfactuals (not done in the paper). For kidney Topic 1, the stain–morphology split and multi-encoder agreement check can help separate batch artifacts from real morphologic change.

06

Deep learning-based pathomics signature predicts prognosis and treatment response in gastric cancer: a multicenter retrospective study

Wang H, Li H, Ma K, et al. Deep learning-based pathomics signature predicts prognosis and treatment response in gastric cancer: a multicenter retrospective study. npj Precis Oncol. 2026;10:206.

doi:10.1038/s41698-026-01381-6 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
PDF 全文(pdftotext;开放获取 CC BY-NC-ND 4.0);补充材料未读
full PDF via pdftotext (open access, CC BY-NC-ND 4.0); supplementary material not read
用多尺度图神经网络 + 门控注意力 MIL 从胃癌 H&E 全切片提特征,再用 LASSO-Cox 选 11 个特征组成线性签名 PSGC;它在 5 个队列(3,138 例)中都能区分总生存,并被用来分层化疗获益和推测免疫治疗反应。
A multi-scale graph + gated-attention MIL model extracts features from gastric-cancer H&E slides, LASSO-Cox keeps 11 of them in a linear signature (PSGC) that separates overall survival in 5 cohorts (3,138 patients) and is used to stratify chemotherapy benefit and infer immunotherapy response.

问题Problem

胃癌预后主要靠 TNM 分期,但同分期患者结局差异很大;分子标志物又贵又慢。作者想用常规 H&E 切片做一个便宜、可解释的签名,预测总生存,并判断谁能从辅助化疗或免疫治疗获益。

Gastric-cancer prognosis relies on TNM staging, but outcomes vary widely within a stage and molecular markers are costly. The authors want a cheap, interpretable H&E signature that predicts overall survival and indicates who benefits from adjuvant chemotherapy or immunotherapy.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 预处理:20× 扫描,切 512×512 patch,去背景,Macenko 染色归一化,数据增强。
  2. 多尺度 bag:每张 WSI 在 10× 和 20× 各取一组 patch,用预训练 Swin Transformer 提 1024 维特征。
  3. 图 + GCN:同一尺度内 patch 为节点,特征余弦相似度 > δ 时连边(按特征相似,不是空间邻接),GCN 更新节点特征。
  4. 多头门控注意力 MIL 把节点特征汇总为 bag 向量,两个尺度拼接成 2048 维表示(训练目标在正文中未写明)。
  5. LASSO-Cox(10 折交叉验证)在训练队列选出 11 个特征,得到 PSGC 线性公式;阈值 −1.37 分高/低风险,验证队列直接套用。
  6. 评估:KM、RMST、多因素 Cox、E-value、时间依赖 ROC、校准、决策曲线,以及相对 TNM 的 C-index / NRI / IDI 增量。
  7. 治疗与机制:II/III 期按是否化疗分组比较;oncoPredict、TIDE、ImmuCellAI、submap 做药敏和免疫反应预测;SHAP、热图、patch 聚类 + 病理医生盲评;GSEA、WGCNA、xCell 做转录组关联。
  1. Tile 20× slides into 512×512 patches; background removal, Macenko normalization, augmentation.
  2. Two-scale bags (10×, 20×) encoded by a pretrained Swin Transformer (D = 1024).
  3. Per-scale graphs with edges for feature cosine similarity > δ; GCN; multi-head gated-attention pooling; concatenate scales (training target not stated in the main text).
  4. LASSO-Cox (10-fold CV) selects 11 features into the PSGC formula; cutoff −1.37 applied to validation cohorts.
  5. Evaluate with KM, RMST, multivariable Cox, E-values, time-dependent ROC, calibration, DCA, and C-index / NRI / IDI over TNM.
  6. Explore treatment and biology: chemotherapy subgroups, oncoPredict / TIDE / ImmuCellAI / submap predictions, SHAP, patch clustering with blinded pathologists, GSEA / WGCNA / xCell.

术语Terms

TNM 分期
按原发肿瘤(T)、淋巴结(N)、远处转移(M)给癌症分期的临床标准。
总生存 OS
从手术到任何原因死亡(或末次随访)的时间。
RMST 限制平均生存时间
在固定时间点(如 60 个月)之前,平均能活多少个月;两组的差值直观表示获益。
辅助化疗
根治性手术后进行的化疗;本文方案为 XELOX 或 SOX。
R0 切除
显微镜下切缘无肿瘤残留的手术切除。
肠上皮化生
胃黏膜被肠型上皮替代的癌前改变,常与幽门螺杆菌感染相关。
TIDE
根据肿瘤表达谱预测免疫检查点抑制剂反应的算法(预测,不是实测)。
TNM
clinical cancer staging by tumour, nodes and metastasis
RMST
mean survival time up to a fixed horizon; group differences are in months
TIDE
an algorithm that predicts checkpoint-inhibitor response from expression (a prediction, not an observation)

数据Data

5 个队列共 3,138 例胃癌患者:训练 HMUCH 1,257 例;验证 HMUSH 752、QMUSH 540、YKPH 319、TCGA-STAD 270。中位年龄 60 岁,男性 71.64%,进展期 69.50%。训练队列 193 例 mRNA-seq(GEO GSE184336、GSE179252)加 TCGA 225 例用于机制分析。

3,138 gastric-cancer patients in 5 cohorts: training HMUCH 1,257; validation HMUSH 752, QMUSH 540, YKPH 319, TCGA-STAD 270. Median age 60, 71.64% male, 69.50% advanced. 193 training-cohort mRNA-seq (GEO GSE184336, GSE179252) plus 225 TCGA samples for mechanism analyses.

主要结果Key results

5 个队列高/低 PSGC 的 OS 差异均为 log-rank p < 0.0001Fig. 2A
60 个月 RMST 差:训练 −21.0 个月,4 个验证队列 −17.4 至 −21.9 个月Table 2
PSGC + TNM 的时间依赖 AUROC 高于单用 TNM,NRI / IDI 在多个时间点为正Fig. 2B; Table 3
高 PSGC 且接受辅助化疗者预后更好,低 PSGC 者无类似获益Fig. 3A–B
免疫治疗(算法预测)TIDE 149/246 vs 48/172,OR 0.253Results; Supplementary Fig. 6B
SHAP:feature17 最重要,其次 feature8、feature3、feature34Fig. 4A
高风险 patch 簇为低分化腺癌伴肿瘤–间质纤维化;低风险簇为上皮内瘤变和肠上皮化生Figs. 4C–D, 5A
高 PSGC 富集肌生成、血管生成、EMT 通路;低 PSGC 富集 DNA 修复、增殖、代谢Fig. 6A
High vs low PSGC OS differs with log-rank p < 0.0001 in all 5 cohortsFig. 2A
60-month RMST difference: −21.0 months (training), −17.4 to −21.9 months (validation)Table 2
PSGC + TNM beats TNM alone on time-dependent AUROC; positive NRI / IDIFig. 2B; Table 3
High-PSGC patients who received adjuvant chemotherapy did better; low-PSGC did notFig. 3A–B
Predicted immunotherapy response: TIDE 149/246 vs 48/172, OR 0.253Results; Supplementary Fig. 6B
High-risk clusters: poorly differentiated adenocarcinoma with tumour–stroma fibrosisFigs. 4C–D, 5A
High PSGC enriched for myogenesis, angiogenesis and EMT pathwaysFig. 6A

结论Conclusions

作者认为:PSGC 是胃癌总生存的独立预后因素,加入 TNM 后能显著提高预后预测,并可能帮助识别最能从辅助治疗获益的患者;需要大规模前瞻性多中心研究验证。

The authors conclude that PSGC is an independent prognostic factor that improves on TNM and may identify patients who benefit from adjuvant therapy, pending large prospective multicentre validation.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:回顾性设计,有隐藏混杂;TCGA 随访短。
  • 作者:化疗获益来自小亚组,未控制 ECOG、淋巴结检出数、切缘、腹膜受累;GDSC / TIDE 预测是假设性的;未分析 PD-L1、MSI、MMR。
  • 我们的理解:高 PSGC 组既'更能从化疗获益'又被预测'对化疗药更耐药',论文没有解释这一矛盾。
  • 我们的理解:正文未说明 11 个 featureN 如何从网络得到、网络训练目标和 δ 取值;无代码,数据仅应要求提供;主要 C-index 和 HR 只在补充材料中。
  • Authors: retrospective, hidden confounders, short TCGA follow-up.
  • Authors: chemotherapy benefit from a small, under-adjusted subgroup; GDSC / TIDE predictions are hypothetical; PD-L1, MSI, MMR not analysed.
  • Our reading: the high-PSGC group is both 'more likely to benefit' from chemotherapy and predicted 'more drug-resistant' — unexplained.
  • Our reading: how the 11 featureN are derived, the training target and δ are not stated; no code; data on request; key C-index / HR only in the supplement.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是有监督的'WSI → 预后签名'论文,与 detect → model → revert 关系间接。注意力和 Grad-CAM 给出的是'风险区域'而非无监督异常;转录组和药敏关联只是相关或算法推断,不能直接用于'哪些基因能逆转异常'。可借鉴的是多尺度图 + 门控注意力 MIL 的编码骨架,以及完整的预后评估清单(RMST、E-value、DCA、NRI/IDI),作为肾脏 H&E 预后基线的参考。

Our reading: a supervised WSI → prognostic-signature paper, only indirectly related to detect → model → revert. Attention / Grad-CAM gives supervised risk regions, not label-free anomalies, and the transcriptomic links are correlational. Useful as a reference encoder (multi-scale graph + gated MIL) and as a complete prognostic-reporting checklist for a kidney H&E baseline.

07

A Deep Learning-Based Pathomics Methodology for Quantifying and Characterizing Nucleated Cells in the Bone Marrow Microenvironment

Krichevsky S, Ouseph MM, Zhang Y, Abu-Zeinah G, Scandura JM, Gupta R. A Deep Learning-Based Pathomics Methodology for Quantifying and Characterizing Nucleated Cells in the Bone Marrow Microenvironment. Blood. 2023;142(Suppl 1):2294.

doi:10.1182/blood-2023-191272 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

仅摘要abstract only无本地 PDFno local PDF
仅会议摘要(ASH 2023,Blood 增刊;摘要原文取自 Crossref,含方法和数值结果);摘要图 1–2 未见
conference abstract only (ASH 2023, Blood supplement; verbatim abstract from Crossref, with methods and numbers); abstract Figures 1–2 not seen
在 54 例真性红细胞增多症(PV)患者的骨髓活检 H&E 切片上,用深度学习分割细胞核、用经典图像分析分割脂肪,自动估计骨髓细胞密度并和病理报告比较;基于细胞的估计明显好于基于组织面积的估计。
On bone-marrow H&E slides from 54 polycythemia vera patients, deep-learning nuclear segmentation plus classical fat segmentation estimates marrow cellularity automatically; a cell-based estimate agrees with pathology reports far better than a tissue-area estimate.

问题Problem

骨髓细胞密度是 WHO 区分骨髓增殖性肿瘤(MPN)的标准之一,但病理医生的人工估计主观、不能规模化,而且只给一个全局数,忽略骨髓内部的区域差异。作者想用深度学习做客观、分区域的估计。

Marrow cellularity is a WHO criterion for MPNs, but manual estimates are subjective, unscalable and give one global number. The authors want an objective, region-resolved estimate.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 数据:54 例人工确认的 PV 患者(17% 新诊断、61% 已治疗、17% 进展)的骨髓 H&E 全切片,病理报告中的活检长度和细胞密度作为参考。
  2. 核分割:比较 3 个模型——作者团队的 U-Net、HistoCartography(图神经网络)、CellPose。
  3. 用颜色/形态规则分割脂肪细胞和可评估的骨髓区域,排除血管和组织撕裂。
  4. 两种细胞密度:组织法 = 非脂肪组织 / 可评估区域;细胞法 = 有核细胞面积与脂肪细胞面积之比。
  5. 评估:与报告比较,数值用相关系数 r 和组内相关 ICC,分类用 Cohen's κ;活检长度看平均差。
  6. 探索:提取每个核的形状、纹理、颜色特征,用 kNN 和 K-means 聚类比较 PV 不同阶段,并画细胞密度热图。
  1. 54 manually confirmed PV patients (17% new, 61% treated, 17% progressing); reports give core size and cellularity as reference.
  2. Compare three nuclear segmenters: the group's U-Net, HistoCartography, CellPose.
  3. Colour/morphology segmentation of fat cells and evaluable marrow, excluding vessels and tears.
  4. Tissue-based cellularity (non-fat / evaluable area) vs cell-based (nucleated-cell vs fat-cell area).
  5. Agreement with reports via r, ICC and Cohen's κ; core size via mean difference.
  6. Cluster nuclear shape/texture/colour features (kNN, K-means) and draw cellularity heatmaps.

术语Terms

骨髓细胞密度 cellularity
骨髓中造血细胞相对脂肪所占的比例;正常约为 (100 − 年龄) ± 20%。
MPN 骨髓增殖性肿瘤
骨髓造血细胞过度增殖的一类血液肿瘤。
PV 真性红细胞增多症
一种 MPN,红细胞过多,骨髓细胞密度偏高。
MF 骨髓纤维化
另一种 MPN,骨髓被纤维组织取代。
ICC 组内相关系数
衡量两种测量数值一致程度的指标,比相关系数更严格。
Cohen's κ
两种分类结果去除偶然一致后的一致性指标。
Cellularity
share of haematopoietic cells vs fat in marrow; normal ≈ (100 − age) ± 20%
PV / MF
polycythemia vera / myelofibrosis, two myeloproliferative neoplasms
ICC
intra-class correlation, a stricter agreement measure than r

数据Data

单中心(Weill Cornell Medicine)54 例 PV 患者的骨髓活检 H&E 全切片,配有化验、分子检测和病理报告。摘要未给扫描参数和切片数。

Single centre (Weill Cornell Medicine): bone-marrow H&E WSIs of 54 PV patients with labs, molecular studies and pathology reports. Scanner and slide counts not reported.

主要结果Key results

估计与实测活检长度相差 3 ± 4 mmAbstract, Results
组织法细胞密度:r = 0.2、ICC = 0.0、κ = 0.2Abstract, Results
细胞法 + 作者 U-Net:r = 0.7、ICC = 0.6、κ = 0.7Abstract, Results
细胞法 + HistoCartography:r = 0.7、ICC = 0.5、κ = 0.6;+ CellPose:r = 0.2、ICC = 0.2、κ = 0.7Abstract, Results
细胞大小聚类中占多数的 Int1 类(5.1–9 μm)可能包含红细胞和髓系祖细胞Abstract, Results
K-means 聚类分开了新诊断和进展期 PV(无定量指标)Abstract, Figure 2
Core size estimate within 3 ± 4 mm of measurementAbstract, Results
Tissue-based cellularity: r = 0.2, ICC = 0.0, κ = 0.2Abstract, Results
Cell-based with authors' U-Net: r = 0.7, ICC = 0.6, κ = 0.7Abstract, Results
HistoCartography r = 0.7, ICC = 0.5, κ = 0.6; CellPose r = 0.2, ICC = 0.2, κ = 0.7Abstract, Results
K-means separated new from progressing PV (no number given)Abstract, Figure 2

结论Conclusions

作者认为:这项 pilot 研究得到了一个实用、可解释的 pathomics 流程,可作为假设驱动研究的概念验证;下一步是更大的诊断期队列、其他 MPN 亚型,并与免疫细胞检测等工具结合。

The authors present a practical, interpretable pathomics pipeline as a proof of concept, to be extended to larger diagnostic cohorts, other MPN subtypes and immune-cell detection.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:pilot 研究,需要更大队列和其他 MPN 亚型。
  • 我们的理解:仅 54 例、单中心、只有 PV;参考标准是病理报告,而报告本身就是作者所批评的主观估计。
  • 我们的理解:最好的一致性 r = 0.7 / ICC = 0.6 只是中等;聚类结论没有数值和统计检验;无全文、无代码。
  • Authors: pilot study only.
  • Our reading: 54 PV patients, one centre; reference = pathology reports, the very estimates criticised.
  • Our reading: best agreement is moderate; clustering claim has no statistics; no full paper or code.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:与 detect → model → revert 关系间接,价值在组织表征:'核分割 → 每个细胞的形态特征 → 区域统计/热图'正是肾脏 H&E 细胞级描述要做的事。它也提醒我们,换一个分割模型结果差别很大(CellPose ICC 0.2 vs 作者 U-Net 0.6),参考标准本身也可能不可靠,所以肾脏基准要把分割器当变量报告,并用多模态证据交叉核对。

Our reading: indirectly relevant — a cell-level tissue representation ('segment nuclei → per-cell features → regional maps'). It shows that segmenter choice can swing results (CellPose ICC 0.2 vs 0.6) and that the reference standard may be unreliable, so a kidney benchmark should report segmenters as a variable and cross-check with other modalities.

08

Cassie07/PathOmics

Ding K, Zhou M, Metaxas DN, Zhang S. Pathology-and-genomics Multimodal Transformer for Survival Outcome Prediction. MICCAI 2023:622–631. Code: Cassie07/PathOmics.

https://github.com/Cassie07/PathOmics · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

网页 / READMEweb page / README无本地 PDFno local PDF
GitHub README(2026-10-27 读取)及其中的 Figure 1;MICCAI 2023 论文正文未读
GitHub README (read 2026-10-27) and its Figure 1; the MICCAI 2023 paper itself was not read
PathOmics 是 MICCAI 2023 Oral 论文的官方代码:先无监督预训练对齐病理切片嵌入和基因组(miRNA)嵌入,再微调做癌症生存预测,微调时可用双模态,也可只用一种模态。
Official code of a MICCAI 2023 Oral paper: unsupervised pretraining aligns pathology-slide and genomics (miRNA) embeddings, then finetuning predicts cancer survival from both modalities or just one.

问题Problem

按 README:要融合病理图像和基因组数据做预后预测,并让微调阶段能灵活处理多模态或单模态数据。我们的理解:后者对应临床上常见的缺模态情况。

Per the README: fuse pathology images and genomics for prognosis, with finetuning that flexibly handles multi- or single-modal data. Our reading: the latter addresses missing modalities.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 图像:从 TCGA-COAD / READ 下载 WSI,切前景 patch,用 ImageNet 预训练 ResNet50/101 提特征,每张切片存一个 .npz。
  2. 组学:从 cBioPortal 下载 coadread_tcga_pan_can_atlas_2018,示例用 miRNA,并分组。
  3. 预训练:patch 组经 Attention Refiner + Image-wise Transformer + 全局注意力池化(GAP)得到图像嵌入;组学组经 SNN + Omics-wise Transformer + GAP 得到组学嵌入;两者无监督对齐(示例损失 MSE)。
  4. 微调:双模态时两种嵌入融合(示例 concat)后接风险分类器;单模态时只用一种嵌入接风险分类器,输出生存预测。
  5. 实验设置:COAD 4 折交叉验证 + 留出集评估;或 COAD 预训练、READ 微调与评估(5 折);可选少数据微调(--less_data)。
  1. Tile TCGA-COAD / READ WSIs into foreground patches; ImageNet ResNet features, one .npz per slide.
  2. Download cBioPortal coadread_tcga_pan_can_atlas_2018 omics (miRNA in the example), grouped.
  3. Pretrain: image branch (attention refiner, image-wise transformer, global attention pooling) and omics branch (SNN, omics-wise transformer, GAP), aligned without labels (MSE in the example).
  4. Finetune with fused embeddings (concat) or a single embedding into a risk classifier for survival.
  5. Settings: COAD 4-fold CV + hold-out, or COAD pretrain → READ finetune (5-fold); optional data-efficient mode.

术语Terms

TCGA
美国癌症基因组图谱,提供配对的病理切片和多组学数据。
COAD / READ
结肠腺癌 / 直肠腺癌。
miRNA
微小 RNA,调控基因表达的短 RNA 分子,这里作为组学特征。
生存预测
预测患者的风险或生存时间,常用 C-index 评估。
TCGA
The Cancer Genome Atlas: paired slides and multi-omics
miRNA
short regulatory RNAs, used here as omics features

数据Data

TCGA-COAD 和 TCGA-READ 的 WSI(需自行下载);组学来自 cBioPortal 的 coadread_tcga_pan_can_atlas_2018。README 没有给出病例数,仓库不含数据和特征。

TCGA-COAD and TCGA-READ WSIs (user-downloaded) and cBioPortal coadread_tcga_pan_can_atlas_2018 omics. No case counts in the README; no data or features shipped.

主要结果Key results

README 未给出任何数值结果(无 C-index、无对比表)README
论文被 MICCAI 2023 接收为 Oral,README 标注 top 9%README 开头 / 仓库描述
README 不提供预训练权重,--load_model_finetune 需要自己先训好README "Usage"
README 末尾的病理–基因组多模态方法文献表共 48 条(2019 至 2025.12)README 文献表

Literature table of 48 pathology–genomics multimodal methods (2019–2025.12)README table

结论Conclusions

README 的说法:有了多模态预训练 backbone,多模态或单模态数据都可以用于微调做预后预测。具体性能见论文。

Per the README, multimodal pretrained backbones let either multi- or single-modal data be used for prognostic finetuning; metrics are in the paper.

局限Limitations

  • 我们的理解:只覆盖 TCGA 结直肠癌 + miRNA;特征提取建议用 ImageNet ResNet 而非病理基础模型。
  • 我们的理解:无数值结果、无权重,复现要从下载 TCGA 开始。
  • 我们的理解:任务是 slide / 患者级生存预测,不做空间定位、异常检测或扰动。
  • Our reading: only TCGA colorectal + miRNA; ImageNet ResNet features rather than pathology foundation models.
  • Our reading: no numbers or weights; reproduction starts from raw TCGA.
  • Our reading: slide-level survival only; no spatial localisation, anomaly detection or perturbation.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:属于组织表征 / 多模态建模这一环。'多模态预训练、单模态也能用'的思路适合肾脏项目中并非每个样本都有 snRNA / Xenium 的情况;但要支持'模态不一致即异常',需要改成 spot / 细胞级的空间对齐并设计不一致分数,仓库不提供。README 文献表是了解病理–基因组融合方法的快速入口。

Our reading: a multimodal-representation component. 'Pretrain multimodal, use single-modal' fits kidney samples that lack snRNA / Xenium, but cross-modal-disagreement anomaly detection would need spot/cell-level alignment and a disagreement score, which the repo does not provide. The README table is a handy map of fusion methods.

病理基础模型与基准Pathology foundation models & benchmarks

09

A foundation model for clinical-grade computational pathology and rare cancers detection

Vorontsov E, Bozkurt A, Casson A, Shaikovski G, et al. A foundation model for clinical-grade computational pathology and rare cancers detection. Nature Medicine. 2024;30:2924–2935.

doi:10.1038/s41591-024-03141-0 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
Nature Medicine 出版版 PDF 全文(pdftotext,CC BY 4.0);架构细节另核对 Hugging Face 模型卡
full published PDF via pdftotext (CC BY 4.0); architecture details cross-checked on the Hugging Face model card
Virchow 是用 DINOv2 在 MSKCC 约 150 万张 H&E 切片上训练的 632M 参数 ViT-H/14;在它的冻结嵌入上训练的泛癌检测器在 16 种癌上标本级 AUC 0.950,罕见癌 0.937。
Virchow is a 632M-parameter ViT-H/14 trained with DINOv2 on ~1.5M MSKCC H&E slides; a pan-cancer detector on its frozen embeddings reaches specimen-level AUC 0.950 over 16 cancers and 0.937 on rare cancers.

问题Problem

针对单一癌种训练的病理模型只见过部分组织形态,泛化差,也难以处理罕见癌、少见基因变异这类数据少的任务。以往病理基础模型用 3–40 万张切片、最多 3.07 亿参数。作者想验证:百万级切片 + 6 亿参数级模型,能否用一个通用嵌入支撑泛癌检测和生物标志物预测,并在标注更少时接近商用临床级模型。

Cancer-specific pathology models see only part of tissue morphology and struggle on data-poor tasks such as rare cancers. Can a million-slide, 600M-parameter foundation model support pan-cancer detection and biomarker prediction and approach commercial clinical-grade models with fewer labels?

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 数据:MSKCC 去标识的 1,488,550 张 H&E WSI(119,629 名患者、17 个组织大类),Leica 扫描,20×(0.5 µm/px)。
  2. 切 tile:16× 下采样后用 HSV 阈值找组织,收集组织面积 ≥ 25% 的不重叠 224 px tile;从约 130 亿个 tile 中采样 20 亿个训练。
  3. 自监督:ViT-H/14(632M)用 DINOv2 训练——学生在被遮挡的全局/局部视图上匹配教师(学生的 EMA)的 class token 和被遮挡的 patch token;AdamW、fp16、131,072 个 prototype。
  4. 嵌入:每个 tile 取 class token 与 256 个 patch token 均值拼接,得 2,560 维向量;模型冻结,不微调。
  5. 聚合:Agata 聚合器做弱监督 MIL——一个可学习 query 对标本内所有 tile 做 cross-attention(内存线性),用报告抽取的标本级癌/非癌标签训练,交叉熵、25 epoch。
  6. 评测:16 种癌的标本级 AUC 与 95% 灵敏度下特异度(DeLong + Holm 检验),对比 UNI、Phikon、CTransPath 和三款 Paige 商用模型;9 个生物标志物;8 个 tile 级 linear probe 基准。
  7. 失败分析:病理医生复核误检和漏检病例,并归类原因。
  1. Tile 1,488,550 MSKCC H&E WSIs (20×) into 224 px tissue tiles; sample 2B of ~13B tiles.
  2. Train a ViT-H/14 (632M) with DINOv2: the student matches an EMA teacher's class token and masked patch tokens across global/local crops.
  3. Embed each tile as CLS + mean of 256 patch tokens (2,560-d); keep the encoder frozen.
  4. Train the Agata attention-MIL aggregator on specimen-level cancer labels extracted from reports.
  5. Evaluate AUC and specificity at 95% sensitivity on 16 cancers against UNI, Phikon, CTransPath and three Paige products; plus 9 biomarkers and 8 tile-level linear-probe benchmarks; pathologist review of errors.

术语Terms

WSI 全切片图像
整张病理玻片的数字扫描,常达数万 × 数万像素。
H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理染色。
标本 specimen
一次取材得到的组织样本,可切出多个蜡块和多张切片;诊断标签通常在标本级。
MIL 多示例学习
一袋 tile 只有一个整体标签,模型需从众多 tile 中聚合出判断。
罕见癌
美国 NCI 定义:年发病率低于每 10 万人 15 例的癌症。
癌前病变 precursor
尚未浸润的异常病变(如高级别异型增生),形态上可能很像癌。
MSK-IMPACT
MSKCC 的靶向测序 panel,用来确定基因突变等生物标志物标签。
specimen
one tissue sample, possibly several blocks and slides; diagnoses are usually specimen-level
MIL
multiple-instance learning: one label for a bag of tiles
precursor lesion
non-invasive abnormal lesion that can look like cancer

数据Data

预训练:1,488,550 张 H&E 切片、119,629 名患者、392,268 个标本,活检 63%、切除 37%。泛癌测试集:22,932 张切片、6,142 个标本(常见癌 3,547、罕见癌 2,595;MSKCC 内部 3,033、外部机构 3,109)。另有 9 个生物标志物数据集和 8 个公开/内部 tile 基准。

Pretraining: 1,488,550 H&E slides from 119,629 patients (MSKCC). Pan-cancer test set: 22,932 slides from 6,142 specimens (3,547 common, 2,595 rare cancers; 3,033 internal, 3,109 external). Plus 9 biomarker datasets and 8 tile benchmarks.

主要结果Key results

泛癌检测标本级 AUC:Virchow 0.950、UNI 0.940、Phikon 0.932、CTransPath 0.907Fig. 2b
罕见癌 AUC 0.937;宫颈 0.875、骨 0.841 最难Fig. 2a,c
95% 灵敏度下特异度 72.5%(UNI 68.9%)Fig. 2b
内部 / 外部机构 AUC 0.938 / 0.934Fig. 2e
对比商用模型:前列腺 0.980 vs 0.995,乳腺 0.985 vs 0.992,淋巴结大转移 0.999 vs 0.994Results; Fig. 3
假阳性 53.2% 为癌前病变、17.0% 为组织伪影;假阴性 45.2% 为肿瘤灶很小Fig. 3d
生物标志物 9 项中 7 项 AUC 第一;tile 基准 8 项中 7 项最佳或并列Fig. 4c; Fig. 5b
Pan-cancer AUC: Virchow 0.950, UNI 0.940, Phikon 0.932, CTransPath 0.907Fig. 2b
Rare-cancer AUC 0.937; cervix 0.875 and bone 0.841 hardestFig. 2a,c
Internal / external AUC 0.938 / 0.934Fig. 2e
Vs Paige products: prostate 0.980 vs 0.995, breast 0.985 vs 0.992, BLN macrometastases 0.999 vs 0.994Results; Fig. 3
False positives: 53.2% precursors, 17.0% artefacts; false negatives: 45.2% small fociFig. 3d
Top AUC on 7/9 biomarkers; best or tied on 7/8 tile benchmarksFig. 4c; Fig. 5b

结论Conclusions

作者认为,大规模病理基础模型同时带来泛化能力和训练数据效率:基于 Virchow 的泛癌检测器在罕见癌和外部数据上泛化良好,训练标签更少却接近商用临床级模型,并在部分罕见变体上更好。

The authors conclude that a large pathology foundation model brings both generalisation and data efficiency: the Virchow-based pan-cancer detector generalises to rare cancers and external data and nearly matches commercial clinical-grade models with fewer labels.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:训练数据来自单一中心、扫描仪类型有限。
  • 作者:tile 级嵌入需要另训聚合器;低数据任务可能需要 slide 级模型;模型大,部署可能需要蒸馏。
  • 作者:生物标志物结果统计功效不足,报告的是按测试集选出的最佳学习率下的 AUC。
  • 我们的理解:多数作者为 Paige 员工并持股,对比的商用模型也是 Paige 产品;训练数据不公开;评测只涉及癌症,没有非肿瘤肾病任务。
  • Authors: single-centre data with limited scanner types; tile-level embeddings need an aggregator; large model for deployment.
  • Authors: biomarker results underpowered; AUC reported at the best test-set learning rate.
  • Our reading: Paige-affiliated authors and comparators; proprietary data; cancer-only evaluation, no non-tumour kidney task.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:Virchow 适合作为 detect 环节 H&E 模态的冻结特征器——在健康肾组织 tile 的嵌入上建立参考分布,用 kNN 或密度模型给新 tile 打异常分数;但论文只做了监督的癌/非癌分类,这种无监督用法需要我们自己验证。它的失败分析提醒我们:癌前病变、伪影和反应性改变是主要误报来源,肾脏异常检测 benchmark 应把这些干扰单独标注。后续的 Virchow2 在 PathBench 中更强。

Our reading: use Virchow as a frozen H&E feature extractor for the detect stage — fit a reference distribution on healthy kidney tiles and score new tiles by kNN or density; the paper only tests supervised cancer classification, so this needs our own validation. Its error analysis shows precursors, artefacts and reactive changes as the main false-positive sources, which a kidney anomaly benchmark should label separately. Virchow2 is stronger in PathBench.

10

MahmoodLab/UNI2-h

Mahmood Lab. UNI2-h: pretrained ViT-H/14 for histopathology. Hugging Face model card MahmoodLab/UNI2-h. Accessed 2026-10-27.

https://huggingface.co/MahmoodLab/UNI2-h · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

网页 / READMEweb page / README无本地 PDFno local PDF
Hugging Face 公开模型卡全文(权重需申请);定量结果来自本库已读的 PathBench 论文
public Hugging Face model card (weights gated); quantitative results taken from the PathBench paper in this library
UNI2-h 是 Mahmood Lab 发布的 681M 参数 ViT-H/14 病理 backbone,用 DINOv2 配方在 Mass General Brigham 的 2 亿+ H&E 与 IHC 图块上自监督训练,每个 224×224 tile 输出 1,536 维特征。
UNI2-h is the Mahmood Lab's 681M-parameter ViT-H/14 pathology backbone, trained with the DINOv2 recipe on 200M+ H&E and IHC tiles from Mass General Brigham; it outputs a 1,536-dim feature per 224×224 tile.

问题Problem

模型卡不陈述研究问题,只定位为多用途病理视觉 backbone。我们的理解:它为计算病理提供一个通用 tile 级 encoder,让下游只需线性头或 MIL 头,而不必每个任务从头训练。

The card states no research question. Our reading: it provides a general tile-level encoder so that pathology tasks need only light linear or MIL heads.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 输入:组织病理 tile,resize 到 224,并按 ImageNet 均值方差归一化。
  2. 架构:定制 ViT-H/14——24 层、24 头、embed_dim 1,536、SwiGLU FFN、8 个 register token、LayerScale(init 1e-5),共 681M 参数。
  3. 训练数据:2 亿+ 图块,来自 MGB 的 H&E 与 IHC 切片(卡上分别写 30 万+ 与 35 万+ 张)。
  4. 目标:DINOv2 配方 = DINO 自蒸馏(multi-crop)+ iBOT 掩码图像建模 + CLS 上的 KoLeo 正则;bf16 + PyTorch-FSDP,A100 80GB。
  5. 输出:每个 tile 的 [1, 1536] CLS 特征,冻结使用。
  6. 下游:linear probe、kNN、SimpleShot 分类,最近邻检索,在一袋 CLS token 上训练 MIL 做 slide 分类;分割任务建议用 ViTDet / ViT-Adapter 微调。
  1. Resize tiles to 224 and normalise with ImageNet statistics.
  2. Custom ViT-H/14: 24 layers, 24 heads, dim 1,536, SwiGLU, 8 register tokens, 681M params.
  3. Train on 200M+ MGB H&E/IHC tiles with DINO self-distillation + iBOT masked image modelling + KoLeo (bf16, FSDP, A100).
  4. Use the frozen [1, 1536] CLS feature for linear probe / kNN / SimpleShot, retrieval and MIL; fine-tune for segmentation.

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理染色。
IHC 免疫组化
用抗体给特定蛋白染色的切片。
tile / ROI
从全切片图像上切下的小图块或感兴趣区域。
MIL 多示例学习
一张切片的所有 tile 共享一个标签,模型从中聚合出整体判断。
TCGA / CPTAC / PANDA
公开的癌症病理数据集;官方为它们提供了预提取的 UNI2-h 特征。
H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理染色。
IHC 免疫组化
用抗体给特定蛋白染色的切片。
tile / ROI
从全切片图像上切下的小图块或感兴趣区域。
MIL 多示例学习
一张切片的所有 tile 共享一个标签,模型从中聚合出整体判断。
TCGA / CPTAC / PANDA
公开的癌症病理数据集;官方为它们提供了预提取的 UNI2-h 特征。

数据Data

预训练:2 亿+ 图块,采样自 Mass General Brigham 的 H&E 与 IHC 切片;切片数卡内写法不一(30 万+ / 35 万+),PathBench 表格记为 0.35M WSI。卡上没有染色比例、倍率或器官分布。官方另提供 TCGA、CPTAC、PANDA 的预提取特征。

200M+ tiles from MGB H&E and IHC slides (300k+ or 350k+ slides — the card says both). No stain, magnification or organ breakdown. Pre-extracted features for TCGA, CPTAC and PANDA are provided.

主要结果Key results

模型卡本身没有任何定量基准model card
PathBench:TCGA 19 个任务平均 AUROC 0.824(95% CI 0.779–0.868),位列前五PathBench Fig. 1b
PathBench:非 TCGA 22 个任务平均 AUROC 0.824PathBench Fig. 1c
PathBench:非 TCGA patch 级任务平均 AUROC 0.953,仅次于 Virchow2 的 0.962PathBench Fig. 2b
PathBench:按累计 balanced accuracy 从 TCGA 第 6 降到非 TCGA 第 9PathBench Fig. 5a–b
No benchmark numbers on the model cardmodel card
PathBench: mean AUROC 0.824 on 19 TCGA tasks (top five)PathBench Fig. 1b
PathBench: mean AUROC 0.953 on non-TCGA patch tasks (Virchow2 0.962)PathBench Fig. 2b
PathBench: 6th on TCGA, 9th on non-TCGA by cumulative balanced accuracyPathBench Fig. 5a–b

结论Conclusions

模型卡称冻结特征无需微调即可在 ROI 分类、检索和 slide 分类上取得有竞争力的结果,并建议分割任务做微调。卡上要求引用 UNI 论文(Chen et al., Nature Medicine 2024),该论文描述的是前代 UNI。

The card says frozen features give competitive results on ROI classification, retrieval and slide classification without fine-tuning, and recommends fine-tuning for segmentation. It asks users to cite the UNI paper, which describes the earlier UNI.

局限Limitations

  • 卡上:CC-BY-NC-ND 4.0,仅限非商业学术研究,禁止再分发,用其输出训练的模型和构建的数据集也算衍生物;需机构邮箱申请。
  • 我们的理解:没有 UNI2-h 自己的论文或技术报告,训练数据组成不透明,切片数卡内不一致。
  • 我们的理解:单一机构数据,需要检查扫描仪和染色的域偏移;只有形态特征,不涉及分子模态,也非为异常检测设计。
  • Card: CC-BY-NC-ND 4.0, non-commercial only, no redistribution, derivatives include models trained on its outputs; institutional e-mail required.
  • Our reading: no UNI2-h paper; opaque, single-institution training data; inconsistent slide count; morphology only, not built for anomaly detection.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:UNI2-h 可作为 detect 环节 H&E 模态的冻结 encoder——在健康肾组织 tile 的特征上建 kNN 或密度模型,用到最近健康特征的距离作 tile 级异常分数,再聚合到区域或活检级;也可作为 H&E 侧嵌入,与 Xenium / snRNA 做跨模态一致性检查。PathBench 中它与 Virchow2 同一梯队、差异多不显著,建议两套特征都提取对照。NC-ND 许可意味着发布 benchmark 时不能再分发其衍生特征。

Our reading: a candidate frozen H&E encoder for the detect stage — fit kNN or density models on healthy kidney tiles and score distance to healthy features, or use it as the H&E embedding for cross-modal checks against Xenium / snRNA. PathBench puts it in the same tier as Virchow2, so extract both. NC-ND forbids redistributing derived features in a released benchmark.

11

A Generative Foundation Model for Multimodal Histopathology

Xiang J, Li M, Hou S, Chen Y, et al. A Generative Foundation Model for Multimodal Histopathology. arXiv:2604.03635. 2026.

https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03635 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
arXiv v1 PDF 全文(pdftotext);模型仓库信息来自 Hugging Face API
full arXiv v1 PDF via pdftotext; model-repo info from the Hugging Face API
MuPAD 用一个带解耦跨模态注意力的扩散 Transformer,在 H&E、文本和 RNA 通路分数之间做条件生成,一个模型同时完成图像/文本/RNA→H&E 生成、冰冻→石蜡转换和 H&E→IHC/mIF 虚拟染色。
MuPAD is one diffusion transformer with decoupled cross-modal attention that generates H&E conditioned on images, text and RNA pathway scores, and also does frozen→FFPE translation and H&E→IHC/mIF virtual staining.

问题Problem

临床上组织学、分子和临床数据常常不全。已有方法每个只学一对 source→target(如 H&E→IHC),彼此不共享表征,每换一种染色或标志物都要重训。作者想知道:一个在多模态病理数据上大规模预训练的统一生成模型,能否在各任务上都超过专用模型。

Histological, molecular and clinical data are often incomplete. Existing models each learn one source→target pair, share nothing, and must be retrained for every new stain or marker. Can one generative model pretrained across pathology modalities beat the specialists on all tasks?

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 输入:约 1 亿张 512×512、20× 的 H&E patch(TCGA、GTEx、PAIP、PLCO),经冻结 VAE(8× 下采样)编码为潜变量。
  2. 条件:文本和参考图用病理视觉–语言模型 MUSK 编码;bulk RNA-seq 归一化为 TPM 后,用泛癌基因签名压缩成 331 个通路富集分数。
  3. 主干:SiT(flow-matching 扩散 Transformer)在潜空间去噪,预测在条件 {RNA, 文本, 图像} 下的干净 H&E。
  4. 解耦跨模态注意力 DCA:每个模态单独的 K/V 投影和注意力流,都以图像特征为 query,按模态是否存在相加;缺任何模态都可推理。
  5. 辅助损失:CNN projector 把中间特征对齐到冻结的 Virchow2 特征,加 CLS token 去噪损失保持全局语义;classifier-free guidance 用 10% 条件 dropout。
  6. 训练:AdamW,lr 1e-4,bf16,8 张 H100,最多 500K 步;推理 250 步 SDE。
  7. 下游:空间转录组在 MOSAIC 上微调(55 µm spot);FF→FFPE 用文本域提示 + DDIM inversion;H&E→IHC 先在 MUSK 嵌入空间做 flow matching;H&E→mIF 在 ORION 上微调。
  8. 评测:FID/KID、图像相似度、文本–图像对齐、Histoplus 细胞类型分布的 Wasserstein 距离、标志物 PCC、虚拟 IHC 的临床 AUC、few-shot 分类增益。
  1. Encode ~100M H&E patches (512×512, 20×) with a frozen VAE.
  2. Condition on MUSK text/image embeddings and on 331 pathway scores computed from TPM-normalised bulk RNA-seq.
  3. Denoise with a SiT diffusion transformer using Decoupled Cross-Attention: one K/V stream per modality, summed, so any subset of conditions works.
  4. Add a CNN alignment loss to frozen Virchow2 features and a CLS-token denoising loss; 10% condition dropout for classifier-free guidance; 8× H100, up to 500K steps.
  5. Fine-tune per task (MOSAIC spatial transcriptomics, FF→FFPE with DDIM inversion, H&E→IHC via a flow in MUSK space, H&E→mIF on ORION).
  6. Evaluate with FID/KID, similarity, cell-type Wasserstein distance, marker PCC, clinical AUC and few-shot gains.

术语Terms

H&E
苏木精–伊红染色,最常规的病理切片染色,细胞核蓝紫、胞质粉红。
IHC 免疫组化
用抗体给单个蛋白(如 ER、HER2、Ki67)染色,显示该蛋白在组织中的位置。
mIF 多重免疫荧光
在同一张切片上同时测十几个蛋白标志物的荧光成像。
FF / FFPE
冰冻切片(术中快、伪影多)与福尔马林固定石蜡包埋切片(形态好、需 12–48 小时)。
Visium spot
10x Visium 空间转录组的测量单元,直径约 55 µm,包含多个细胞的混合表达。
TPM
transcripts per million,按基因长度和测序深度标准化的表达量。
通路富集分数 pathway score
把一组相关基因的表达汇总成一个数,代表某条生物通路的活性。
IHC
antibody stain for one protein (e.g. ER, HER2, Ki67) on a tissue section
mIF
multiplex immunofluorescence: many protein markers imaged on one section
FF / FFPE
fresh-frozen (fast, artefact-prone) vs formalin-fixed paraffin-embedded (better morphology, 12–48 h)
Visium spot
55 µm spatial-transcriptomics capture spot containing several cells

数据Data

预训练:1 亿 H&E patch、160 万文本–图像对(PathGen-1.6M)、1,080 万 RNA–图像对(TCGA bulk RNA-seq 配 WSI),覆盖 34 个器官。评测:HISTAI、PathGen 留出 caption、5 个 few-shot 分类集、MOSAIC 的 5 种癌共 54,033 个 Visium spot、TCGA 肺/脑冰冻切片、IHC4BC 与 HER2Match、ORION-CRC(41 张 WSI、16 个标志物)。

Pretraining: 100M H&E patches, 1.6M text–image pairs (PathGen-1.6M), 10.8M RNA–image pairs (TCGA), 34 organs. Evaluation: HISTAI, PathGen held-out captions, five few-shot datasets, MOSAIC (5 cancers, 54,033 Visium spots), TCGA frozen sections, IHC4BC, HER2Match, ORION-CRC (41 WSIs, 16 markers).

主要结果Key results

图像→图像 FID 305.12,PixCell 602.45(-49.4%);相似度 0.63 vs 0.46Fig. 2a
文本→图像 FID 576.30 vs PathLDM 1029.62(-44.0%);文本–图像对齐 0.53 vs 0.41Fig. 2b
10-shot PanNuke 准确率 0.492→0.724(+47.2%)Fig. 3a
空间转录组→H&E 膀胱癌 FID 724.6 vs GeneFlow 1012.5;细胞类型分布 Wasserstein 距离几乎全部更低Fig. 4a–c
冰冻→石蜡(肺)FID 323.7 vs AI-FFPE 435.3Results §Fresh frozen to FFPE
虚拟 IHC 临床 AUC:Ki67 0.9772、HER2 0.9556、PR 0.8842Fig. 5c
H&E→mIF 平均 patch PCC 0.238 vs GigaTIME 0.198;slide 级 0.464 vs 0.339Fig. 6a–b
共享 cross-attention 替代 DCA 使 FID 变差 7.9–13.6%Fig. 7a
Image→image FID 305.12 vs PixCell 602.45; similarity 0.63 vs 0.46Fig. 2a
Text→image FID 576.30 vs PathLDM 1029.62Fig. 2b
10-shot PanNuke accuracy 0.492→0.724 (+47.2%)Fig. 3a
Spatial transcriptomics→H&E, bladder FID 724.6 vs GeneFlow 1012.5Fig. 4a
Virtual IHC clinical AUC: Ki67 0.9772, HER2 0.9556, PR 0.8842Fig. 5c
H&E→mIF mean patch PCC 0.238 vs GigaTIME 0.198; slide-level 0.464 vs 0.339Fig. 6a–b
Shared cross-attention instead of DCA worsens FID by 7.9–13.6%Fig. 7a

结论Conclusions

作者认为:一个在多种病理模态上预训练的统一生成模型,可以在多个跨模态任务上显著超过专用模型;各个生成任务可看作同一个跨模态生物分布的不同投影。虚拟染色结果在前瞻性验证之前只应作为产生假设的工具。

The authors conclude that a single generative model pretrained across pathology modalities substantially outperforms specialist models, and that virtual-staining outputs should be treated as hypothesis-generating until prospectively validated.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:扩散生成是随机的,分布漂移下可能产生解剖上不合理的图像,需要不确定性量化。
  • 作者:虚拟染色需要与实验室测量做前瞻性一致性验证,目前不能用于诊断。
  • 我们的理解:RNA 条件是 bulk 预训练 + 331 个通路分数,丢失了基因级和单细胞信息;Visium spot 本身是多细胞混合。
  • 我们的理解:所有评测都在肿瘤数据上,没有肾脏或非肿瘤疾病;文中有几处 Results 与 Methods 不一致(如图像→图像 baseline、IHC 数据集名单)。
  • Authors: stochastic generation may hallucinate implausible anatomy under distribution shift.
  • Authors: virtual staining needs prospective concordance validation before clinical use.
  • Our reading: RNA conditioning is bulk-derived and reduced to 331 pathway scores; no kidney or non-tumour evaluation; some Results/Methods inconsistencies (baselines, IHC datasets).

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:MuPAD 是正向生成模型,本身不输出异常分数,也不给出要改哪些基因。对 detect → model → revert,它最直接的用处是跨模态一致性:用 H&E 预测标志物或用通路分数生成 H&E,再与实测模态比较,差异大的区域作为异常候选,这与课题 1 中把多模态当作正交证据的设计一致。改动通路分数观察形态变化可作为 revert 的 what-if 模拟,但论文没有验证这种干预用法,也没有肾脏数据。

Our reading: MuPAD is a forward generator with no anomaly score. Its most direct use for detect → model → revert is cross-modal consistency: predict markers from H&E (or H&E from pathway scores), compare with the measured modality, and flag regions that disagree. Editing pathway scores to watch morphology change is a possible revert what-if, but the paper does not test interventions and has no kidney data.

12

A benchmark study of vision and pathology foundation models for computational pathology

Bareja R, Carrillo-Perez F, Zheng Y, Pizurica M, et al. A benchmark study of vision and pathology foundation models for computational pathology. Nature Communications. 2026;17:9012.

doi:10.1038/s41467-026-76004-6 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
Nature Communications 出版版 PDF 全文(pdftotext,CC BY-NC-ND 4.0)
full published PDF via pdftotext (CC BY-NC-ND 4.0)
Stanford 的 PathBench 在统一的冻结特征 + linear probe 协议下,用 41 个任务、53 个数据集、17,500+ 张 WSI 评测 32 个基础模型:病理专用视觉模型领先但头部差异多不显著,规模不能预测表现,集成在总体上更稳。
PathBench evaluates 32 foundation models under one frozen-feature + linear-probe protocol on 41 tasks, 53 datasets and >17,500 WSIs: pathology vision models lead with mostly non-significant gaps among the top, scale does not predict performance, and ensembling is more robust in aggregate.

问题Problem

病理基础模型多用 TCGA 或单一机构数据预训练,各自用不同任务和协议自评,彼此不可比,也不清楚离开 TCGA 是否仍然成立。已有 benchmark 主要只比病理编码器,任务多为弱监督分类。作者要在统一协议下比较四类模型,分析规模和集成的作用,并把 in-domain(TCGA)与域外评测分开。

Pathology foundation models are pretrained on TCGA or single-institution data and self-evaluated with different protocols, so they are not comparable, and it is unclear whether results hold outside TCGA. The authors compare four model categories under one protocol, separating in-domain (TCGA) from out-of-domain evaluation.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 任务:41 个(TCGA 19 个视为 in-domain;CPTAC 7 个、Stanford/NLST/DHMC 等 OOD slide 任务 7 个、外部 patch 基准 8 个为非 TCGA)。
  2. 预处理:WSI 在 20× 切成 256×256 tile,Otsu 去背景,每张最多 4,000 个 tile;再 resize 到各模型输入(多为 224)。
  3. 编码:32 个模型(VM、VLM、Path-VM、Path-VLM)全部冻结,每个任务提一次特征。
  4. 分类头:slide 级把 tile 特征取平均,接一个线性层(lr 0.001、30 epoch、无增强);按患者 70/30 划分;patch 基准逐图分类。
  5. 统计:测试集 bootstrap 1,000 次得 95% CI;两两 AUROC 差用 bootstrap 方差做 Wald 检验,FDR 校正;汇总为胜负矩阵和 performance score。
  6. 规模分析:ViT-S / B / L 与预训练数据 small / medium / large 分组,Welch t 检验 + FDR、Cohen's d、Spearman。
  7. 集成:非 TCGA 前五(Virchow2、H-optimus-0、Prov-GigaPath、UNI、UNI2)多数投票,用 balanced accuracy 比较。
  1. Tile WSIs at 20× into 256 px tiles (≤4,000 per WSI) and resize for each model.
  2. Extract features with 32 frozen models (VM, VLM, Path-VM, Path-VLM).
  3. Mean-pool tile features and train a linear probe (70/30 patient split); classify patches directly for external benchmarks.
  4. Compare models with 1,000 bootstraps, Wald tests and FDR; analyse model and data scale; ensemble the top five by majority vote.

术语Terms

WSI 全切片图像
整张病理玻片的数字扫描。
TCGA
癌症基因组图谱,公开的多癌种病理 + 组学数据,也常被用于预训练。
CPTAC
临床蛋白质组肿瘤分析联盟,另一套公开癌症病理 + 组学数据,这里作为独立测试。
out-of-domain (OOD)
在采集机构、预处理或任务定义上不同于 TCGA/CPTAC 的数据,如 Stanford 院内队列。
RCC 肾细胞癌
肾脏最常见的癌;本文唯一的肾脏任务是 ccRCC / ChRCC / PRCC1 / PRCC2 四分类。
通路活性 pathway activation
某条信号通路(如 mTOR、p53)是否被突变或表达改变激活,这里作为分类标签。
CPTAC
public cancer pathology + proteomics cohort used here as an independent test set
RCC
renal cell carcinoma; the only kidney task is 4-class RCC subtyping

数据Data

41 个任务、53 个数据集、17,500+ 张 WSI,任务包括诊断分型/分级、生物标志物、转录组亚型和通路活性。TCGA 19 个任务(如 IDH 突变训练 884 / 测试 368;RCC 四分类训练 194 / 测试 80),CPTAC 7 个泛癌任务,OOD 来自 Stanford 院内肺/脑队列、NLST、DHMC,另有 8 个公开 patch 基准。

41 tasks, 53 datasets, >17,500 WSIs: 19 TCGA tasks (e.g. RCC subtyping, 194 train / 80 test), 7 CPTAC tasks, OOD cohorts (Stanford lung/brain, NLST, DHMC) and 8 public patch benchmarks.

主要结果Key results

TCGA 平均 AUROC 前五:Virchow2 0.830、Prov-GigaPath 0.829、H-optimus-0 / UNI / UNI2 0.824,彼此无显著差异Fig. 1b; Suppl. Fig. 4
非 TCGA 平均 AUROC:Virchow2 0.836、UNI 0.835、H-optimus-0 0.832Fig. 1c
8 个外部 patch 任务:Virchow2 0.962,与其他头部模型差异显著(FDR p < 0.001)Fig. 2b,d
Path-VM 显著优于 Path-VLM;与通用 VM 无显著差异Fig. 3a
规模:收益主要在 TCGA 多分类任务;外部与 OOD 上效应小且不显著Fig. 4
fusion 在 TCGA 累计 balanced accuracy 第 1(0.702 vs Virchow2 0.696),非 TCGA 第 2Fig. 5a–b
排名漂移:UNI2 从 TCGA 第 6 降到非 TCGA 第 9,Virchow 从第 7 降到第 10Results; Fig. 5a–b
Top-5 mean TCGA AUROC: Virchow2 0.830, Prov-GigaPath 0.829, H-optimus-0 / UNI / UNI2 0.824; no significant differencesFig. 1b; Suppl. Fig. 4
8 external patch tasks: Virchow2 0.962, significantly above other top modelsFig. 2b,d
Path-VM > Path-VLM significantly; Path-VM vs VM not significantFig. 3a
Scaling helps mainly on TCGA multi-class tasks, not on external/OODFig. 4
Fusion 1st on TCGA balanced accuracy (0.702 vs 0.696), 2nd on non-TCGAFig. 5a–b

结论Conclusions

作者认为病理专用视觉模型在各设置中稳定领先,但头部模型差异小且依赖任务;模型规模和预训练数据量都不能单独解释性能差异;组合互补模型的集成能提升跨数据集和组织的稳健性。

The authors conclude that pathology vision models are consistently strong but top-model differences are small and task-dependent, that model and data scale do not by themselves explain performance, and that ensembling complementary models improves robustness.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:只做冻结特征 + linear probe,不代表微调后的上限。
  • 作者:TCGA / CPTAC 与预训练数据可能重叠,会抬高 in-domain 成绩。
  • 作者:规模分析用粗代理且是相关性的;slide 级 mean pooling 可能稀释局部信号。
  • 我们的理解:任务几乎都是肿瘤,肾脏只有 RCC 分型和 CPTAC 中的 CCRCC 类;没有非肿瘤疾病或异常检测任务,也不能说明哪个编码器做无监督异常检测最好。
  • Authors: linear probing only; possible TCGA/CPTAC pretraining overlap; coarse correlational scaling proxies; mean pooling.
  • Our reading: almost all tasks are oncologic, kidney only as RCC; no anomaly-detection tasks.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是评测层而非 detect → model → revert 的某一环。对课题 1:头部病理编码器同一梯队、域外排名会变,所以肾脏 H&E 应同时提 2–3 套特征在 Aachen 数据上比较或集成;它的协议(冻结特征 + 简单头、in-domain 与域外分开、bootstrap + 两两检验 + FDR)可直接搬到肾脏异常检测 benchmark,只需把 linear probe 换成异常打分器;41 个任务里没有非肿瘤病变和异常检测,这正是课题 1 要补的空白。

Our reading: an evaluation layer for Topic 1. Top encoders are one tier and rankings shift out of domain, so extract 2–3 feature sets for kidney H&E and compare or ensemble on Aachen data; reuse the protocol (frozen features, in- vs out-of-domain split, bootstrap + FDR) with anomaly scorers instead of a linear probe; the absence of non-tumour and anomaly tasks is the gap Topic 1 fills.

空间组学Spatial omics

13

Detecting anomalous anatomic regions in spatial transcriptomics with STANDS

Xu K, Lu Y, Hou S, et al. Detecting anomalous anatomic regions in spatial transcriptomics with STANDS. Nature Communications. 2024;15:8223.

doi:10.1038/s41467-024-52445-9 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
PDF 全文(pdftotext),含 Methods;Fig. 2、3 的数值从论文图中标注读出。补充材料未读。
Full PDF via pdftotext, including Methods; Fig. 2 and 3 values read from the figure labels. Supplementary material not read.
在正常参考切片上训练一个重建 GAN,把目标切片里重建得差的 spot 判为异常组织域,再跨样本对齐并聚成共享 / 样本特有的亚型,全程不需要异常 marker。
A reconstruction GAN trained on normal reference sections flags poorly reconstructed spots in target sections as anomalous tissue domains, then aligns samples and clusters anomalies into shared or sample-specific subtypes, without anomaly markers.

问题Problem

多个病人的空间转录组切片里,哪些区域被疾病改变了?哪些改变是所有病人共有、哪些只出现在个别病人?现有空间聚类方法不区分异常簇,或依赖专家给出的癌症 marker;唯一无 marker 的方法 Spatial-ID 需要带注释的单细胞参考、假阳性高、也不支持多样本。作者把这个任务称为 DDATD:检测、对齐、亚型三步。

Across several patients' spatial-transcriptomics sections, which regions has disease changed, and which changes are shared vs patient-specific? Spatial clustering does not separate anomalous clusters or needs expert cancer markers; the only marker-free method, Spatial-ID, needs an annotated single-cell reference, has many false positives and handles one sample. The authors call the task DDATD: detect, align, subtype.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 预处理:去低质量基因,归一化 + log,用 SpatialDE 选 3000 个空间可变基因;每个 spot 截 112×112 像素 H&E 图块,ResNet-34 提 256 维特征;spot 坐标建 k=6 近邻图。
  2. C1 检测模型(GAN module I):表达用两层 GATv2 编码,图像特征用另一个 GAT 编码,两者经 Transformer 融合成 512 维;memory bank 用注意力从记忆队列重组嵌入(防 mode collapse);MLP / 反卷积解码器重建表达和图块。
  3. 训练目标:只在正常参考上训练,损失 = L1 重建 + 对抗损失(判别器带梯度惩罚)。
  4. 异常分数:目标 spot 的原始与重建在判别器 16 维输出上的余弦距离;对分数拟合带先验的两分量 GMM(MAP-EM),自动划出异常 spot。
  5. 无正常 ST 时:改用正常 scRNA-seq 训练,编码器换成 MLP,把 spot 当单细胞打分。
  6. C2 对齐:去掉异常后,module II 学非负映射矩阵 M,为每个目标 spot 找最像的参考 spot(kin 对);module III 学每个批次一个风格向量,相减后解码去重建 kin 参考 spot,从而把所有目标 spot 映射到参考空间。
  7. C3 亚型:对齐后的异常 spot 的嵌入与重建残差 x − x̂ 融合,用 DEC 迭代聚类,得到共享或样本特有的亚型。
  8. 评测:accuracy、F1、PR 曲线、新指标 SGD(比较预测与真值异常 spot 的 kNN 图结构);对齐用 iLISI、BatchKL、ASW、ARI;亚型用 Macro-F1、NMI、multi-SGD。
  1. Preprocess: 3,000 spatially variable genes (SpatialDE); a 112×112-px H&E patch per spot embedded by ResNet-34; a k=6 spatial neighbour graph.
  2. C1 (GAN module I): GATv2 encoders for expression and image features, transformer fusion to 512-d, a memory bank against mode collapse, decoders for expression and patch; trained on normal reference only with L1 reconstruction + adversarial (gradient-penalty) loss.
  3. Score = cosine distance between real and reconstructed spot in the discriminator's 16-d output; a two-component GMM with priors (MAP-EM) sets the threshold.
  4. No normal ST: train on normal scRNA-seq with an MLP encoder and score spots as cells.
  5. C2 alignment: module II learns a non-negative matrix M giving each target spot a kin reference spot; module III subtracts a per-batch style vector and decodes to the kin spot, mapping targets into reference space.
  6. C3 subtyping: fuse embedding and residual x − x̂ of aligned anomalies, cluster with DEC.
  7. Evaluate with accuracy, F1, PR, the new SGD metric; iLISI, BatchKL, ASW, ARI for alignment; Macro-F1, NMI, multi-SGD for subtypes.

术语Terms

空间转录组 spatial transcriptomics (ST)
在组织切片上逐点测全基因表达并保留坐标的技术。
spot
ST 的一个测量点;10x Visium 一个 spot 覆盖若干细胞。
10x Visium / Slide-seqV2 / Stereo-seq
三种 ST 平台,分辨率从多细胞 spot 到接近单细胞不等。
H&E
苏木精-伊红染色的常规病理切片图像。
异常组织域 ATD
与正常参考相比发生改变的连片组织区域,例如癌区。
原位癌 CIS / 浸润性癌 IC
尚局限在导管内的癌 / 已突破基底膜向周围浸润的癌。
批次效应 batch effect
不同切片、不同实验带来的非生物学差异。
三级淋巴结构 TLS
在慢性炎症或肿瘤中新形成的类淋巴结免疫细胞聚集。
spot
One ST measurement location; a 10x Visium spot covers several cells.
ATD
Anomalous tissue domain: a contiguous region changed relative to normal reference, e.g. tumour.
TLS
Tertiary lymphoid structure: lymph-node-like immune aggregates formed in chronic inflammation or tumours.

数据Data

26 组实验(Table 1),覆盖 10x Visium、Slide-seqV2、Stereo-seq,人和小鼠。参考:健康人乳腺 Visium、健康肝 Visium、健康胰腺导管 scRNA-seq 等;目标:乳腺癌、胰腺导管腺癌、原发性硬化性胆管炎、肾细胞癌(GSE175540,异常为 5 种 TLS 相关细胞类型)以及小鼠胚胎。真值是原数据集的组织域注释,部分实验中某个域被人为指定为异常。

26 experiments (Table 1) on 10x Visium, Slide-seqV2 and Stereo-seq, human and mouse. References include healthy breast and liver Visium and healthy pancreatic-duct scRNA-seq; targets include breast cancer, pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma, primary sclerosing cholangitis, renal cell carcinoma (GSE175540; anomalies are five TLS cell types) and mouse embryo. Ground truth is the original domain annotation; some domains are curated as anomalous.

主要结果Key results

单样本乳腺癌检测:SGD (0.78, 0.21),其余 5 个方法均 ≥ (1.31, 0.95);accuracy 与 F1 最高Fig. 2b
被误报的癌旁 spot 在 H&E 上看似正常,但 ACTB、TMSB10 表达介于正常(n=238)与已知癌(n=160)之间,作者解读为早期癌Fig. 2c–e
跨模态(scRNA-seq 参考 → 胰腺癌 Visium):accuracy、F1 最高,SGD 最低 (0.97, 0.53)Fig. 2f–g
两张乳腺癌切片联合检测:SGD (1.24, 1.04),次优 Spatial-ID (1.57, 1.49)Fig. 3b
乳腺癌纵向切片对齐:ASW_type 0.52 vs Harmony 0.50;联合聚类 ARI 0.24 vs 0.23Supp. Fig. 7(正文)
参考中结缔组织 spot 减 1/3、2/3:目标结缔组织假阳性 14 → 20 → 35Fig. 9c
从参考中去掉乳腺腺体:该类假阳性约增 3.30 倍(去掉免疫浸润约 2.94 倍)Fig. 9f
去掉 memory bank / 组织学 / TF 块或改用 MNN 对齐,均使指标下降Supp. Fig. 16
Single breast-cancer section: SGD (0.78, 0.21) vs ≥ (1.31, 0.95) for five baselines; best accuracy and F1Fig. 2b
Flagged peritumoural spots look normal on H&E but have ACTB/TMSB10 levels between normal (n=238) and known cancer (n=160)Fig. 2c–e
Cross-modal (scRNA-seq reference → pancreatic-cancer Visium): best accuracy and F1, lowest SGD (0.97, 0.53)Fig. 2f–g
Two breast-cancer sections jointly: SGD (1.24, 1.04) vs next-best Spatial-ID (1.57, 1.49)Fig. 3b
Vertical breast slices: ASW_type 0.52 vs Harmony 0.50; joint-clustering ARI 0.24 vs 0.23Supp. Fig. 7 (main text)
Removing 1/3 and 2/3 of reference connective-tissue spots: target false positives 14 → 20 → 35Fig. 9c
Removing breast glands from the reference: ~3.30-fold more false positives in that type (immune infiltrates ~2.94-fold)Fig. 9f

结论Conclusions

STANDS 在检测、对齐、亚型三项任务和多种平台上都优于组合基线;正常参考可以用 scRNA-seq 代替;异常分数还能提示 H&E 上尚看不出的早期病变。

STANDS beats composite baselines on detection, alignment and subtyping across platforms; scRNA-seq can replace a normal ST reference; scores can hint at early lesions not visible on H&E.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:GAN 可换成扩散模型以更准地重建正常数据、减少假阳性。
  • 作者实验:对参考集规模和多样性敏感,参考里缺的正常组织类型会被误报。
  • 我们的理解:很多评测异常是把某个已知域从参考里剔除后人为指定的,更接近新类别检测,对渐进性疾病变化未直接检验。
  • 我们的理解:accuracy / F1 依赖阈值,AUC 只在 Fig. 9 出现;3 个 GAN + DEC,调参成本高。
  • Authors: a diffusion model could replace the GAN for more accurate normal reconstruction.
  • Authors' experiments: sensitive to reference size and diversity.
  • Our reading: many anomalies are held-out domain labels (novel-class detection); graded disease change is not tested; three GANs plus DEC are costly to tune.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

对应 detect 一环,是空间组学上"正常参考 → 重建 → 异常分数"的代表方法。我们的理解:可作为肾脏组学异常检测基准的重建类基线,而且论文本身含肾细胞癌 Visium 数据(GSE175540)可先复现;Fig. 9 提醒基准必须报告参考集覆盖了哪些正常组织类型;"假阳性 = 早期病变"说明标记需要专家复核。重建残差能指出偏离的基因,但不是因果扰动预测,revert 还要另接扰动模型。

Covers the detect step with the reconstruction recipe on spatial omics. Our reading: a reconstruction baseline for the kidney-omics benchmark, with a renal-cell-carcinoma Visium set (GSE175540) in the paper to reproduce first; Fig. 9 says a benchmark must report which normal tissue types the reference covers; 'false positive = early lesion' argues for expert review of flags. Residuals point to deviating genes but are not causal perturbation predictions, so revert needs a separate perturbation model.
