Agent 与证据Agents & evidence

25

Empowering biomedical evidence exploration and synthesis with deep knowledge graph research

Wang Z, Chen Z, Yang Z, et al. Empowering biomedical evidence exploration and synthesis with deep knowledge graph research. Nat Mach Intell. 2026;8:1142–1156.

doi:10.1038/s42256-026-01266-0 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
PDF 全文(pdftotext):正文 + Methods;补充材料未读
full PDF via pdftotext: main text + Methods; supplement not read
DeepEvidence 是生物医学 deep research agent:orchestrator 调度广度优先(BFRS)和深度优先(DFRS)两类子 agent,写代码查询 17 个知识库与文献,边查边构建带出处的 evidence graph;所有 agent 统一用 GPT-5 时,它在公开基准和新建任务上都超过 Biomni 与 ToolUniverse。
DeepEvidence is a biomedical deep-research agent: an orchestrator dispatches breadth-first (BFRS) and depth-first (DFRS) subagents that write code against 17 knowledge bases and the literature while building an evidence graph with provenance; with every agent on GPT-5 it beats Biomni and ToolUniverse on open benchmarks and new tasks.

问题Problem

生物医学证据分散在结构不同、持续变化、彼此难以对齐的文献、数据库、本体和知识图谱里。通用 deep research 系统主要处理网页文本,不适合这种场景。作者观察到 Biomni、ToolUniverse 这类生物医学 agent 虽然接了很多 API,却只查几次就回答,工具太多还会选错工具。

Biomedical evidence is spread across literature, databases, ontologies and knowledge graphs that differ in structure, change constantly and rarely align. Generic deep-research systems work over web text and fit poorly. Biomedical agents such as Biomni and ToolUniverse have many APIs but search only a few times before answering and get confused by large tool suites.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 输入一个研究问题;orchestrator(LangGraph 状态机)先写研究计划,决定 BFRS 与 DFRS 的先后与组合,并跟踪搜索预算。
  2. orchestrator 通过 tool call 派发子 agent,参数为检索目标(实体名 + PubTator ID 或 UMLS CUI 等标准 ID)、要查的知识库和 action 预算。
  3. BFRS 子 agent 做多图查询,快速扫一跳邻域(如找与某疾病相关的全部药物);DFRS 子 agent 从关键实体出发多跳深挖(如沿参考文献递归追溯)。
  4. 子 agent 的动作是写 Python 代码,在沙箱中并行调用 17 个知识库的统一检索接口(返回 DataFrame + 文本摘要);报错回传给 agent 自我修正,沙箱限制步数。
  5. 发现被写入 evidence graph:只存规范化实体(基因、疾病、药物、通路、论文、发现),每个节点和关系带出处;添加前先查重,冲突证据分开记录。
  6. orchestrator 读取 evidence graph,迭代下一轮检索,最后汇总给出答案。
  7. 评测:4 个公开基准 + 药物发现、临床前、临床试验、循证医学任务;多数为选择题准确率,两项开放题由专家按量表打分,另有消融和对 evidence graph 的人工审计。
  1. The orchestrator (a LangGraph state machine) plans the research, choosing the order of BFRS and DFRS and tracking search budgets.
  2. It dispatches subagents by tool call with a target (entity names plus standard IDs such as PubTator IDs or UMLS CUIs), knowledge bases and an action budget.
  3. BFRS sweeps first-hop neighbourhoods across graphs; DFRS goes multihop from key entities, e.g. recursively following references.
  4. Actions are Python code run in a sandbox calling unified search tools over 17 knowledge bases; errors return to the agent for self-correction.
  5. Findings go into an evidence graph of normalized entities with per-item provenance; duplicates are merged and conflicting evidence kept as separate links.
  6. The orchestrator reads the graph, iterates, and finally aggregates an answer; evaluation uses MCQ accuracy, expert rubrics, an ablation and a manual graph audit.

术语Terms

知识图谱 knowledge graph
以实体为节点、关系为边的结构化知识库,如 KEGG 中基因与通路的关系。
KEGG
收录通路、疾病、药物的数据库;本文多个任务的真值由其通路拓扑规则生成。
UMLS CUI
美国国立医学图书馆统一医学术语系统中的概念编号,用来把不同写法的同一概念对齐。
RCT 随机对照试验
把受试者随机分组比较治疗效果的临床试验,是循证医学的高等级证据。
系统综述 / Cochrane
按预设标准系统检索并汇总所有相关研究的综述;Cochrane 是最权威的系统综述库,会定期更新版本。
替代终点 surrogate endpoint
比真正临床结局更早可测的指标(如生物标志物),用来提前判断疗效。
DLT 剂量限制性毒性
早期试验中决定能否继续加剂量的严重不良反应。
可成药性 druggability
一个蛋白靶点能否被药物分子有效结合与调节。
UMLS CUI
Concept ID in the US National Library of Medicine's unified medical vocabulary, used to align synonyms.
Systematic review / Cochrane
A review that searches and pools all eligible studies by fixed criteria; Cochrane reviews are versioned and updated.
Surrogate endpoint
An earlier-measurable marker used in place of the true clinical outcome.

数据Data

四个公开基准经筛选改造:HLE-Medicine 30 题(去掉需要图像的题)、LabBench-LitQA2 184 题、SuperGPQA 困难临床医学 172 题、TrialPanorama-EvidenceQA 最新 50 题(删去题中给出的摘要)。新建任务多为 n = 14–25 的选择题,真值多由 KEGG 通路拓扑、TrialPanorama 或 Cochrane 综述版本差分自动生成;另有 30 道证据冲突的靶点验证题和两项专家打分开放题。测试集公开在 HuggingFace。

Four adapted open benchmarks: HLE-Medicine (30 questions, no images), LabBench-LitQA2 (184), SuperGPQA hard clinical medicine (172), TrialPanorama-EvidenceQA (latest 50, provided abstracts removed). New tasks are mostly MCQs with n = 14–25 whose truth is generated from KEGG pathway topology, TrialPanorama or Cochrane version diffs, plus 30 conflicting-evidence target-validation questions and two expert-rated open tasks. Test sets are on HuggingFace.

主要结果Key results

HLE-Medicine 40.0%,Biomni 20.0%,GPT-5 3.3%;ToolUniverse 与 Sonnet-4.5 的数字正文(10.0% / 3.3%)与图(6.7% / 10.0%)不一致Results;Fig. 1e
LabBench-LitQA2 64.7%,Biomni 48.9%,带检索的 GPT-5 50.0%;TrialPanorama-EvidenceQA 96.0%,Sonnet-4.5 88.0%,Biomni 84.0%Fig. 1e
靶点识别 68%(Biomni 56%);MOA 推理 72%(Biomni 44%);体内代谢通量 80%(Biomni 68%)Fig. 2b;Fig. 3b,d
样本量估计 68%,Biomni 20%,ToolUniverse 32%;给药方案 52%,Biomni 36%Fig. 4c,e
EBM 证据缺口:gap detection 90.0%(Biomni 50.0%),recall@30 44.14%(Biomni 30.17%)Fig. 5d
消融:ReAct+MCP gap detection 25.0%,加代码执行 80.0%,朴素多 agent 70.0%,BFRS+DFRS 完整版 89.5%Fig. 5f
Evidence graph 审计(10 张图、316 项):provenance 100%,规范化 99.7%,claim alignment 93.3%(节点 89.2%)Fig. 5g
专家量表总分:靶点优先级 1.40 vs Biomni 1.15(P 小于 0.001);转化验证 1.64 vs 1.46(P 小于 0.05)Fig. 2c;Fig. 3f
HLE-Medicine 40.0% vs Biomni 20.0%, GPT-5 3.3%; ToolUniverse and Sonnet-4.5 differ between text (10.0% / 3.3%) and figure (6.7% / 10.0%)Results; Fig. 1e
LabBench-LitQA2 64.7% vs Biomni 48.9% and search-equipped GPT-5 50.0%; TrialPanorama-EvidenceQA 96.0% vs Sonnet-4.5 88.0%, Biomni 84.0%Fig. 1e
Target identification 68% (Biomni 56%); MOA reasoning 72% (Biomni 44%); in vivo flux 80% (Biomni 68%)Fig. 2b; Fig. 3b,d
Sample size 68% vs Biomni 20%, ToolUniverse 32%; regimen design 52% vs Biomni 36%Fig. 4c,e
EBM evidence gap: detection 90.0% (Biomni 50.0%), recall@30 44.14% (Biomni 30.17%)Fig. 5d
Ablation gap detection: ReAct+MCP 25.0%, +code execution 80.0%, naive multi-agent 70.0%, full BFRS+DFRS 89.5%Fig. 5f
Evidence-graph audit (10 graphs, 316 items): provenance 100%, normalization 99.7%, claim alignment 93.3% (nodes 89.2%)Fig. 5g

结论Conclusions

作者认为基线并不缺工具,而是探索太浅、被大量工具搞混;evidence graph 引导下的 BFRS–DFRS 结构化探索带来更深、更可靠的证据综合,并让研究过程可追踪、可由专家验证。他们把 DeepEvidence 定位为通用 AI agent 的补充组件。

Baselines do not lack tools; they explore shallowly and are confused by large tool sets. Evidence-graph-guided BFRS–DFRS exploration gives deeper, more reliable evidence synthesis and a traceable, expert-checkable process. The authors see DeepEvidence as a complement to general-purpose agents.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:知识源覆盖不完整,基准只代表部分研究任务;依赖 KEGG、Gene Ontology 这类 schema 稳定的精选库,私有数据源需要 agent 自己造工具。
  • 作者:没有显式概率或不确定性机制来调和冲突证据,靠底座 LLM 权衡;只有两种策略;未达专家水平;只处理文本。
  • 作者:LLM 的不确定性和外部 API 的变化限制完全复现。
  • 我们的理解:样本量小(n = 14–30),很多差距落在宽 CI 内;HLE 正文与图数字不一致;任务数在摘要(7)与正文(6)中不一致。
  • 我们的理解:多个选择题的真值由 KEGG 拓扑规则生成,而 agent 本身能查 KEGG;专家量表只与 Biomni 比,正文未报告评审人数与一致性。
  • Authors: incomplete source coverage; reliance on well-curated knowledge bases; no explicit uncertainty mechanism for conflicting evidence; only two strategies; not expert-level; text-only.
  • Our reading: small n (14–30) and wide CIs; HLE text–figure mismatch; task count inconsistent (7 vs 6).
  • Our reading: several MCQ truths come from KEGG rules while the agent can query KEGG; expert rubrics compare only with Biomni.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是课题 2(agent 结论核查)和 Sikander 所说「把 Evidence 加进来」最直接的参照。它的 evidence graph 给出了「结论 → 文献/知识库证据」的数据结构,对图本身的审计(provenance、规范化、claim alignment)几乎就是课题 2 指标的原型;即便如此,节点级 claim alignment 也只有 89.2%,说明「有引用」不等于「被支撑」。它缺少的两样 —— 不确定性/置信度和「agent 自己跑出的数据」这一侧证据 —— 正是课题 2 要补的;课题 1 的肾脏组学已知答案可以给数据侧证据提供真值。在 detect → model → revert 中,它可用于给 virtual tissue 提名的回退基因/扰动收集证据并排序。

Our reading: the closest reference for Topic 2 (verifying agent claims) and for Sikander's "include Evidence". The evidence graph is a data structure for claim → literature evidence, and its audit (provenance, normalization, claim alignment) is nearly a template for Topic 2 metrics — yet node-level claim alignment is only 89.2%, so cited is not the same as supported. What it lacks — confidence and evidence from the agent's own computed data — is what Topic 2 adds, with Topic 1's kidney known answers as data-side ground truth. In detect → model → revert it could gather and rank evidence for perturbations the virtual tissue nominates.

↑ 回到目录back to index · deep-read.md

26

Large Language Models as Biomedical Hypothesis Generators: A Comprehensive Evaluation

Qi B, Zhang K, Tian K, et al. Large Language Models as Biomedical Hypothesis Generators: A Comprehensive Evaluation. COLM. 2024. arXiv:2407.08940.

https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08940 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
arXiv v2 PDF 全文(pdftotext),含附录表格
full arXiv v2 PDF via pdftotext, including appendix tables
作者从 PubMed 论文中抽取「背景 → 假设」配对,按发表时间切分以保证测试数据未被模型训练见过,系统评测 2023 年代 LLM 在 zero-shot、few-shot、微调、工具调用和多智能体设置下提出生物医学假设的能力,用新颖性、相关性、重要性、可验证性四维打分。
The authors extract background → hypothesis pairs from PubMed, split them by publication date so test data is unseen in training, and evaluate 2023-era LLMs at biomedical hypothesis generation zero-shot, few-shot, fine-tuned, with tools and as multi-agent systems, scoring novelty, relevance, significance and verifiability.

问题Problem

LLM 的训练数据无法追溯,以往工作无法保证测试数据没被模型见过,因此分不清模型是在「发现」还是在「背诵」。假设也没有唯一正确答案,词重叠指标不够。作者想知道 LLM 能否真正 zero-shot 提出假设,以及 few-shot、领域适配、微调、工具和多智能体各有什么影响。

LLM training data cannot be traced, so prior work cannot guarantee unseen test data and cannot separate discovery from recall. Hypotheses have no single right answer, so word overlap is not enough. The paper asks whether LLMs truly generate hypotheses zero-shot and how few-shot examples, domain adaptation, fine-tuning, tools and multi-agent setups affect this.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 按主题组织 PubMed 论文,用 ChatGPT 和 GPT-4 总结文献并生成「背景知识–假设」配对,过滤低质量数据(仿 Self-Instruct 流程)。
  2. 按发表时间切分:2023 年 1 月前的 2,700 对作训练(2,500)和 seen 测试(200);2023 年 8 月论文的 200 对作 unseen 测试。
  3. 只评测训练语料早于 2023 年 3 月的模型:gpt-3.5-turbo、Vicuna、Llama-2-70B-chat、WizardLM、Openchat,以及医学模型 PMC-LLaMA、MedAlpaca。
  4. 三种提示:zero-shot、随机 5-shot、相似度检索 5-shot*;另在训练对上微调 WizardLM-13B(3 epoch,学习率 3e-5)。
  5. 评价:BLEU/ROUGE 对比参考假设;LLM 评审给新颖性、相关性、重要性、可验证性各打 0–3 分;3 位专家对 100 个 unseen 样本打分,检验与 LLM 评审的相关性。
  6. 用 SelfBLEU(多次生成之间的相似度)作为「不确定性」代理,分析它与新颖性、可验证性的关系。
  7. 多智能体:Analyst 抽关键词 → Engineer 检索 PubMed(过滤 2023 年后的文献)→ Scientist 提出假设 → Critic 评估反馈;对比 ReAct 与 OpenAI function calling 两种工具调用。
  1. Build background–hypothesis pairs from PubMed papers with ChatGPT and GPT-4, filter low quality.
  2. Split by date: 2,700 pre-Jan-2023 pairs (2,500 train, 200 seen test); 200 pairs from Aug-2023 papers as the unseen test.
  3. Evaluate only models trained before March 2023 (gpt-3.5-turbo, Llama-2, WizardLM, Vicuna, Openchat, PMC-LLaMA, MedAlpaca) with 0-shot, 5-shot and retrieved 5-shot* prompts; fine-tune WizardLM-13B.
  4. Score with BLEU/ROUGE, an LLM judge on four 0–3 dimensions, and three experts on 100 unseen examples; use SelfBLEU as an uncertainty proxy.
  5. Test an Analyst → Engineer (time-filtered PubMed) → Scientist → Critic multi-agent loop and ReAct vs function-calling tool use.

术语Terms

PubMed
美国国立医学图书馆的生物医学文献检索库。
假设 hypothesis
这里指从背景知识推出、尚待实验检验的科学猜想,例如「某药物与某癌症风险降低相关」。
数据污染 data contamination
测试数据已出现在模型训练语料中,使评测高估模型的真实能力。
领域适配 domain adaptation
在医学语料上继续训练通用模型,如 PMC-LLaMA、MedAlpaca。
PubMed
美国国立医学图书馆的生物医学文献检索库。
假设 hypothesis
这里指从背景知识推出、尚待实验检验的科学猜想,例如「某药物与某癌症风险降低相关」。
数据污染 data contamination
测试数据已出现在模型训练语料中,使评测高估模型的真实能力。
领域适配 domain adaptation
在医学语料上继续训练通用模型,如 PMC-LLaMA、MedAlpaca。

数据Data

约 10,000 篇 PubMed 医学论文;seen 集 2,700 对(训练 2,500 + seen 测试 200,均为 2023 年 1 月前),unseen 集 200 对(2023 年 8 月论文)。附录分别报告 GPT-3.5 和 GPT-4 构建的两套数据结果。人类评估为 3 位生物医学专家、100 个 unseen 样本。

About 10,000 PubMed medical papers; seen set 2,700 pairs (2,500 train + 200 seen test, all pre-Jan-2023); unseen set 200 pairs from Aug-2023 papers. The appendix reports separate GPT-3.5- and GPT-4-built datasets. Human evaluation: three biomedical experts, 100 unseen examples.

主要结果Key results

zero-shot 更新颖、few-shot 更可验证:gpt-3.5-turbo 新颖性 0-shot 1.42 → 5-shot* 1.02,可验证性 1.97 → 2.36Fig. 4;附录 Table 2
unseen 集上 BLEU 往往更高,例 gpt-3.5-turbo 0-shot seen 13.93、unseen 15.52Fig. 3;附录 Table 2
LLM 评审与人评相关:Pearson 0.73–0.97,Spearman 0.73–0.94;人评最高 Llama-2-70b-chat 5-shot 2.33Table 1
BLEU 与人评相关性仅 −0.58 到 0.32附录 Table 5
微调后 WizardLM-13B 人评新颖性仅 0.85、重要性 1.23Table 1
ChatGPT 单模型平均 1.92;加工具 ReAct 1.56、function calling 1.49;多智能体 2.09;多智能体 + 工具 2.07附录 Table 6
Zero-shot more novel, few-shot more verifiable: gpt-3.5-turbo novelty 1.42 → 1.02 (5-shot*), verifiability 1.97 → 2.36Fig. 4; Appendix Table 2
BLEU often higher on unseen, e.g. gpt-3.5-turbo 0-shot 13.93 seen vs 15.52 unseenFig. 3; Appendix Table 2
LLM judge vs humans: Pearson 0.73–0.97, Spearman 0.73–0.94; best human average Llama-2-70b-chat 5-shot 2.33Table 1
BLEU–human correlation only −0.58 to 0.32Appendix Table 5
ChatGPT 1.92; with tools ReAct 1.56, function calling 1.49; multi-agent 2.09; multi-agent + tools 2.07Appendix Table 6

结论Conclusions

作者认为 LLM 能在训练未见的文献上提出新颖且被验证的假设;通过多智能体和工具增加不确定性有助于产生多样的候选、提升 zero-shot 表现;但 few-shot 和工具引入的外部知识不一定带来提升,需要谨慎选择知识的类型与范围。

LLMs can propose novel, validated hypotheses on unseen literature; raising uncertainty through multi-agent interaction and tools helps diverse candidate generation and zero-shot performance; but external knowledge from few-shot examples and tools does not always help.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:背景–假设对由 ChatGPT/GPT-4 自动抽取,准确性和偏差需要人工标注子集来评估。
  • 作者:幻觉对假设的影响未研究;只用了 Web 搜索和 PubMed,生物信息工具和知识图谱留作未来工作;人类评估只做了一部分。
  • 我们的理解:「可验证性」只是评审打分,不是实验或文献核对;「后来被证实」只有 Fig. 1 一个个例。
  • 我们的理解:正文说 ReAct 略差于 function calling、工具能进一步提升多智能体,但附录 Table 6 的平均分显示相反(1.56 vs 1.49;2.09 → 2.07);正文称 5% 抽样,但 100 例是 unseen 集的一半。
  • 我们的理解:评审与数据抽取同属 ChatGPT/GPT-4,可能有同源偏差;评审身份正文写 GPT-4、附录写 ChatGPT;模型均为 2023 年代。
  • Authors: automatic extraction by ChatGPT/GPT-4 needs manual checking; hallucination effects not studied; only web search and PubMed; partial human evaluation.
  • Our reading: verifiability is a rating, not a fact check; the text's tool/multi-agent claims do not match Appendix Table 6; the "5% sampling" does not match 100 of 200 examples.
  • Our reading: judge and extractor come from the same model family; the text says GPT-4 judge, the appendix says ChatGPT.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:对课题 2(agent 结论核查),这篇提供两样可直接借用的东西 —— 按发表时间切分 + 检索端时间过滤来区分「发现」与「复述」,以及 LLM 评审对人类专家的一致性检验。但它的「可验证性」只是 0–3 分的评审意见,课题 2 要做的是把结论真正追溯到 agent 自己的代码、数据或引用文献。新颖性与可验证性此消彼长也提醒我们:核查器要区分「无证据」与「新但可检验」。在 detect → model → revert 中,它对应把回退候选写成可检验假设并排序的环节,但不接触任何组学数据。

Our reading: for Topic 2, two reusable pieces — date-based splits with time-filtered retrieval to separate discovery from restatement, and an LLM-judge vs expert agreement check. But its verifiability is a 0–3 opinion, while Topic 2 traces claims to the agent's own code, data or cited papers; the novelty–verifiability trade-off warns a verifier to separate unsupported from new-but-testable. In detect → model → revert it matches writing revert candidates as ranked testable hypotheses, without any omics data.

Biomni: A General-Purpose Biomedical AI Agent

Huang K, Zhang S, Wang H, et al. Biomni: A General-Purpose Biomedical AI Agent. bioRxiv. 2025. doi:10.1101/2025.05.30.656746.

doi:10.1101/2025.05.30.656746 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
bioRxiv v1 PDF 全文(pdftotext,CC-BY 4.0);补充材料未读
full bioRxiv v1 PDF via pdftotext (CC-BY 4.0); supplement not read
Biomni 是通用生物医学 agent:先从 2,500 篇 bioRxiv 论文中自动挖出任务、软件和数据库,人工实现成统一环境 Biomni-E1(150 个工具、105 个软件包、59 个数据库);再由基于 CodeAct 的 Biomni-A1 检索相关资源、写计划、写代码执行并迭代,零样本完成跨领域任务。
Biomni is a general biomedical agent: tasks, software and databases mined from 2,500 bioRxiv papers are implemented into the environment Biomni-E1 (150 tools, 105 packages, 59 databases), and the CodeAct agent Biomni-A1 retrieves resources, plans, writes and runs code, and iterates to solve cross-domain tasks zero-shot.

问题Problem

已有生物医学 agent 多为窄任务定制的 workflow,难以跨子领域泛化。通用 agent 需要一个显式定义生物医学动作空间(工具、数据库、软件、实验 protocol)的环境,以及能在其中自主组合动作、不依赖预设流程的架构。

Existing biomedical agents are narrow workflows that generalise poorly. A generalist needs an environment that explicitly defines the biomedical action space and an architecture that composes actions in it without fixed templates.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 取 bioRxiv 25 个学科各最新 100 篇论文,LLM 分块阅读 PDF,抽取任务、软件、数据库三类要素。
  2. 按与药物发现和临床的相关性筛选,收敛到约 1,900 个常见任务;人工去重,剔除几行代码就能做的琐碎任务。
  3. 人类科学家与带网页搜索的软件工程 agent 合作实现工具,每个工具需有通过的测试用例和 LLM 可读的输出;大型数据库每个封装成一个自然语言查询函数,无 API 的库预处理成 pandas 表放进本地 data lake。
  4. 收到用户问题后,另一个 LLM 从动作空间中检索相关的工具、数据和软件子集,避免上下文过长。
  5. Biomni-A1(CodeAct)写出编号计划,逐步生成 Python/R/Bash 代码执行,读取输出后修改计划,直到给出答案。
  6. 评测:LAB-Bench 与 HLE 问答;8 个新任务(与无工具 Claude Sonnet 3.7、ReAct+Code、自身 ReAct 版本比较);可穿戴、单细胞 multiome、克隆三个案例,其中一个克隆 protocol 由科学家原样执行并测序验证。
  1. An LLM reads the 100 latest papers in each of 25 bioRxiv fields and extracts tasks, software and databases; ~1,900 recurring tasks are filtered manually.
  2. Humans plus software-engineering agents implement and test 150 tools; large databases get one natural-language query function each; API-less ones go into a local data lake.
  3. For each query a separate LLM retrieves the relevant tools, data and software.
  4. Biomni-A1 (CodeAct) writes a numbered plan, then generates and runs Python/R/Bash code, revising the plan from outputs until it answers.
  5. Evaluation: LAB-Bench and HLE, 8 new tasks vs tool-less Claude Sonnet 3.7, ReAct+Code and Biomni-ReAct, and three case studies incl. one wet-lab cloning run.

术语Terms

CRISPR 筛选 screen
用 CRISPR 批量敲除或激活基因,测量每个基因扰动对表型的影响。
sgRNA
引导 Cas9 切割特定 DNA 位置的短 RNA;克隆进质粒后用于敲除目标基因(本文为 B2M)。
snRNA-seq / snATAC-seq
单细胞核转录组测序 / 单细胞核染色质可及性测序;二者合称 multiome。
GRN 基因调控网络
转录因子与其靶基因之间的调控关系网络;regulon 指一个转录因子及其靶基因集合。
GWAS
全基因组关联研究,找出与性状相关的基因组位点;一个位点里通常有多个候选基因。
CGM
连续血糖监测仪;本文所用传感器同时记录皮肤温度。
药物重定位 drug repurposing
把已有药物用于新的适应症。
snRNA-seq / snATAC-seq
Single-nucleus transcriptome / chromatin-accessibility sequencing (together: multiome).
GRN / regulon
Gene regulatory network; a regulon is one transcription factor plus its target genes.
sgRNA
Short guide RNA that directs Cas9 to cut a chosen DNA site.

数据Data

LAB-Bench DbQA 与 SeqQA 的 12.5% 子集(45 题开发 + 315 题测试,3 次运行平均)和 HLE 52 题。8 个新任务来自 Open Targets Genetics、Shringarpure et al.、Schmidt et al.(18,939 个候选基因)、CellxGene 等(每个数据集 20k–50k 细胞)、5 个微生物组数据集、Huang et al.、MyGene2 / Alsentzer et al.。案例:30 人可穿戴数据(458 个文件,Methods 写 485)、约 336,000 个细胞核的人胚胎骨骼 multiome。

A 12.5% subset of LAB-Bench DbQA and SeqQA (45 dev + 315 test, 3 runs) and 52 HLE questions. Eight new tasks from Open Targets Genetics, Shringarpure et al., Schmidt et al. (18,939 candidate genes), CellxGene and others (20k–50k cells each), five microbiome datasets, Huang et al. and MyGene2 / Alsentzer et al. Cases: wearables from 30 people (458 files; Methods says 485) and a ~336,000-nucleus embryonic skeleton multiome.

主要结果Key results

LAB-Bench DbQA 74.4%(人类专家 74.7%,ReAct+Code 40.8%);SeqQA 81.9%(人类 78.8%)Results;Fig. 2a
HLE 子集 17.3%,base LLM 6.0%,coding agent 12.8%,literature agent 12.2%Results
8 个新任务平均相对提升:比 base LLM 402.3%,比 ReAct+Code 43.0%,比 Biomni-ReAct 20.4%Results;Fig. 2b
每个任务平均 6–24 步,用 0–4 个专用工具、1–8 个软件包、0–3 个 data lake 条目Results
可穿戴:餐后体温平均升高 2.19 °C,中位 1.10 °C,个体范围 −0.11 到 15.56 °CResults;Fig. 3d
Multiome GRN:约 5 小时、566–589 个 regulon,复现 RUNX2 相关已知调控,提出 AUTS2、ZFHX3、PBX1Results;Fig. 3f–h
克隆湿实验:B2M sgRNA 克隆进 lentiCRISPR v2 Blast,挑 2 个菌落测序,均完全比对Results;Fig. 4e
LAB-Bench DbQA 74.4% (human experts 74.7%, ReAct+Code 40.8%); SeqQA 81.9% (humans 78.8%)Results; Fig. 2a
HLE subset 17.3% vs base LLM 6.0%, coding agent 12.8%, literature agent 12.2%Results
8 new tasks: average relative gain 402.3% over base LLM, 43.0% over ReAct+Code, 20.4% over Biomni-ReActResults; Fig. 2b
Wearables: mean post-meal temperature rise 2.19 °C, median 1.10 °C, range −0.11 to 15.56 °CResults; Fig. 3d
Multiome GRN in ~5 h with 566–589 regulons; recovers known RUNX2 regulation; proposes AUTS2, ZFHX3, PBX1Results; Fig. 3f–h
Wet lab: B2M sgRNA cloned into lentiCRISPR v2 Blast; two sequenced colonies aligned perfectlyResults; Fig. 4e

结论Conclusions

作者认为 Biomni 在多个子领域上零样本泛化良好,在数据库查询、序列分析和分子克隆上接近人类水平,可以把研究者从繁琐流程中解放出来;未来方向包括用强化学习训练、整合多模态输入、让 agent 自主发现新工具。

Biomni generalises zero-shot across subfields and approaches human level on database queries, sequence analysis and cloning, freeing researchers from tedious workflows; future work includes RL training, multimodal inputs and self-discovery of new tools.

局限Limitations

  • 作者:评测任务只覆盖领域一部分;动作发现只用最新文献,可能漏掉经典方法;在细致临床判断、新实验推理和深度综合上仍有困难。
  • 我们的理解:LAB-Bench 只用 12.5% 子集;8 个任务在正文只给平均相对提升,base LLM 接近 0 时(Fig. 2b 有 +inf)会夸大平均值。
  • 我们的理解:结论只由作者读 trace 和代码人工核对,没有置信度和重跑一致性;PBX1 被同时说成「新」和「已确立」。
  • 我们的理解:15.56 °C 的个体餐后升温在生理上不太可能,更像数据伪影,文中未讨论;传感器测的是皮肤温度,Fig. 3d 写成核心体温。
  • 数字前后不一:克隆基准 10 题 vs 20 题;可穿戴文件 458 vs 485;摘要称「数万篇论文」而正文为 2,500 篇。
  • Authors: tasks cover part of the field; recent-literature bias in action discovery; weak on nuanced clinical judgement and deep synthesis.
  • Our reading: 12.5% LAB-Bench subset; average relative gains inflated by near-zero baselines (+inf in Fig. 2b); conclusions checked only by humans reading traces, no confidence or rerun stability.
  • Our reading: PBX1 called both novel and established; a 15.56 °C rise looks like an artefact; skin vs core temperature; 10 vs 20 cloning tasks; 458 vs 485 files.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:Biomni 是课题 2(agent 结论核查)要核查的典型对象 —— 它自主写代码分析 snRNA/snATAC 这类数据并报告「新调控因子」,但只有人工读 trace 的核对。它自己的报告里已有可供核查器练手的例子(PBX1 新旧矛盾、15.56 °C 升温、皮肤温度写成核心体温)。可以把 multiome GRN 案例移植到肾脏数据,用课题 1 的已知答案(疾病细胞 vs doublet / 低质量细胞)检查它是否把伪影说成生物学。后续评测中它的 label collapse(心脏 MERFISH 准确率 0.394,偏向内皮细胞)也提示异常检测中少见病变细胞可能被归入常见类别。

Our reading: Biomni is the archetypal system Topic 2 would verify — it writes code over snRNA/snATAC-type data and reports novel regulators, checked only by humans reading traces. Its own report already contains claims a verifier should flag (PBX1 novel vs known, 15.56 °C, skin vs core temperature). The multiome GRN case can be ported to kidney data, using Topic 1's known answers to test whether it reports artefacts as biology; its later label collapse (0.394 accuracy on heart MERFISH, skewed to endothelial) warns that rare lesion states may be folded into common classes.

The Virtual Biotech: A Multi-Agent AI Framework for Therapeutic Discovery and Development

Zhang HG, Eckmann P, Miao J, Mahon AB, Zou J. The Virtual Biotech: A Multi-Agent AI Framework for Therapeutic Discovery and Development. bioRxiv. 2026. doi:10.64898/2026.02.23.707551.

doi:10.64898/2026.02.23.707551 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

bioRxiv v1 全文网页(…707551v1.full,约 20 万字符,分段抓取),含 Results、Extended Methods、图注、Data availability;非逐字 PDF 提取,附件 PDF(Supplementary Notes/Tables)未读。CC-BY-ND。
bioRxiv v1 full-text web page (…707551v1.full, about 200k characters, fetched in segments) covering Results, Extended Methods, figure legends and Data availability; not a verbatim PDF extract, and the supplementary PDF (Notes and Tables) was not read. CC-BY-ND.
把一家生物技术公司的组织架构照搬成 LLM agent 团队:一个首席科学官(CSO)agent 接收科学问题、拆解并分派给四个部门下的八个专科 scientist agent,后者经 MCP server 调用 100 多个工具与公共数据库做真实计算,再由 CSO 汇总;作者用它做了三个转化医学案例。
A biotech company's org chart rebuilt as LLM agents: a Chief Scientific Officer agent decomposes a scientific question and delegates to eight specialist scientist agents in four divisions, which call 100-plus tools and public databases through MCP servers, with the CSO integrating the results across three translational case studies.

问题Problem

药物发现需要整合跨尺度、跨模态的证据——人群遗传学、功能基因组学、单细胞与空间图谱、通路与蛋白互作、化学信息学、临床数据——但数据、工具与专业知识分散在不同团队和机构之间,整合本身就是瓶颈。单个 LLM agent 面对这种任务会受上下文长度、工具数量与专业深度的限制。作者的问题是:能否用模仿人类研发组织的多 agent 分工做端到端的计算发现,同时保持人类科学家在环?

Drug discovery requires integrating evidence across biological scales and modalities, yet data, tools and expertise are fragmented across teams and organisations, making integration the bottleneck. A single LLM agent runs into limits of context length, tool count and domain depth. The authors ask whether a multi-agent division of labour mirroring a human therapeutics organisation can support end-to-end computational discovery while keeping humans in the loop.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 组织结构即架构:用户在 chat 界面向 CSO agent 提问;CSO 负责澄清意图、拆解问题、路由子任务、综合结果,自己不跑分析。CSO 办公室另有 Chief of Staff(生成领域简报)与 Scientific Reviewer(审查输出并可触发重新分派)。Introduction 自述「四个科研部门、十一个 agent、100 多个工具」。
  2. 八个专科 scientist agent 分在四个部门:Target Identification and Prioritization(统计遗传学、功能基因组学与扰动、单细胞图谱);Target Safety(通路与蛋白互作、FDA 安全官);Modality Selection(靶点生物学家、药理学家);Clinical Officers(临床试验专家)。
  3. 工具层:数据访问经 10 个 MCP server 中介,接到 Open Targets v25.09、CELLxGENE Census、Tabula Sapiens v2、Tahoe-100M、cBioPortal、ClinicalTrials.gov、PubMed、OpenFDA、ChEMBL;agent 实际运行 Scanpy、Harmony、PyDESeq2、LIANA、Cell2Location、PROGENy 等分析。
  4. 模型与实现:所有 agent 用 Claude Sonnet 4.5,Chief of Staff 与 Scientific Reviewer 用更便宜的 Claude Haiku 4.5;系统建在 Claude Agent SDK 上;模型知识截止 2025 年 1 月,据此挑选 readout 在截止之后的案例以降低预训练泄漏。
  5. 应用一(大规模并行标注):把 55,984 条可映射到靶点的临床试验各交给一个 clinical-trialist agent 实例,37,075 个实例并行约 6 小时完成(顺序执行估计 1,839 小时);每个实例把自由文本结果整理成结构化终点与不良事件率,并把靶点连到多组学注释,包括 agent 自行从单细胞图谱算出的细胞类型特异性特征。
  6. 验证与统计:人工复核 100 条随机试验(Phase II、III 各 50 条),另与 TDC 在 7,278 条上比对;统计端对 z-score 化的单细胞特征做 logistic 与 beta 回归,配 1,000 次置换检验、以药物类型与治疗领域为交叉随机截距的混合效应模型、以遗传证据为协变量的调整,以及在无遗传证据的 74.6% 子集中重复,FDR 用 Benjamini–Hochberg 控制在 5%。
  7. 应用二(B7-H3 肺癌靶点评估):一次端到端查询串起遗传学、单细胞差异表达与 LIANA 配体–受体、12 个 LUAD Visium 样本的 Cell2Location 解卷积与 kNN 免疫邻域分析、TCGA LUAD 566 例 Cox 生存分析,最后在 modality 侧判断小分子与 PROTAC 缺乏可成药口袋,建议走抗体偶联药物(ADC)。
  8. 应用三(失败试验复盘):针对 MOONGLOW(NCT06137183,vixarelimab 的 OSMRβ II 期,入组 79 人后因无效终止),整合遗传学支持、UC 图谱、五个生物制剂队列的基线表达与 TAURUS 纵向单细胞(PROGENy + decoupler + lme4),指出方案未做 OSMR 富集入组,建议生物标志物指导入组加预设 target-engagement 终点。
  1. The org chart is the architecture: a CSO agent clarifies intent, decomposes the query, routes sub-tasks and synthesises results without running analyses itself; its office adds a Chief of Staff for field briefings and a Scientific Reviewer that audits outputs and can force re-delegation. The Introduction states four divisions, eleven agents and over 100 tools.
  2. Eight scientist agents across four divisions: Target Identification and Prioritization (statistical genetics, functional genomics and perturbation, single-cell atlas); Target Safety (bio-pathways and protein interactions, FDA safety officer); Modality Selection (target biologist, pharmacologist); Clinical Officers (clinical trialist).
  3. Tool layer: data access is mediated through ten MCP servers onto Open Targets v25.09, CELLxGENE Census, Tabula Sapiens v2, Tahoe-100M, cBioPortal, ClinicalTrials.gov, PubMed, OpenFDA and ChEMBL, with the agents running Scanpy, Harmony, PyDESeq2, LIANA, Cell2Location and PROGENy.
  4. Models: all agents on Claude Sonnet 4.5 except the Chief of Staff and Scientific Reviewer on the cheaper Claude Haiku 4.5, built on the Claude Agent SDK; the January 2025 knowledge cutoff drives the choice of case studies whose readouts come later.
  5. Application 1: 55,984 target-mapped trials each go to their own clinical-trialist agent instance; 37,075 instances in parallel finish in about 6 hours versus an estimated 1,839 hours sequentially, converting free text into structured endpoints and adverse-event rates linked to multi-omic annotations including agent-derived cell-type-specificity features.
  6. Validation and statistics: 100 manually reviewed random trials (50 Phase II, 50 Phase III) and comparison with TDC on 7,278 trials; logistic and beta regressions on z-scored single-cell features with 1,000 permutations, mixed-effects models with crossed random intercepts, adjustment for genetic evidence, a no-genetic-evidence subset (74.6%) and 5% Benjamini-Hochberg FDR.
  7. Application 2: one end-to-end B7-H3 lung-cancer query chaining genetics, single-cell differential expression and LIANA, Cell2Location plus kNN immune-neighbourhood analysis of 12 LUAD Visium samples, and Cox survival on 566 TCGA LUAD patients, concluding that small molecules and PROTACs lack druggable pockets and proposing an antibody-drug conjugate.
  8. Application 3: a post-mortem of MOONGLOW (NCT06137183, the OSMR-beta vixarelimab Phase II terminated for futility after 79 participants), integrating genetic support, a UC atlas, baseline expression across five biologic cohorts and TAURUS longitudinal single cell (PROGENy, decoupler, lme4), then critiquing the absence of OSMR-based enrolment and proposing biomarker-guided enrolment with a prespecified target-engagement endpoint.

术语Terms

靶点 (target)
药物要结合并改变其功能的那个分子,通常是一个蛋白;「靶点发现」即决定去干预哪个分子。
MCP server
Model Context Protocol 服务端:把数据库或分析工具包装成 LLM agent 可以调用的标准接口。
GWAS / 统计遗传学证据
在大规模人群中寻找与疾病相关的基因位点;若某靶点有遗传学支持,其成药成功率通常更高。
Visium / 空间转录组
在组织切片上按空间位点(spot)测基因表达,因此能看出某个基因高表达的位置周围有哪些细胞。
Cell2Location
空间转录组的解卷积方法:每个 spot 混有多个细胞,它据单细胞参考估计各细胞类型的占比。
ADC / 抗体偶联药物
把细胞毒药物挂在抗体上,由抗体带到表达该靶点的细胞旁再释放药物。
PROTAC
一类小分子,把靶蛋白拉到细胞的降解机器旁使其被降解;需要蛋白上有可结合的口袋。
临床终点 / 不良事件率
试验预设用来判断成败的测量指标,以及治疗引起的副作用发生比例。
target
The molecule, usually a protein, that a drug binds and modulates; target discovery is deciding which molecule to intervene on.
MCP server
A Model Context Protocol server: a database or analysis tool wrapped in a standard interface an LLM agent can call.
Visium / spatial transcriptomics
Gene expression measured at spatial spots on a tissue section, so one can see which cells surround a spot high in a given gene.
Cell2Location
Deconvolution for spatial data: each spot mixes several cells, and the method estimates cell-type proportions against a single-cell reference.
ADC
Antibody-drug conjugate: a cytotoxic payload carried by an antibody to cells expressing the target.
clinical endpoint / adverse-event rate
The prespecified measurement that decides whether a trial succeeded, and the proportion of patients with treatment-caused side effects.

数据Data

临床试验层:ClinicalTrials.gov 上 55,984 条可映射到靶点的试验(Phase I 14,237、II 22,164、III 14,911、IV 4,672),经 Open Targets 映射,并与 TDC 在 7,278 条上交叉比对。组学层:Open Targets v25.09、CELLxGENE Census、Tabula Sapiens v2、Tahoe-100M 扰动转录组、cBioPortal/TCGA。案例数据:12 个 LUAD Visium 空间样本(Data availability 引 PubMed 38782901)、TCGA LUAD 566 例、五个 UC 生物制剂队列(GEO GSE12251、GSE16879、GSE92415、GSE206285、GSE73661)、TAURUS 纵向单细胞 22 位患者 485,868 个细胞(PubMed 39438660)。知识源:PubMed、OpenFDA、ChEMBL。

Trials: 55,984 target-mapped ClinicalTrials.gov trials (Phase I 14,237, II 22,164, III 14,911, IV 4,672) mapped via Open Targets and cross-checked against TDC on 7,278. Omics: Open Targets v25.09, CELLxGENE Census, Tabula Sapiens v2, Tahoe-100M, cBioPortal/TCGA. Case data: 12 LUAD Visium samples (PubMed 38782901), 566 TCGA LUAD patients, five UC biologic cohorts (GEO GSE12251, GSE16879, GSE92415, GSE206285, GSE73661) and TAURUS longitudinal single cell with 22 patients and 485,868 cells (PubMed 39438660). Knowledge: PubMed, OpenFDA, ChEMBL.

主要结果Key results

37,075 个 clinical-trialist agent 实例并行约 6 小时完成 55,984 条试验标注,顺序执行估计需 1,839 小时(约 184 倍)Results §Massively parallel extraction
抽取准确性:主要终点一致 89.7%(78/87)、次要终点 83.9%(73/87)、不良事件率 92.4%(61/66)Results §Massively parallel extraction
与 Therapeutics Data Commons 在 7,278 条试验上的一致率 85.3%Results §Massively parallel extraction
靶向细胞类型特异基因的药物:Phase I→II 推进概率高 40%、到 Phase IV 高 48%、不良事件率低 32%Abstract; Fig. 3A-B
同一段 Results 给出的 Phase I→II odds ratio 为 1.27(95% CI 1.22–1.33),这是 z-score 化连续特征的 OR,与摘要的 40% 不是同一量,论文未加解释Results; Fig. 3A
稳健性:1,000 次置换、混合效应模型、以遗传证据调整、以及在无遗传证据的 74.6% 子集中重复,结论保持Fig. S2-S4
B7-H3 在成纤维细胞中上调(SCLC log2FC 1.94、LUAD 1.46);12 个 LUAD Visium 样本中 B7-H3 高 spot 周围免疫细胞被耗竭Results §B7-H3; Fig. S5
TCGA LUAD 566 例中 B7-H3 高表达对应更差总生存 HR 1.62、疾病特异生存 HR 1.82;建议 ADC 路线,单次查询成本 $46.00Results §B7-H3
OSMR 遗传学支持靶点(rs395157,OR 1.06,p = 2.68 × 10⁻¹⁰;L2G 0.970),但所分析图谱中 UC 与健康组织 OSMR 无显著差异Results §OSMR
TAURUS 纵向数据(22 患者、485,868 细胞)中抗 TNF 后无应答者成纤维细胞 OSMR 与 JAK-STAT 活性升高;建议生物标志物指导入组,成本 $54.00Results §OSMR
37,075 parallel clinical-trialist agent instances annotate 55,984 trials in about 6 hours versus an estimated 1,839 hours sequentially, roughly 184-foldResults, Massively parallel extraction
Extraction accuracy: 89.7% (78/87) on primary endpoints, 83.9% (73/87) on secondary, 92.4% (61/66) on adverse-event ratesResults, Massively parallel extraction
85.3% agreement with Therapeutics Data Commons across 7,278 trialsResults, Massively parallel extraction
Drugs against cell-type-specific genes: 40% more likely Phase I to II, 48% more likely to reach Phase IV, 32% lower adverse-event ratesAbstract; Fig. 3A-B
The same Results paragraph gives an odds ratio of 1.27 (95% CI 1.22-1.33) for Phase I to II, an OR for a z-scored continuous feature that does not correspond to the abstract's 40% and is left unreconciledResults; Fig. 3A
Robustness: 1,000 permutations, mixed-effects models, adjustment for genetic evidence and repetition in the 74.6% subset without genetic evidence preserve the conclusionFigs. S2-S4
B7-H3 is upregulated in fibroblasts (SCLC log2FC 1.94, LUAD 1.46) and immune cells are depleted around B7-H3-high spots across 12 LUAD Visium samplesResults, B7-H3; Fig. S5
In 566 TCGA LUAD patients high B7-H3 gives HR 1.62 for overall and 1.82 for disease-specific survival; an ADC is proposed, at a query cost of $46.00Results, B7-H3
Genetics support OSMR (rs395157, OR 1.06, p = 2.68 x 10^-10; locus-to-gene 0.970) yet the atlas shows no significant UC-versus-healthy OSMR differenceResults, OSMR
In TAURUS (22 patients, 485,868 cells) OSMR and JAK-STAT activity rise in non-responder fibroblasts after anti-TNF; biomarker-guided enrolment is proposed, at a cost of $54.00Results, OSMR

结论Conclusions

作者认为 Virtual Biotech 能让多尺度治疗学分析更透明、更高效、更全面,从而加速早期药物发现,同时把人类科学家留在环中。他们强调三个案例分别展示了大规模结构化数据生产、前瞻性靶点评估与失败试验的机制复盘,并明确指出案例输出属于假设,仍需实验与临床验证。

The authors argue the Virtual Biotech makes multi-scale therapeutic analysis more transparent, efficient and comprehensive, accelerating early discovery while keeping human scientists in the loop. The three applications are presented as large-scale structured data production, prospective target assessment and mechanistic post-mortem of a failed trial, with the case-study outputs explicitly framed as hypotheses needing experimental and clinical validation.

局限Limitations

  • 作者自述:AI 的分析或解释错误会沿链条传播,因此人类专家必须留在环中。
  • 作者自述:试验层面的分析是观察性的,可能被适应证混杂、终点定义差异与申办方效应干扰。
  • 作者自述:只纳入在 ClinicalTrials.gov 注册且靶点可映射的试验。
  • 作者自述:存在预训练泄漏的可能,作者以挑选 readout 晚于知识截止的案例并检索是否已有先前报道来缓解。
  • 作者自述:案例输出是假设,需要实验与临床验证。
  • 我们的理解:全文(含 Data availability)没有代码可用性声明或仓库链接,界面所称可下载的 reproducible code 未给出位置;对一篇以系统为贡献的论文,这使独立复现几乎不可能。
  • 我们的理解:数字口径不自洽——摘要的 40%/48%/32% 与正文 OR 1.27 (1.22–1.33) 对不上且未解释;工具数在 over 100 tools、more than 100 customized MCPs、hundreds of tools 与 10 个 MCP server 之间摇摆;37,075 个 agent 是并行实例数而非不同 agent,与 55,984 条试验的关系未说明。
  • 我们的理解:没有与非 agent 基线的对比(单 agent、无部门结构的 agent 池、或纯脚本 pipeline),因此「组织结构带来了什么」无法量化;抽取一致率衡量的是 LLM 抽取能力,与多 agent 架构无关。
  • 我们的理解:验证只覆盖抽取、不覆盖推理。两个案例的正确性分别靠一个事后的监管里程碑(ifinatamab deruxtecan 于 2025 年 8 月获突破性疗法认定)和一个合理但未检验的试验重设计方案支撑,都是 n=1 的轶事性证据;Scientific Reviewer 的拦截率与漏报率未报告。
  • 我们的理解:CC-BY-ND 许可不允许发布改编版本,对想在其架构上改造的后续工作是实际障碍。
  • Authors': AI analysis or interpretation errors can propagate, so human experts must stay in the loop.
  • Authors': the trial analyses are observational, with possible confounding by indication, endpoint definitions and sponsor effects.
  • Authors': only ClinicalTrials.gov-registered trials with mappable targets are included.
  • Authors': pretraining leakage is possible, mitigated by case studies with readouts after the cutoff and checks for prior reports.
  • Authors': case-study outputs are hypotheses requiring validation.
  • Our reading: no code-availability statement or repository link appears anywhere in the text, and the downloadable reproducible code the interface is said to offer has no stated location, so a system-contribution paper cannot be independently reproduced.
  • Our reading: several numbers do not line up - the abstract's 40/48/32% versus the Results' OR 1.27 (1.22-1.33); the tool count drifting between over 100 tools, more than 100 customized MCPs, hundreds of tools and ten MCP servers; and the 37,075 agents being parallel instances, with no explanation of how that relates to 55,984 trials.
  • Our reading: there is no non-agent baseline (single agent, flat agent pool or scripted pipeline), so the benefit of the organisational structure is unquantified; the agreement rates measure LLM extraction, not the multi-agent architecture.
  • Our reading: validation covers extraction but not reasoning. The case studies rest on one post-hoc regulatory milestone (ifinatamab deruxtecan's Breakthrough Therapy Designation in August 2025) and one untested trial redesign - n=1 anecdotes - and no catch or miss rate is reported for the Scientific Reviewer.
  • Our reading: the CC-BY-ND licence forbids distributing adapted versions, an obstacle for follow-up work modifying the architecture.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这篇正对 Topic 2(验证生物医学分析 agent 的声明),同时是 detect → model → revert 中 revert 端「选靶点」的最大自由度版本——GPNMB 那篇把 LLM 关在「给 7 个特征配权重 + 从 100 个基因里选」的笼子里,这篇让 agent 自己决定调什么库、跑什么分析。它送上一份现成的待验证声明清单,可按可核查性分三层做成三个难度梯度的评测任务:① 可机械核查的试验抽取(有人工与 TDC 金标准,最适合做幻觉基准);② 可统计复核的「细胞类型特异性 ↔ 试验成功」关联(但须先解决 40% 与 OR 1.27 的口径问题,这本身就是一个很好的论文内部数字不一致检测案例);③ 只能定性评估的两个案例推理链。对 Topic 1,两点直接可借:把「细胞类型特异性 → 可干预性」这个先验搬到肾脏,作为异常分数的正交证据维度与 revert 候选排序先验;以及用「每单元一个 agent 实例并行标注 + 人工抽样复核并报一致率」的工程模式来生产我们的专家验证 flag。架构上最值得抄的是用便宜小模型做审查与简报、以及让 reviewer 有权要求重新分派——而本文没报 reviewer 拦截率,正是我们可以补的空白。

Our reading: this is squarely Topic 2 material and the maximum-autonomy version of revert-end target picking - where the GPNMB paper cages the LLM into weighting seven features and choosing from 100 genes, here the agents decide which databases to query and which analyses to run. It hands us a ready list of claims to verify, stratified into three difficulty tiers: mechanically checkable trial extractions with a gold standard (the best place to benchmark hallucination); statistically re-checkable association between cell-type specificity and trial success (after resolving the 40%-versus-OR-1.27 discrepancy, itself a good test case for detecting internal numerical inconsistency); and only qualitatively assessable case-study reasoning chains. For Topic 1, two transfers: port the cell-type-specificity-implies-tractability prior to kidney as an orthogonal evidence dimension for anomaly scores and a prior for ranking revert candidates; and reuse the engineering pattern of one agent instance per unit in parallel plus a human-reviewed sample with a reported agreement rate to produce our expert-validated flags. Architecturally, the pieces worth copying are a cheap small model for review and briefing and a reviewer empowered to force re-delegation - and the unreported reviewer catch rate is exactly the gap we can fill.

AI-driven discovery of GPNMB CAR T cells as a multi-cancer therapy

Baker DJ, Frommer LM, Uslu U, et al. AI-driven discovery of GPNMB CAR T cells as a multi-cancer therapy. Cell. 2026;189(13):3871-3882.e12.

doi:10.1016/j.cell.2026.06.002 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

全文已读full text read无本地 PDFno local PDF
PMC 作者稿全文(PMC13428302):Results、Discussion、Limitations、STAR Methods、图注与 Key Resource Table。期刊排版版付费。
Full text of the NIH author manuscript at PMC (PMC13428302): Results, Discussion, Limitations, STAR Methods, figure legends and Key Resource Table. The typeset journal version is paywalled.
把单细胞图谱、六个公共知识库的打分特征和三个 LLM 的加权与提名串成一条可复现的靶点发现流水线,头号候选 GPNMB 被做成 CAR T 细胞,在白血病、黑色素瘤、结直肠癌三种小鼠移植瘤模型中显示强效抗肿瘤活性。
A reproducible target-discovery pipeline chaining a single-cell atlas, scored features from six public knowledge bases, and three LLMs for feature weighting and nomination; its top candidate GPNMB was built into a CAR T cell that cleared tumours in mouse models of leukaemia, melanoma and colorectal cancer.

问题Problem

CAR T 在血液肿瘤中已有治愈级疗效,但安全靶点太少是扩展到实体瘤的主要瓶颈。理想靶点必须同时满足肿瘤高表达、要害器官(心肺脑)几乎不表达、位于细胞表面、并且最好已有临床级抗体可借用。传统做法是人工翻数据库逐条筛选,慢、碎片化且带偏见。作者要把这个多目标权衡自动化,并验证它在没有资深专家时是否仍可用。

CAR T therapy is curative in some blood cancers, but the scarcity of safe target antigens is the main bottleneck for solid tumours. A good target must be high on tumour cells, near-absent in vital organs, located on the cell surface, and ideally already have a clinical-grade antibody. The classical workflow is manual database mining: slow, fragmented and bias-prone. The authors automate this multi-objective trade-off and test whether it still works without deep domain expertise.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 建图谱:4 个公开皮肤癌 scRNA-seq 数据集用 Harmony(以 sample ID 为批次变量)整合成 285,557 cells / 91 samples;抽出 106,834 个 malignant 细胞,与 Tabula Sapiens 的 17,786 个健康皮肤细胞再做一次 Harmony,得到 124,620 cells / 111 samples 的工作图谱。
  2. 造 7 个确定性特征:从 ClinicalTrials.gov API v2、OpenTargets 25.03、GTEx v8、ProteomicsDB 1.1、HPA 24.0、UniProt 2025_02 程序化抓取,得到临床可成药性(相位+状态+癌症相关+modality 的加分规则,每基因只留最强的一项试验)、亚细胞定位(HPA 与 UniProt 双证据 10/8/7/5/0 分)、cancer composition(Z_malignant − Z_non-malignant,即表达>0 的细胞百分比各自跨基因取 Z-score 后相减)、GTEx 要害/非要害组织均值、ProteomicsDB 蛋白要害/非要害均值。
  3. 归一化聚合:每特征 min–max 到 [0,1],越低越好的特征取反,重复基因取最大值,缺失补 0,最终分数为加权和。
  4. LLM 第一遍——定权重:专家先给出 base weights(临床试验 1.0、HPA 2.0、单细胞 Z 差 5.0、要害 2.0、非要害 1.0、蛋白要害 2.0、蛋白非要害 1.0);把 7 个特征定义、7 套候选权重与各自 top-100 列表放进固定 prompt,强制机器可读表头,对 GPT-4o、Claude-3.7-Sonnet、Gemini-2.5-Pro 各跑 1000 次(temperature 0.70–0.85 循环),用分层 regex 解析,并刻意不告诉模型哪套来自专家。
  5. LLM 第二遍——提名:用共识权重取 top-100,每模型再跑 1000 次,从这 100 个基因里排序选 10 个,输出必须是固定 CSV,理由只能逐字取自 5 条预设短语;解析后过滤只保留三模型都提名过的基因,再按总提名次数排序。
  6. 消融与鲁棒性:累计 3000 次模拟/模型;去掉专家 base weights 重跑;把单细胞 composition 特征换成 bulk(TCGA-SKCM vs GTEx 日晒皮肤,DESeq2 1.46.0 的 log2FC 跨基因 Z-score)。
  7. 湿实验验证链:TCGA bulk 差异表达 → 4 个黑色素瘤细胞系免疫荧光 → 非透膜流式(只染真正在表面的蛋白)→ 多器官组织微阵列 IHC(盲评 1–5 分)。
  8. 造 CAR 并评估:取 CR011(全人源抗 GPNMB 抗体,即 glembatumumab vedotin 的结合域)的 VL/VH 用 (G4S)3 连成 scFv,装入第二代 CAR(CD8α hinge/跨膜 + 4-1BB + CD3ζ),pTRPE 慢病毒转导健康供者 T 细胞;体外测增殖、细胞因子、杀伤;体内用 NSG 小鼠做 U937 白血病(1e5 细胞 i.v.,D0 给 5e6 CAR T)与 SK-MEL-3 黑色素瘤 / SNU-503 结直肠癌皮下模型(5e5 细胞,瘤达约 100 mm³ 即 day 22 随机化,给 4e6 CAR T),以卡尺瘤体积与 Kaplan–Meier 生存为读数。
  1. Integrate four public skin-cancer scRNA-seq datasets with Harmony (285,557 cells / 91 samples), extract the 106,834 malignant cells and re-integrate them with 17,786 healthy Tabula Sapiens skin cells to give a 124,620-cell / 111-sample working atlas.
  2. Compute 7 deterministic features from six public resources (ClinicalTrials.gov API v2, OpenTargets 25.03, GTEx v8, ProteomicsDB 1.1, HPA 24.0, UniProt 2025_02): clinical tractability, surface localisation (HPA plus UniProt concordance scored 10/8/7/5/0), cancer composition as Z_malignant minus Z_non-malignant of the percentage of cells with expression above zero, and GTEx plus ProteomicsDB means over vital and non-vital tissues.
  3. Min-max normalise each feature across genes, invert the features where lower is better, deduplicate by maximum, impute missing as 0, and score genes by a weighted sum.
  4. LLM pass 1 - weights: experts set base weights (clinical trials 1.0, HPA 2.0, single-cell Z difference 5.0, vital 2.0, non-vital 1.0, protein vital 2.0, protein non-vital 1.0); a fixed prompt with 7 feature definitions, 7 candidate schemes and their top-100 lists forces a machine-readable header, and GPT-4o, Claude-3.7-Sonnet and Gemini-2.5-Pro are each queried 1,000 times (temperature cycling 0.70-0.85) with a tiered regex parser. The models are never told which scheme came from experts.
  5. LLM pass 2 - nomination: the consensus weights give a top-100 shortlist; each model is run 1,000 more times to rank-select 10 of the 100 in a fixed CSV with reasons drawn verbatim from five preset phrases; only genes nominated by all three models survive, ranked by total nominations.
  6. Ablations: 3,000 cumulative simulations per model; the expert base weights removed; and the single-cell composition feature replaced by bulk (DESeq2 log2 fold-change Z-scores for TCGA-SKCM versus GTEx sun-exposed skin).
  7. Wet-lab chain: TCGA bulk differential expression, immunofluorescence in four melanoma lines, non-permeabilised flow cytometry for genuine surface protein, and blind-scored multi-organ tissue-microarray IHC.
  8. Build and test the CAR: the CR011 (glembatumumab vedotin) VL/VH joined by a (G4S)3 linker into a second-generation CAR (CD8alpha hinge and transmembrane, 4-1BB, CD3zeta) delivered by pTRPE lentivirus into donor T cells; in vitro proliferation, cytokine and killing assays; in vivo NSG models of U937 leukaemia (1e5 cells i.v., 5e6 CAR T on day 0) and subcutaneous SK-MEL-3 melanoma or SNU-503 colorectal cancer (5e5 cells, randomised at about 100 mm3 on day 22, 4e6 CAR T), read out by caliper tumour volume and Kaplan-Meier survival.

术语Terms

CAR T cell
把患者或供者 T 细胞改造,装上一个人工受体,使其识别某个细胞表面蛋白后被激活并杀死该细胞的活细胞药物。
GPNMB
一型跨膜糖蛋白,参与细胞黏附、迁移、组织修复与免疫抑制;本文中的头号候选靶点。
scRNA-seq / 单细胞转录组
逐个细胞测量基因表达,从而能区分肿瘤细胞与浸润的免疫细胞;bulk RNA-seq 则只给组织的平均值。
Harmony
单细胞数据的批次校正方法,把来自不同实验的细胞在低维嵌入空间中对齐。
on-target / off-tumor 毒性
CAR T 杀死了确实带有该靶点、但属于健康组织的细胞,是 CAR T 最严重的毒性来源。
非透膜流式 (non-permeabilised flow cytometry)
不打破细胞膜就染色,因此只能检出真正暴露在细胞表面的蛋白——CAR 只能识别表面蛋白,故必须这样验证。
xenograft / NSG 小鼠
把人源肿瘤细胞移植到免疫缺陷小鼠体内;小鼠自身没有免疫系统,所以能长出人肿瘤,但也无法评估免疫微环境。
IHC / 免疫组化
用抗体在组织切片上染出某蛋白的位置与强度,用来在真实病人组织中确认表达。
CAR T cell
A living drug: T cells engineered with an artificial receptor so they activate and kill any cell carrying a chosen surface protein.
GPNMB
A type I transmembrane glycoprotein involved in adhesion, migration, tissue repair and immunosuppression; the paper's top candidate target.
on-target / off-tumour toxicity
CAR T cells killing healthy cells that genuinely carry the target antigen - the most serious CAR T toxicity.
non-permeabilised flow cytometry
Staining without breaking the membrane, so only protein truly exposed on the cell surface is detected - required because a CAR can only see surface protein.
xenograft / NSG mouse
Human tumour cells grown in an immunodeficient mouse: the tumour engrafts, but the immune microenvironment cannot be studied.
Harmony
A batch-correction method that aligns cells from different single-cell experiments in a shared low-dimensional embedding.

数据Data

单细胞来自 4 个公开皮肤癌数据集(GEO GSE185386、GSE144240、GSE123139、GSE123814)与 Tabula Sapiens 正常皮肤(GSE201333),整合后 285,557 cells / 91 samples,最终工作图谱 124,620 cells / 111 samples(106,834 恶性 + 17,786 正常)。知识层用 ClinicalTrials.gov API v2、OpenTargets 25.03、GTEx v8、ProteomicsDB 1.1、HPA v24.0、UniProt 2025_02;bulk 用 TCGA-SKCM 与 GEPIA。实验材料包括 11 条人源细胞系、两张组织微阵列(MC5004b、BN1021a)、2 位健康供者 T 细胞(ND607、ND657)与 NSG 小鼠。

Single cell: four public skin-cancer scRNA-seq datasets (GEO GSE185386, GSE144240, GSE123139, GSE123814) plus Tabula Sapiens normal skin (GSE201333); 285,557 cells from 91 samples after integration and a 124,620-cell / 111-sample final atlas (106,834 malignant, 17,786 normal). Knowledge layer: ClinicalTrials.gov API v2, OpenTargets 25.03, GTEx v8, ProteomicsDB 1.1, HPA v24.0, UniProt 2025_02; bulk from TCGA-SKCM and GEPIA. Experimental material: 11 human cell lines, two tissue microarrays (MC5004b, BN1021a), T cells from two healthy donors and NSG mice.

主要结果Key results

GPNMB 是被提名次数最多的靶点,一贯因肿瘤特异性被选,同时在多癌种潜力上有强信号;对照 PMEL 几乎只因肿瘤特异性被提名,IGF1R 相反Fig. 1F-G
去掉专家先验权重后,top-100 基因有 94/100 重叠,最终 top-15 提名有 12/15 相同Supp Fig. 3B-F
把输入换成 bulk RNA-seq 后,top-100 只剩 21/100 共享、top-15 只剩 2/15 共享;bulk 转录本仅 48.2% 在单细胞图谱中有代表Supp Fig. 3H-L, Results
bulk 的 top-15 中 TIGIT、CTLA4(实为 T 细胞)与 CD83(髓系细胞)在单细胞分辨率下暴露为不适合的靶点Supp Fig. 3M
稳定性:再跑两轮,每模型累计 3,000 次独立模拟,结论不变Supp Fig. 3N
GPNMB、ERBB3、IGF1R 在 TCGA 黑色素瘤中相对正常组织上调(one-way ANOVA,*P<0.01),并由非透膜流式确认位于细胞表面Fig. 1I, Fig. 1K
GPNMB 在 TCGA 中跨多癌种显著富集,包括两种肾癌 KICH 与 KIRP;健康组织普遍低或无表达Fig. 2A, Supp Fig. 5A
白血病模型:对照 T 细胞组数周内达人道终点,CAR T 组存活数月且无疾病征象(n=15 vs 14,log-rank,****P<0.0001)Fig. 2L
黑色素瘤模型:瘤体显著缩小,多数小鼠完全缓解(n=16 CAR T vs 14 对照,two-way ANOVA,****P<0.0001)Fig. 3E
结直肠癌模型:全部小鼠完全缓解,生存显著改善;体外对结直肠、胰腺、肾癌来源细胞均见抗原依赖杀伤Fig. 3K-L, Fig. 3G
GPNMB is the most frequently nominated target, chosen for tumour specificity with a strong multi-cancer signal; PMEL is nominated almost only for specificity and IGF1R the reverseFig. 1F-G
Removing the expert prior weights leaves 94 of the top 100 genes and 12 of the top 15 nominees unchangedSupp Fig. 3B-F
Switching the input to bulk RNA-seq leaves only 21 of 100 and 2 of 15 shared; just 48.2% of bulk transcripts are represented in the single-cell atlasSupp Fig. 3H-L, Results
Among the bulk top 15, TIGIT and CTLA4 (T cells) and CD83 (myeloid) are exposed as unsuitable at single-cell resolutionSupp Fig. 3M
Stability: two further iterations, 3,000 independent simulations per model, leave the conclusion unchangedSupp Fig. 3N
GPNMB, ERBB3 and IGF1R are up in TCGA melanoma versus normal (one-way ANOVA, *P<0.01) and confirmed on the surface by non-permeabilised flowFig. 1I, Fig. 1K
GPNMB is significantly enriched across many TCGA cancers including both kidney cancers KICH and KIRP, with low or absent expression in healthy tissueFig. 2A, Supp Fig. 5A
Leukaemia model: control-T mice reach humane endpoints within weeks while CAR T mice survive for months disease-free (n=15 vs 14, log-rank, ****P<0.0001)Fig. 2L
Melanoma model: tumour volume drops sharply with complete remission in the majority of mice (n=16 CAR T vs 14 control, two-way ANOVA, ****P<0.0001)Fig. 3E
Colorectal model: all mice achieve complete remission with significantly better survival; in vitro killing extends to colorectal, pancreatic and renal cancer cellsFig. 3K-L, Fig. 3G

结论Conclusions

作者认为这建立了一条可扩展、可泛化的 CAR T 靶点发现流水线:把 LLM 接到真实数据与公共知识上,既能做出合理的多目标权衡,也可能降低幻觉风险。GPNMB 因跨癌种表达、要害组织低表达、且已有临床验证过的抗体结合域可借用,被认为具备多癌种治疗的转化潜力;流水线本身还可推广到蛋白组、糖组或胞内肽等其他 T 细胞疗法模态。

The authors claim a scalable, generalisable CAR T target-discovery pipeline: grounding LLMs in real data and public knowledge yields sensible multi-objective trade-offs and may reduce hallucination risk. GPNMB, expressed across diverse tumours, low in vital tissues and already served by a clinically tested antibody binding domain, is put forward as a multi-cancer therapeutic candidate; the framework could extend to proteomic, glycomic or intracellular-peptide targets for other T-cell modalities.

局限Limitations

  • 作者自述:全部体内实验在免疫缺陷 xenograft 小鼠中完成,无法评估 T 细胞外在机制、肿瘤微环境影响与 on-target/off-tumor 毒性。
  • 作者自述:该 CAR 只识别人 GPNMB、不识别小鼠 GPNMB,因此无法在小鼠组织中直接做安全性测试。
  • 作者自述:复发的黑色素瘤肿瘤未取到做机制分析;白血病监测只看外周血,而血中未检出循环肿瘤细胞。
  • 作者自述:发现阶段的打分受限于目前单细胞分辨率研究的数量。
  • 作者自述:为跨模型可比而用了通用 prompt 结构,可能低估针对单模型优化 prompt 的收益。
  • 我们的理解:LLM 的自由度很窄(特征集、7 套候选权重、100 基因短名单、5 条理由均由人设定),真正决定 GPNMB 胜出的是特征工程,尤其 cancer-composition Z 差。
  • 我们的理解:缺少与简单基线(直接用专家权重排序、或无 LLM 的均匀权重 ensemble)的对比,故 LLM 这一步的增益无法量化;消融结果(94/100 不变)暗示增益有限。
  • 我们的理解:三个模型的共识不是独立证据,训练语料高度重叠且 GPNMB / glembatumumab vedotin 的文献早已公开,共识可能部分来自共享的文献先验。
  • 我们的理解:健康组织低表达的结论基于正常组织数据,未涵盖病变(纤维化、损伤)肾脏,而 GPNMB 在 KICH/KIRP 两种肾癌中显著富集,这一空白需要我们自行核查。
  • Authors': all in vivo work is in immunodeficient xenograft mice, precluding assessment of T-cell-extrinsic mechanisms, the microenvironment and on-target/off-tumour toxicity.
  • Authors': the CAR recognises human but not murine GPNMB, so safety cannot be tested directly in mouse tissue.
  • Authors': recurrent melanoma tumours were unavailable for mechanistic analysis, and leukaemia monitoring was limited to blood, where no circulating tumour cells were found.
  • Authors': discovery-stage scoring is limited by how many single-cell-resolution studies exist today.
  • Authors': a generalised prompt structure was used for cross-model comparability, possibly underrepresenting model-specific prompt optimisation.
  • Our reading: the LLM's degrees of freedom are narrow - feature set, candidate weightings, 100-gene shortlist and five reasons are all human-set - so the feature engineering, especially the cancer-composition Z difference, is what really selects GPNMB.
  • Our reading: no comparison with a simple baseline (expert weights alone, or an LLM-free uniform-weight ensemble), so the LLM step's contribution is unquantified; the ablation hints it is small.
  • Our reading: agreement across three models is not independent evidence, since their corpora overlap and the GPNMB literature was already public.
  • Our reading: the low-in-healthy-tissue conclusion rests on normal tissue only and does not cover diseased kidney, while GPNMB is enriched in KICH and KIRP - a gap to check ourselves.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是 detect → model → revert 中最靠 revert 一端的样本,直接回答「选哪个分子去干预」。三点可借用:① cancer composition 的 Z_malignant − Z_non-malignant 与我们的异常评分在形式上同构,换成 Z_disease − Z_reference 即可当作肾脏 snRNA/Xenium 的 reference-based 异常检测朴素基线;② 可干预性约束层(表面定位、要害组织低表达、已有临床抗体)正是我们 revert 端缺的一层,换成肾脏版(肾小管/足细胞特异、肾外低表达、已有药物)就是可落地的筛选器;③ 把 LLM 当分布来审计(1000 次采样、受限输出词表、分层 parser、ANOVA/FDR、跨模型共识过滤)是 Topic 2 现成的评测骨架,而「理由分布分歧大但结论一致」正是 agent 可信度研究要刻画的现象。此外这是 Sikander 团队参与的工作(他为通讯作者之一,组内 Leon Frommer 为并列第二作者,代码在 hayatlab/cart_llms),是理解该组方法论的最佳入口。

Our reading: this is the revert end of detect to model to revert, answering which molecule to intervene on. Three reusable pieces: the cancer-composition contrast Z_malignant minus Z_non-malignant is formally our anomaly score, so Z_disease minus Z_reference gives a naive reference-based baseline for kidney snRNA or Xenium; the tractability constraint layer (surface, low vital-tissue, existing antibody) is the layer our revert end lacks and ports straight to a kidney version; and auditing an LLM as a distribution (1,000 samples, closed output vocabulary, tiered parser, ANOVA/FDR, cross-model consensus filtering) is a ready evaluation skeleton for Topic 2, where diverging reasons under an agreeing conclusion is exactly the phenomenon to characterise. It is also work from Sikander's own team (co-corresponding author, with lab member Leon Frommer as joint second author; code at hayatlab/cart_llms), so it is the best entry point to the group's methodology.

数据集与竞赛Datasets & challenges

30

HEST-1k: A Dataset for Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology Image Analysis

Jaume G, Doucet P, Song AH, et al. HEST-1k: A Dataset for Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology Image Analysis. arXiv:2406.16192v2. 2024.

https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.16192 · source.pdf

全文已读full text readPDF
arXiv v2 PDF 全文(pdftotext)+ GitHub README
full arXiv v2 PDF via pdftotext + GitHub README
把 153 个队列的 1,229 个空间转录组样本统一成“H&E 全切片 + 对齐的 spot 表达 + 元数据”,并配一个“从 H&E patch 预测基因表达”的基准,评测 11 个病理基础模型。
Unifies 1,229 spatial-transcriptomics samples from 153 cohorts into H&E whole-slide image + aligned spot expression + metadata, plus a benchmark that scores 11 pathology foundation models on predicting gene expression from H&E patches.

问题Problem

空间转录组(ST)数据格式杂乱、对齐信息缺失或不准,计算方法大多只在几个病人上开发。配套的 H&E 形态信息常被忽略,而现有病理 patch 基准已接近饱和。作者想建立一个统一的大规模配对资源,并用“形态预测表达”作为评测病理基础模型的新任务。

Spatial-transcriptomics (ST) data come in inconsistent formats with missing or poor alignment, so methods are built on a few patients. The paired H&E morphology is often ignored, and existing pathology patch benchmarks are near saturation. The authors build one large unified paired resource and propose morphology-to-expression prediction as a new benchmark.

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 收集:从 10x Genomics、NCBI、Zenodo、Mendeley 等公开库和内部队列抓取所有带 H&E 全切片的 ST 数据;丢弃像素尺寸大于 1.15 µm/px 的图像(§3, §3.1)。
  2. 统一格式:图像转为金字塔 TIFF;表达以原始 count 存为 AnnData;用微调的 DeepLabV3 分割组织与背景;癌种用 OncoTree 编码(§3.1–3.4)。
  3. 对齐:Visium 用微调 YOLOv8 检测芯片四角的 fiducial 标记,再由已知 spot 间距反推像素尺寸;Xenium 用 VALIS 把 DAPI 图配准到 H&E(§4, Fig. 5)。
  4. 切 patch:20× 下围绕每个 spot 切 224×224 像素(112×112 µm)patch;Xenium 按 55×55 µm 方格汇总成伪 spot;共约 210 万对 (patch, 表达)(§3.2)。
  5. 核分割:CellViT 对所有切片做核分割与 5 类分类,共 7,640 万个核(§3.3)。
  6. 基准:每任务选 50 个最高变基因并 log1p;冻结编码器提 patch embedding,PCA 到 256 维后 Ridge 回归(另有 XGBoost、直接 Ridge);按病人分层 k 折;指标为预测与实测的 Pearson 相关(§5.1–5.2)。
  7. 示范 1:把 CellViT 核与 Xenium 细胞按质心匹配,计算 12 个核特征与基因表达的相关(§6, App. E)。
  8. 示范 2:用 InfoNCE 对比损失对齐 CONCH 图像编码器(只调最后 3 层)与 3 层 MLP 表达编码器,在独立队列 BCNB 上做 ER/PR/HER2 分型(§7, App. D)。
  1. Collect every public/internal ST dataset that has an H&E whole-slide image; drop images coarser than 1.15 µm/px (§3).
  2. Unify: pyramidal TIFF images, raw-count AnnData expression, DeepLabV3 tissue segmentation, OncoTree cancer codes (§3.1–3.4).
  3. Align: YOLOv8 finds Visium corner fiducials and pixel size is inferred from spot spacing; VALIS registers Xenium DAPI to H&E (§4).
  4. Cut 224×224-px (112 µm) patches at 20× around each spot; Xenium pooled into 55 µm pseudo-spots; ~2.1M pairs (§3.2).
  5. Benchmark: frozen encoder embeddings, PCA-256 + Ridge (also XGBoost, plain Ridge) predicting 50 top-variance log1p genes; patient-stratified k-fold; Pearson r (§5).
  6. Demos: CellViT nuclear features vs Xenium expression (§6); InfoNCE alignment of CONCH with an MLP expression encoder, tested on ER/PR/HER2 (§7).

术语Terms

空间转录组 spatial transcriptomics (ST)
在组织切片上同时测基因表达和它在切片上的位置。
spot
Visium 一类技术的测量单元,直径约 55 µm 的小圆点,通常包含多个细胞。
Visium / Xenium
10x Genomics 的两类 ST:Visium 基于测序、按 spot 测全转录组;Xenium 基于成像、亚细胞精度但只测几百到几千个基因。
H&E 全切片 WSI
苏木精-伊红染色的组织切片扫描图,常规病理用,单张可达十万像素见方。
fiducial
Visium 芯片四角的定位标记,用来把 spot 坐标对到图像上。
FFPE / 冷冻切片
两种组织保存方式:福尔马林固定石蜡包埋,或新鲜冷冻;影响图像和 RNA 质量。
ccRCC
透明细胞肾细胞癌,最常见的肾癌;HEST-Benchmark 唯一的肾脏任务。
空间转录组 spatial transcriptomics (ST)
在组织切片上同时测基因表达和它在切片上的位置。
spot
Visium 一类技术的测量单元,直径约 55 µm 的小圆点,通常包含多个细胞。
Visium / Xenium
10x Genomics 的两类 ST:Visium 基于测序、按 spot 测全转录组;Xenium 基于成像、亚细胞精度但只测几百到几千个基因。
H&E 全切片 WSI
苏木精-伊红染色的组织切片扫描图,常规病理用,单张可达十万像素见方。
fiducial
Visium 芯片四角的定位标记,用来把 spot 坐标对到图像上。
FFPE / 冷冻切片
两种组织保存方式:福尔马林固定石蜡包埋,或新鲜冷冻;影响图像和 RNA 质量。
ccRCC
透明细胞肾细胞癌,最常见的肾癌;HEST-Benchmark 唯一的肾脏任务。

数据Data

HEST-1k v2 共 1,229 个样本、153 个队列、26 个器官、人和小鼠两个物种;367 个癌症样本覆盖 25 种癌型;约 210 万对 patch–表达,7,640 万个细胞核(Abstract)。其中肾脏样本 70 个(Fig. 1a),包括 Lake 等人类肾脏图谱的 23 个 Visium 样本(Table A5)。基准的 9 个任务大多只有 2–4 个病人,ccRCC 有 24 个(Table A11)。

HEST-1k v2: 1,229 samples, 153 cohorts, 26 organs, human and mouse; 367 cancer samples from 25 cancer types; ~2.1M patch–expression pairs and 76.4M nuclei (Abstract). 70 kidney samples (Fig. 1a), including 23 Visium samples from the Lake et al. kidney atlas (Table A5). Most of the 9 benchmark tasks have 2–4 patients; ccRCC has 24 (Table A11).

主要结果Key results

PCA+Ridge 下平均 Pearson 最高:H-Optimus-0 0.4146,UNIv1.5 0.4090;最低 ResNet50(ImageNet)0.326Table 1
任务间差距大:H-Optimus-0 在 SKCM 0.6432,在 READ 0.2292§5.3
肾癌 ccRCC 在所有模型上只有 0.2179–0.2788Table 1
编码器参数量(对数)与平均表现 R=0.81(P<0.01);预训练 patch 数 R=0.48(P=0.13)Fig. 2
直接 Ridge 读出时 CONCH 平均最高(0.3541),排名随读出头改变Table A13
Xenium 乳腺癌中肿瘤核面积与 GATA3 表达 R=0.47(P<10⁻⁴);形状/拓扑特征 R<0.2Fig. 3e, §6
CONCH-FT 在 BCNB(1,058 张 WSI)上 ER AUC 0.881→0.884、PR 0.810→0.818、HER2 0.715→0.724,但 HER2 balanced accuracy 0.624→0.615Table 2
Best mean Pearson (PCA+Ridge): H-Optimus-0 0.4146, UNIv1.5 0.4090; lowest ResNet50 (ImageNet) 0.326Table 1
Large task spread: H-Optimus-0 0.6432 on SKCM vs 0.2292 on READ§5.3
Kidney cancer ccRCC: 0.2179–0.2788 across all modelsTable 1
Log encoder size vs performance R=0.81 (P<0.01); pretraining patches R=0.48 (P=0.13)Fig. 2
With plain Ridge, CONCH has the best mean (0.3541): ranking depends on the readoutTable A13
Neoplastic nuclear area vs GATA3 R=0.47 (P<10⁻⁴); shape/topology features R<0.2Fig. 3e, §6
CONCH-FT on BCNB (1,058 WSIs): ER AUC 0.881→0.884, PR 0.810→0.818, HER2 0.715→0.724; HER2 balanced accuracy 0.624→0.615Table 2

结论Conclusions

作者认为 HEST-1k 让大规模的形态–分子联合建模成为可能;基准显示模型规模与表现呈对数增长,但部分癌种(READ、ccRCC)的表达很难从形态预测,可能是形态本身信息不足或队列噪声大(§5.3, §8)。

The authors see HEST-1k as enabling large-scale joint morphology–molecular modelling; performance grows logarithmically with model size, but some cancers (READ, ccRCC) remain hard, either because morphology carries little expression signal or because those cohorts are noisy (§5.3, §8).

局限Limitations

  • 作者:ST 数据有噪声,染色、压缩伪影与流程差异会影响质量(§8)。
  • 作者:跨样本/数据集/技术的批次效应没有量化或校正,只提供工具(§8)。
  • 作者:HEST-Library 不能覆盖所有格式;CONCH-FT 只用了 5 张切片(§7, §8)。
  • 我们的理解:多数任务只有 2–4 个病人,模型间 0.5% 量级的差异未必显著。
  • 我们的理解:基准只有癌症任务,不含 CKD/AKI 这类非肿瘤肾病。
  • Authors: ST data are noisy; staining, compression and protocol differences affect quality (§8).
  • Authors: batch effects are not quantified or corrected; only tools are provided (§8).
  • Authors: the library cannot cover every format; CONCH-FT used only 5 slides (§7, §8).
  • Our reading: with 2–4 patients per task, ~0.5% model differences are unlikely to be significant.
  • Our reading: cancer tasks only; no CKD/AKI (non-tumour kidney disease).

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:HEST 不是异常检测方法,而是 model 一环的数据底座和 detect 一环的跨模态工具。它的格式和对齐流程可直接用于整理我们 95 例活检的 H&E + Xenium,70 个肾脏样本可作外部参考集。更重要的是,H&E 预测表达只有中等相关(约 0.2–0.6),所以“H&E 与表达不一致”要先减去正常组织的预测误差,才能当作异常信号,否则会把预测器误差误判为疾病。

Our reading: not an anomaly-detection method but a data substrate for "model" and a cross-modal tool for "detect". Its format and alignment pipeline can organise our ~95-biopsy H&E + Xenium data, and its 70 kidney samples can act as an external reference. Because H&E predicts expression only moderately (r ~0.2–0.6), an H&E-vs-expression disagreement must be corrected for normal-tissue prediction error before it counts as an anomaly; otherwise predictor error is mistaken for disease.

Virtual Cell Challenge

Roohani YH, Hua TJ, Tung PY, et al. Virtual Cell Challenge: Toward a Turing test for the virtual cell. Cell. 2025;188(13):3370-3374.

doi:10.1016/j.cell.2025.06.008 · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

网页 / READMEweb page / README无本地 PDFno local PDF
Arc Institute 官方网页(发布公告、Behind the data、2025 总结、2026 公告、State 新闻页)+ Cell 评论摘要;Cell 全文未获取
Arc Institute pages (launch, Behind the data, 2025 wrap-up, 2026 announcement, State news) + Cell commentary abstract; full Cell text not obtained
Arc Institute 的年度公开竞赛:在 H1 人胚胎干细胞里,根据部分基因被 CRISPRi 敲低后的单细胞数据,预测其余未见过的敲低会让细胞表达怎样变化。
Arc Institute's annual open competition: from single-cell data on some CRISPRi knockdowns in H1 human embryonic stem cells, predict how unseen knockdowns change the cells' expression.

问题Problem

virtual cell(细胞状态模型)的核心目标之一是预测细胞对扰动的反应,但领域缺少统一的评测框架和专门生成的数据(Cell 摘要)。Arc 想用一个每年举办的开放竞赛提供框架、数据和交流场所,并检验模型能否泛化到新的细胞语境(发布公告)。

A key goal of virtual-cell models is predicting responses to perturbation, but the field lacked a shared evaluation framework and purpose-built data (Cell abstract). Arc runs a recurring open competition to supply both and to test generalisation to a new cell context (launch page).

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 预筛:在 H1 细胞中做大规模 CRISPRi 预筛,统计每个扰动引起的差异表达基因数(Behind the data)。
  2. 选靶点:按反应强度分档抽样,并用 ContrastiveVI+ 在隐空间聚类,保证 300 个靶基因覆盖不同的反应模式(Behind the data)。
  3. 生成数据:双 guide CRISPRi(dCas9-KRAB 压低转录)+ 10x Flex 单细胞测序,约 30 万个细胞(Behind the data)。
  4. 划分:按差异表达基因数和高质量细胞数平衡地分成训练 150、验证 50、测试 100 个靶基因(Behind the data)。
  5. 参赛:队伍拿训练数据(可外加 Arc Virtual Cell Atlas 等公开数据)训练模型,对未见过的靶基因输出预测的单细胞表达(发布公告)。
  6. 打分:DES(差异表达基因集合重叠)、PDS(预测是否最接近自己那个扰动的真值)、MAE(全基因误差),各自相对扰动均值基线归一化后取平均;只按最终测试集排名(Behind the data, 发布公告)。
  7. 追加 Generalist Prize:因 MAE 不再影响排名,对前 50 名用 7 个指标的平均名次另设大奖(wrap-up)。
  1. Pilot CRISPRi screen in H1 to count DE genes per perturbation; choose 300 targets across strength bins and ContrastiveVI+ response clusters (Behind the data).
  2. Profile ~300,000 cells with dual-guide CRISPRi and 10x Flex single-cell RNA-seq (Behind the data).
  3. Balanced split into 150 train / 50 validation / 100 test targets (Behind the data).
  4. Teams train (public data such as the Arc Virtual Cell Atlas allowed) and predict single-cell expression for held-out targets (launch page).
  5. Score DES, PDS and MAE, each normalised against a perturbation-mean baseline, averaged; final test set only (Behind the data).
  6. Mid-competition Generalist Prize: average rank over 7 metrics for the top 50 entries (wrap-up).

术语Terms

CRISPRi
用失活的 Cas9 带一个抑制结构域(KRAB)去压低某个基因的转录,不切 DNA。
Perturb-seq
一次对大量细胞做不同基因扰动,再用单细胞测序读出每个细胞的扰动和表达。
H1 hESC
一株人胚胎干细胞系,被充分表征;与常见的 K562、A375 等癌细胞系不同。
UMI
单细胞测序里给每个 RNA 分子加的唯一标签,用来计数分子;UMI 越多测得越深。
差异表达基因 DEG
扰动后表达显著上调或下调的基因。
pseudo-bulk
把同一扰动下所有细胞的表达求平均,当成一个“伪 bulk”样本。
CRISPRi
用失活的 Cas9 带一个抑制结构域(KRAB)去压低某个基因的转录,不切 DNA。
Perturb-seq
一次对大量细胞做不同基因扰动,再用单细胞测序读出每个细胞的扰动和表达。
H1 hESC
一株人胚胎干细胞系,被充分表征;与常见的 K562、A375 等癌细胞系不同。
UMI
单细胞测序里给每个 RNA 分子加的唯一标签,用来计数分子;UMI 越多测得越深。
差异表达基因 DEG
扰动后表达显著上调或下调的基因。
pseudo-bulk
把同一扰动下所有细胞的表达求平均,当成一个“伪 bulk”样本。

数据Data

约 300,000 个 H1 细胞、300 个 CRISPRi 靶基因;每个扰动约 1,000 个细胞,每个细胞 UMI 中位数 >50,000;63% 的细胞两个 guide 都正确检出,83% 的细胞敲低 >80%(Behind the data)。2025 数据集现可从 Arc Virtual Cell Atlas 下载(2026 公告)。

~300,000 H1 cells, 300 CRISPRi targets; ~1,000 cells per perturbation; median >50,000 UMIs per cell; 63% of cells with both guides detected, 83% with >80% knockdown (Behind the data). Now downloadable from the Arc Virtual Cell Atlas (2026 announcement).

主要结果Key results

5,000+ 人注册(114 国),1,200+ 队提交,300+ 队最终提交wrap-up
模型没有在所有指标上稳定胜过朴素基线;几乎所有模型 MAE 比基线差wrap-up
基于 L1 的 PDS 会随预测幅度变大而上升,可被利用wrap-up
第 1 名 BM_xTVC(BioMap,10 万美元):scFoundation 改进模型 + 蛋白 embedding + 统计特征,融合 PDS+DES+MAE 损失,pseudo-bulk 训练wrap-up
第 2 名 XLearning Lab(5 万美元):全连接网络 + ESM-2 embedding,只用公开 Perturb-seq;第 3 名 Outlier(2.5 万美元):统计方法 TransPertwrap-up
Generalist Prize(10 万美元)Altos Labs:U-Net flow matching,约 700 万细胞预训练,7 个指标平均名次最高wrap-up
2026 届改为零样本:6 个细胞系(3 验证、3 测试),不发训练集,六指标综合分2026 公告
5,000+ registrants (114 countries), 1,200+ teams submitting, 300+ final submissionswrap-up
Models did not consistently beat naive baselines on all metrics; almost all were worse on MAEwrap-up
L1-based PDS rises with prediction magnitude and can be exploitedwrap-up
1st BM_xTVC (BioMap, $100k): scFoundation-based model + protein embeddings + statistical features, fused PDS+DES+MAE loss, pseudo-bulkwrap-up
2nd XLearning Lab ($50k): fully connected net + ESM-2, public Perturb-seq only; 3rd Outlier ($25k): statistical TransPertwrap-up
Generalist Prize ($100k) Altos Labs: U-Net flow matching pretrained on ~7M cells, best 7-metric average rankwrap-up
2026 edition is zero-shot: 6 cell lines (3 validation, 3 test), no training set, six-metric aggregate2026 announcement

结论Conclusions

Arc 认为:混合“深度学习 + 统计特征”胜过纯神经网络;损失设计与架构同样重要;蛋白 embedding 普遍有用;没有单一指标能代表模型质量,优化一个常伤及其他(wrap-up)。

Arc concludes that hybrids of deep learning and statistics beat purely neural models, loss design matters as much as architecture, protein embeddings help, and no single metric captures model quality (wrap-up).

局限Limitations

  • 主办方:窄的评分函数诱导优化指标而非生物学,2026 年因此改为六指标(2026 公告)。
  • 主办方:准确度、指标设计和生物学泛化仍需改进(2026 公告)。
  • 我们的理解:单一细胞类型、单基因敲低、只有转录组,没有组织或空间信息。
  • 我们的理解:前几名依赖 pseudo-bulk,评分主要奖励平均反应而非单细胞异质性。
  • 我们的理解:未读到 Cell 全文,指标精确公式需另行核对。
  • Organisers: a narrow score invites metric optimisation over biology; hence six metrics in 2026 (2026 announcement).
  • Organisers: accuracy, metric design and biological generalisation still need work (2026 announcement).
  • Our reading: one cell type, single-gene knockdowns, transcriptome only; no tissue or spatial context.
  • Our reading: reliance on pseudo-bulk means the score mainly rewards the average response.
  • Our reading: the full Cell text was not read; exact metric formulas need checking.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这是 model → revert 的前向模型评测:给定扰动预测细胞状态;revert 是它的反问题。对课题 1 最有用的是评测设计——按难度平衡划分、相对朴素基线归一化、用多个互补指标防止钻空子;这些可以直接搬到肾脏异常检测基准。对肾脏 revert 的直接价值有限,因为 H1 干细胞与肾脏细胞差别大,且没有组织尺度。

Our reading: this benchmarks the forward model for model → revert (perturbation → state); reversion is the inverse problem. Most useful for Topic 1 is the evaluation design — difficulty-balanced splits, normalisation against a naive baseline, several complementary metrics to resist gaming — which transfers to a kidney anomaly benchmark. Direct value for kidney reversion is limited: H1 stem cells are far from kidney cells and there is no tissue scale.

Kidney Artificial Intelligence Discovery Challenge: From Nebulae to Nephrons

National Institute of Diabetes and Digestive and Kidney Diseases. Kidney Artificial Intelligence Discovery Challenge: From Nebulae to Nephrons. NIH prize challenge hosted on HeroX. 2026.

https://www.herox.com/KidneyAI · 没有本地 PDF（见下方来源说明）No local PDF (see source note)

网页 / READMEweb page / README无本地 PDFno local PDF
HeroX 挑战页(2026-10-19 逐字抓取,2026-10-27 复核);nih.gov 公告页返回 403
HeroX challenge page (verbatim capture 2026-10-19, re-checked 2026-10-27); nih.gov announcement returned 403
NIDDK 的 10 万美元奖金挑战赛:只用 KPMP 公开数据,在肾活检全切片图像里发现以往未识别的形态模式或异常,并经专家人机协同复核 —— 目标是发现,不是分类。
A US$100,000 NIDDK prize challenge: using only open KPMP data, discover previously unrecognised patterns or anomalies in kidney-biopsy whole-slide images, backed by human–AI expert review — discovery, not classification.

问题Problem

美国约 3,550 万人患肾病,但疾病异质性、进展机制和治疗反应仍有大量空白。主办方认为假设驱动分析和基于分类的机器学习会漏掉稀有、细微、空间复杂或落在现有疾病类别之外的模式,想看异常检测、半监督学习、embedding 相似性检索和人机协同审阅能否像在天文学中那样在肾脏病理中找到新东西(HeroX Overview, Guidelines)。

About 35.5 million people in the US have kidney disease, yet heterogeneity, progression and treatment response remain poorly understood. The organisers argue that hypothesis-driven analysis and classification-based ML miss rare, subtle, spatially complex or out-of-category patterns, and ask whether anomaly detection, semi-supervised learning, embedding similarity search and human–AI review can repeat astronomy's success in kidney pathology (HeroX Overview, Guidelines).

方法（逐步）Method, step by step

  1. 数据:NIH 向所有参赛者提供一套统一的 KPMP 公开图像;只允许用 Kidney Tissue Atlas 的 open 数据(HeroX, Statutory Authority)。
  2. 方法:由参赛者自定,主办方举例异常检测、半监督学习、学到的 embedding 空间中的相似性检索(HeroX, Guidelines)。
  3. 输出:逐条描述每个发现,给出证据与科学理由(为何稀有、新颖或有意义)、技术方法、假设、验证程序、可靠性度量与局限(HeroX, Submission Requirements)。
  4. 可选:用图像异常结合临床参数或其他 KPMP 数据给患者分层或重新分类;给新特征指派分子机制留给未来挑战(HeroX, The Challenge)。
  5. 复现:提交物必须可运行,HeroX 会独立执行并复现所报告的发现(HeroX, Submission Requirements)。
  6. 评审:行政初筛 → Evaluation Panel 按 50/30/20 打分 → NIH Judging Panel 选出获奖者 → NIDDK 主任批准(HeroX, Judging)。
  1. Data: NIH supplies a common set of open KPMP images; only open Kidney Tissue Atlas data are allowed (HeroX, Statutory Authority).
  2. Method of choice, e.g. anomaly detection, semi-supervised learning, similarity search in learned embeddings (HeroX, Guidelines).
  3. Describe each discovery with evidence, rationale, methods, assumptions, validation, reliability measures and limitations; optional patient stratification (HeroX, Submission Requirements).
  4. Submit in runnable form; HeroX re-executes and verifies the findings (HeroX, Submission Requirements).
  5. Triage, then Evaluation Panel scoring (50/30/20), NIH Judging Panel selection, NIDDK Director approval (HeroX, Judging).

术语Terms

KPMP
Kidney Precision Medicine Project,NIDDK 支持的多中心项目,收集 CKD 和 AKI 患者的肾活检并做多尺度分析。
Kidney Tissue Atlas
KPMP 的数据资源,映射肾脏细胞类型、组织结构与疾病通路;分 open 与 controlled 两类数据。
CKD / AKI
慢性肾病 / 急性肾损伤,KPMP 收集活检的两类患者。
全切片图像 WSI
整张组织切片的高分辨率扫描图。
制片 / 扫描伪影
染色深浅、切片折叠、扫描仪差异等与疾病无关的图像变化。
KPMP
Kidney Precision Medicine Project,NIDDK 支持的多中心项目,收集 CKD 和 AKI 患者的肾活检并做多尺度分析。
Kidney Tissue Atlas
KPMP 的数据资源,映射肾脏细胞类型、组织结构与疾病通路;分 open 与 controlled 两类数据。
CKD / AKI
慢性肾病 / 急性肾损伤,KPMP 收集活检的两类患者。
全切片图像 WSI
整张组织切片的高分辨率扫描图。
制片 / 扫描伪影
染色深浅、切片折叠、扫描仪差异等与疾病无关的图像变化。

数据Data

只能用 KPMP Kidney Tissue Atlas 中公开(open)的数据,不得用需数据使用协议的 controlled 数据;NIH 提供统一的合格图像集;公开数据已去标识化,不含个人身份信息(HeroX, Statutory Authority)。评审标准针对肾活检全切片图像(HeroX, Judging Criteria)。

Only open data from the KPMP Kidney Tissue Atlas; no controlled data under a data-use agreement; NIH provides a common set of eligible images; public data are de-identified (HeroX, Statutory Authority). Judging targets whole-slide kidney biopsy images (HeroX, Judging Criteria).

主要结果Key results

尚无结果:2026-10-27 仍为预注册阶段,页面显示 60 个 teamHeroX 页面

Criterion 1, discovery and novelty, 50 pts: separate meaningful findings from background variation, technical artifacts and expected histologyHeroX, Judging Criteria
Criterion 2, technical approach, 30 pts: rank/retrieve rare findings, cut artifact false positives, give confidence and failure modes; e.g. top-k precision, MRR, AUPRCHeroX, Judging Criteria
Criterion 3, interpretability and expert usability, 20 pts: heatmaps, representative patches, stratification; e.g. review time, inter-rater agreementHeroX, Judging Criteria
Eligibility: cash only to US citizens/permanent residents or US entities; non-US members allowed without prize money; no federal fundsHeroX, Eligibility

结论Conclusions

主办方期望短期内形成跨学科研究者网络、KPMP 数据中优先异常检测目标的目录、概念性分析流程和战略白皮书;长期希望发现新的疾病亚型、进展标志物和治疗反应特征(HeroX, The Challenge)。

Organisers expect a cross-disciplinary network, a catalogue of priority anomaly-detection targets in KPMP data, conceptual pipelines and a white paper in the near term, and new disease subtypes, progression markers and treatment-response signatures in the long term (HeroX, The Challenge).

局限Limitations

  • 主办方:提交要求仍在定稿,开放提交前才完整发布(HeroX, Submission Requirements)。
  • 主办方:给新特征指派分子机制不在本次范围(HeroX, The Challenge)。
  • 我们的理解:只能用 open 数据,不能用我们自己的 95 例活检作为提交依据;controlled 的个体层面组学与临床细节不可用。
  • 我们的理解:由专家打分,没有公开排行榜或固定 ground truth,结果难以和其他方法直接比较。
  • 我们的理解:非美国成员不能分奖金,参赛需美国合作者担任 Team Captain。
  • Organisers: submission requirements are still being finalised (HeroX).
  • Organisers: assigning molecular mechanisms is out of scope for now (HeroX).
  • Our reading: open data only, so our own ~95 biopsies cannot underpin a submission.
  • Our reading: expert-panel judging, no public leaderboard or fixed ground truth.
  • Our reading: non-US members get no prize money; a US Team Captain is needed.

对我们方向的意义Takeaway for our direction

我们的理解:这几乎是课题 1 的外部考场 —— 评审要求“区分真发现与染色、制片、扫描伪影”、“给置信度与失败模式”,正对应我们“跨模态一致性区分伪影与疾病”的假设;建议指标(top-k precision、seeded 发现的排名、专家审阅负担)可直接成为 KidneyAnomalyBench 的评测协议。方法可在自有数据上开发,但提交只能基于 open KPMP 数据,具体对“开发数据”的规定需等提交要求公布后核实。

Our reading: practically an external exam for Topic 1 — the demand to separate true findings from staining, preparation and scanner artifacts and to report confidence and failure modes matches our hypothesis that cross-modal agreement separates artifacts from disease, and the suggested metrics (top-k precision, rank of seeded findings, expert review burden) can become KidneyAnomalyBench's protocol. Methods may be developed in-house, but submissions must rest on open KPMP data; check the rules on development data once requirements are published.
